WEARWISE — ALL SOURCE CODE (generated on every build; do not edit this file)
Live site: https://tiffany-is-awesome.github.io/My-Own-Projects/wearwise.html
Repo: https://github.com/tiffany-is-awesome/My-Own-Projects/tree/main/wearwise
Stack: React 18 + Vite, built into one self-contained HTML file. No backend of its own:
  accounts/sync = Supabase (src/auth.jsx), AI = Google Gemini through Puter.js (src/ai/claude.js, src/ai/stylist.js),
  styling logic = rules engine (src/ai/critic.js scores outfits, src/ai/generator.js builds them), data = src/store.jsx.
Unit tests live in src/**/*.test.js (not included here). Change history: CHANGELOG.md.

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from './store.jsx';
import Welcome from './views/Welcome.jsx';
import Closet from './views/Closet.jsx';
import DressingRoom from './views/DressingRoom.jsx';
import Create from './views/Create.jsx';
import StyleMe from './views/StyleMe.jsx';
import Saved from './views/Saved.jsx';
import Family from './views/Family.jsx';
import Settings from './views/Settings.jsx';
import History from './views/History.jsx';
import Calendar from './views/Calendar.jsx';
import { tipsOn } from './components/TipJar.jsx';
import Insights from './views/Insights.jsx';
import Inspiration from './views/Inspiration.jsx';
import { useClaudeStatus, puterUser, puterSignIn, puterSignOut, loadPuter } from './ai/claude.js';
import { useToast } from './components/common.jsx';
import Account from './components/Account.jsx';
import { LookbookList, LookbookDetail, SharedLookbook } from './views/Lookbooks.jsx';

const NAV = [
  ['#/home', 'Home'],
  ['#/closet', 'My Closet'],
  ['#/create', 'Create an Outfit', true],
  ['#/saved', 'Saved'],
  ['#/calendar', 'Calendar'],
  ['#/family', 'Family'],
];

// Phone: a bottom tab bar (thumb reach) + a "More" page for everything else.
const TABS = [
  ['#/home', 'Home', 'M4 11 12 4l8 7v9h-5v-6H9v6H4z'],
  ['#/closet', 'Closet', 'M12 6a2 2 0 1 1 2-2M12 6 3 14h18z M5 14v5h14v-5'],
  ['#/create', 'Create', 'M12 3l2.2 5.6L20 9l-4.5 3.8L17 19l-5-3.2L7 19l1.5-6.2L4 9l5.8-.4z'],
  ['#/saved', 'Saved', 'M12 20s-7-4.4-7-10a4 4 0 0 1 7-2.6A4 4 0 0 1 19 10c0 5.6-7 10-7 10z'],
  ['#/more', 'More', 'M5 12h.01M12 12h.01M19 12h.01'],
];

function MorePage() {
  const links = [
    ['#/calendar', 'Outfit calendar', 'Plan days and trips, with packing lists'],
    ['#/history', 'Outfit History', 'What you wore, with ratings'],
    ['#/insights', 'Closet insights', 'Rediscover My Closet and versatility'],
    ['#/builder', 'Outfit builder', 'Put a look together piece by piece'],
    ['#/family', 'Family looks', 'Coordinated outfits for everyone'],
    ['#/lookbooks', 'Lookbooks & trips', 'Collections and packing lists'],
    ['#/inspiration', 'Inspiration', 'Recreate a look you saw'],
    ['#/settings', 'Settings', 'Account, AI, places, delete data'],
  ];
  return (
    <div className="page narrow">
      <div className="page-head"><div><div className="label">Wearwise</div><h1 className="h1">More</h1></div></div>
      <ul className="more-list">
        {links.map(([h, t, d]) => <li key={h}><a href={h}><b>{t}</b><span className="tiny">{d}</span><span aria-hidden="true">›</span></a></li>)}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}

function ProfileSwitch() {
  const { state, dispatch, profile } = useStore();
  if (state.profiles.length < 2) return null;
  return (
    <label className="profile-switch" title="Whose closet you're looking at">
      <span className="tiny">Closet</span>
      <select value={profile.id} onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'SWITCH_PROFILE', id: e.target.value })} aria-label="Switch family member">
        {state.profiles.map((p) => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.name}</option>)}
      </select>
    </label>
  );
}

function useHash() {
  const [hash, setHash] = useState(window.location.hash || '');
  useEffect(() => {
    const on = () => { setHash(window.location.hash); window.scrollTo(0, 0); };
    window.addEventListener('hashchange', on);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('hashchange', on);
  }, []);
  return hash;
}

export default function App() {
  useStore();
  const hash = useHash();
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [gemUser, setGemUser] = useState(undefined); // undefined = checking, null = signed out
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ai.via !== 'puter') return;
    let alive = true;
    const check = () => loadPuter().then(() => puterUser()).then((u) => alive && setGemUser(u || null));
    check();
    const t = setInterval(check, 20000); // picks up a sign-in that happened in Puter's pop-up during an AI request
    return () => { alive = false; clearInterval(t); };
  }, [ai.via]);
  async function gemClick() {
    if (gemUser) {
      puterSignOut(); setGemUser(null); toast('Signed out of the AI stylist (Puter)');
      return;
    }
    try { await puterSignIn(); const u = await puterUser(); setGemUser(u || null); if (u) toast(`AI stylist on · signed in to Puter as ${u.username}`); }
    catch { toast('Sign-in was closed. The rules engine keeps styling until you sign in.'); }
  }
  const go = (h) => { window.location.hash = h; };
  const [, route, param] = hash.includes('access_token=') || hash.includes('error=') ? ['', 'home'] : hash.split('/');

  let view;
  if (route === 'share' && param) view = <SharedLookbook data={param} go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'closet') view = <Closet key={param || 'all'} go={go} initial={param ? decodeURIComponent(param) : ''} />;
  else if (route === 'builder' || route === 'dressing') view = <DressingRoom go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'create' || route === 'mix') view = <Create key={param || 'c'} go={go} autoRun={param === 'go'} />;
  else if (route === 'style' && param) view = <StyleMe key={param} id={decodeURIComponent(param)} go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'saved') view = <Saved go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'history') view = <History go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'calendar') view = <Calendar go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'insights') view = <Insights go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'family') view = <Family go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'settings') view = <Settings go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'lookbooks' && param) view = <LookbookDetail id={param} go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'lookbooks') view = <LookbookList go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'inspiration') view = <Inspiration go={go} />;
  else if (route === 'more') view = <MorePage />;
  else view = <Welcome go={go} />;

  const ALIAS = { dressing: 'create', builder: 'create', mix: 'create', welcome: 'home', style: 'closet', lookbooks: 'saved', inspiration: 'saved', insights: 'closet', history: 'calendar' };
  const MOBILE_ALIAS = { calendar: 'more', history: 'more', family: 'more', settings: 'more', insights: 'more', lookbooks: 'more', inspiration: 'more' };
  const active = route === 'share' ? '' : '#/' + (ALIAS[route] || route || 'home');

  return (
    <>
      <header className="topbar">
        <div className="topbar-inner">
          <a className="logo" href="#/home" aria-label="Wearwise home"><span className="logo-mark">W</span><span>WEARWISE</span></a>
          <nav className="nav" aria-label="Main">
            {NAV.map(([h, l, primary]) => <a key={h} href={h} className={(active === h ? 'active' : '') + (primary ? ' primary' : '')}>{l}</a>)}
          </nav>
          <ProfileSwitch />
          <a className={'icon-link' + (active === '#/settings' ? ' active' : '')} href="#/settings" aria-label="Settings" title="Settings">
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="18" height="18" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.6"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="3" /><path d="M19.4 15a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 .3 1.8l.1.1a2 2 0 1 1-2.8 2.8l-.1-.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.8-.3 1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1 1.5V21a2 2 0 1 1-4 0v-.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.1-1.5 1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.8.3l-.1.1a2 2 0 1 1-2.8-2.8l.1-.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 .3-1.8 1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.5-1H3a2 2 0 1 1 0-4h.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1.5-1.1 1.7 1.7 0 0 0-.3-1.8l-.1-.1a2 2 0 1 1 2.8-2.8l.1.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1.8.3H9a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1-1.5V3a2 2 0 1 1 4 0v.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1 1.5 1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1.8-.3l.1-.1a2 2 0 1 1 2.8 2.8l-.1.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-.3 1.8V9a1.7 1.7 0 0 0 1.5 1H21a2 2 0 1 1 0 4h-.1a1.7 1.7 0 0 0-1.5 1z" /></svg>
          </a>
          {ai.via === 'puter' ? (
            <button className={'ai-status as-button ' + (gemUser ? 'ready' : '')} onClick={gemClick}
              title={gemUser ? `AI stylist: Gemini, signed in to Puter as ${gemUser.username}. Click to sign out.` : 'AI stylist: Gemini. Free, no key: sign in with a Puter account to switch it on.'}>
              <i />{gemUser ? 'Gemini on' : gemUser === undefined ? 'Gemini' : 'Gemini · Sign in'}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <span className={'ai-status ' + ai.status} title={ai.status === 'ready' ? `AI stylist: ${ai.name}` : 'The AI stylist is not connected here, so the built-in rules engine is used'}>
              <i />{ai.status === 'ready' ? `${ai.name} on` : ai.status === 'checking' ? 'Connecting…' : ai.status === 'signin' ? 'Sign in for AI' : 'Rules engine'}
            </span>
          )}
          <Account />
        </div>
      </header>
      <main key={route || 'home'} className="view-enter">{view}</main>
      <nav className="tabbar" aria-label="Main (mobile)">
        {TABS.map(([h, l, d]) => {
          const on = h === '#/more' ? (route === 'more' || MOBILE_ALIAS[route] === 'more') : active === h && !MOBILE_ALIAS[route];
          return (
            <a key={h} href={h} className={(on ? 'on' : '') + (h === '#/create' ? ' create' : '')} aria-current={on ? 'page' : undefined}>
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="22" height="22" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.7" strokeLinecap="round" strokeLinejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d={d} /></svg>
              <span>{l}</span>
            </a>
          );
        })}
      </nav>
      <footer className="footer">
        Wearwise · AI stylist: Google Gemini via Puter (free, no key: sign in), checked by a built-in rules engine that also works on its own · saved in this browser, or to your account when you sign in · <a href="#/settings">Settings &amp; delete my data</a>{tipsOn() && <> · <a href="#/settings">🍩 Treat the developer</a></>}
      </footer>
    </>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/store.jsx
====================================================================================================

// App state: one reducer, persisted to localStorage (and synced to Supabase when signed in, see auth.jsx).
//
// v2 model:
//   profiles   family members, each with her/his own closet ({ id, name, kind: 'adult' | 'child' })
//   garments   every piece carries ownerId (a profile id); demo pieces carry demo: true
//   outfits    saved looks: { id, name, profileId, slots, context, stage, favorite, createdAt }
//   catalogs   lookbooks & trips (per profile)
//   ensembles  saved family looks: { id, name, context, members: [{ profileId, slots }] }
import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useReducer, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { forDevice, fromDevice, local, refsIn } from './photos.js';
import { DEMO_CLOSET, DEMO_OUTFITS, DEMO_CATALOGS, BAG_ARTS } from './data/closet.js';
import { DEMO_FAMILY } from './data/family.js';
import { SAMPLE_INSPIRATION } from './ai/inspiration.js';
import { parseContext, DEFAULT_CONTEXT } from './ai/context.js';
import { preferenceProfile } from './ai/insights.js';
import { critique } from './ai/critic.js';

const KEY = 'wearwise.v1';
const OLD_KEY = 'unique-ava.v1'; // before the rename: copied over once, so nobody loses their closet
try {
  if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined' && !localStorage.getItem(KEY) && localStorage.getItem(OLD_KEY)) {
    localStorage.setItem(KEY, localStorage.getItem(OLD_KEY));
    localStorage.removeItem(OLD_KEY);
  }
} catch { /* storage unavailable: nothing to migrate */ }
const VERSION = 2;
const uid = (p) => p + '-' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 9);
const ME = 'p-me';
const emptySlots = () => ({ top: null, bottom: null, outer: null, shoes: null, bag: null, accessory: null });

export function initialState() {
  return {
    version: VERSION,
    onboarded: false,
    profiles: [{ id: ME, name: 'Me', kind: 'adult' }],
    activeProfile: ME,
    garments: [],
    outfits: [],
    catalogs: [],
    ensembles: [],
    inspirations: [],
    locations: [],   // where clothes are kept: [{ id, name }] (shared by the household)
    wardrobes: [],   // a person's groups of saved outfits: [{ id, profileId, name }] (e.g. Work, Home)
    plans: [],       // outfits planned on the calendar: [{ id, profileId, date, slots, name, outfitId?, tripId? }]
    trips: [],       // [{ id, profileId, name, start, end, occasion, weather }]
    history: [],     // outfits actually worn: [{ id, profileId, date, slots, context, outfitId?, rating?, tags? }]
    draft: { name: '', slots: emptySlots(), context: DEFAULT_CONTEXT, stage: { mode: 'flatlay', scene: 'tokyo-rain', customBg: null } },
  };
}

// ── Migration from the first version (one person, hardcoded sample closet, bags in "accessory").
const DEMO_IDS = new Set([...DEMO_CLOSET.map((g) => g.id), 't-white-tee', 'a-tote']);
const DEMO_OUTFIT_IDS = new Set(['o-spring-1', 'o-spring-2', 'o-spring-3', 'o-kyoto-1', 'o-kyoto-2', 'o-kyoto-3', 'o-kyoto-4']);
const DEMO_CATALOG_IDS = new Set(['c-spring', 'c-kyoto']);
function fixSlots(slots, byId) {
  const s = { ...emptySlots(), ...(slots || {}) };
  for (const k of Object.keys(s)) if (s[k] && String(s[k]).startsWith('basic-')) s[k] = null; // old "basics" she never owned
  const acc = s.accessory && byId.get(s.accessory);
  if (acc && BAG_ARTS.includes(acc.art) && !s.bag) { s.bag = s.accessory; s.accessory = null; }
  return s;
}
export function migrate(raw) {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== 'object') return initialState();
  if (raw.version === VERSION) return { ...initialState(), ...raw };
  const base = initialState();
  const garments = (raw.garments || []).filter((g) => !g.placeholder).map((g) => ({
    ...g, ownerId: g.ownerId || ME, demo: g.demo ?? (DEMO_IDS.has(g.id) && !g.uploaded),
  }));
  const byId = new Map(garments.map((g) => [g.id, g]));
  return {
    ...base,
    onboarded: !!raw.onboarded,
    garments,
    outfits: (raw.outfits || []).map((o) => ({ ...o, profileId: o.profileId || ME, slots: fixSlots(o.slots, byId), demo: o.demo ?? DEMO_OUTFIT_IDS.has(o.id) })),
    catalogs: (raw.catalogs || []).map((c) => ({ ...c, profileId: c.profileId || ME, demo: c.demo ?? DEMO_CATALOG_IDS.has(c.id) })),
    inspirations: (raw.inspirations || []).map((i) => ({ ...i, profileId: i.profileId || ME, demo: i.demo ?? /^insp-\d$/.test(i.id) })),
    draft: { ...base.draft, ...(raw.draft || {}), slots: fixSlots(raw.draft?.slots, byId) },
    aiCritiques: raw.aiCritiques,
  };
}

function load() {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(KEY);
    if (raw) return migrate(JSON.parse(raw));
  } catch { /* storage unavailable: start fresh */ }
  return initialState();
}

function demoBundle(state) {
  const owner = state.activeProfile;
  const now = Date.now();
  const garments = DEMO_CLOSET.map((g) => ({ ...g, ownerId: owner }));
  const outfits = DEMO_OUTFITS.map((o) => ({
    id: o.id, name: o.name, profileId: owner, demo: true, favorite: o.id === 'o-kyoto-3',
    slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...o.slots }, context: parseContext(o.prompt), stage: o.stage || { mode: 'flatlay', scene: 'studio' }, createdAt: now,
  }));
  const catalogs = DEMO_CATALOGS.map((c) => ({ ...c, profileId: owner, demo: true, createdAt: now }));
  const inspirations = SAMPLE_INSPIRATION.map((i) => ({ ...i, profileId: owner, demo: true }));
  // Demo family members (so family looks can be tried right away)
  const profiles = DEMO_FAMILY.profiles.filter((p) => !state.profiles.some((x) => x.id === p.id)).map((p) => ({ ...p, demo: true }));
  const familyGarments = DEMO_FAMILY.garments;
  return { garments: [...garments, ...familyGarments], outfits, catalogs, inspirations, profiles };
}

function reducer(state, a) {
  const me = state.activeProfile;
  switch (a.type) {
    case 'ONBOARDED': return { ...state, onboarded: true };

    // ── family members
    case 'ADD_PROFILE': {
      const p = { id: uid('p'), name: a.name || 'New member', kind: a.kind || 'adult' };
      return { ...state, profiles: [...state.profiles, p], activeProfile: p.id, draft: { ...state.draft, name: '', slots: emptySlots() } };
    }
    case 'UPDATE_PROFILE': return { ...state, profiles: state.profiles.map((p) => (p.id === a.profile.id ? { ...p, ...a.profile } : p)) };
    case 'DELETE_PROFILE': {
      if (a.id === ME || state.profiles.length <= 1) return state;
      return {
        ...state,
        profiles: state.profiles.filter((p) => p.id !== a.id),
        garments: state.garments.filter((g) => g.ownerId !== a.id),
        outfits: state.outfits.filter((o) => o.profileId !== a.id),
        catalogs: state.catalogs.filter((c) => c.profileId !== a.id),
        ensembles: state.ensembles.map((e) => ({ ...e, members: e.members.filter((m) => m.profileId !== a.id) })).filter((e) => e.members.length),
        activeProfile: state.activeProfile === a.id ? ME : state.activeProfile,
        history: state.history.filter((h) => h.profileId !== a.id),
        wardrobes: state.wardrobes.filter((w) => w.profileId !== a.id),
        plans: state.plans.filter((p) => p.profileId !== a.id),
        trips: state.trips.filter((t) => t.profileId !== a.id),
      };
    }
    case 'SWITCH_PROFILE':
      if (a.id === state.activeProfile) return state;
      return { ...state, activeProfile: a.id, draft: { ...state.draft, name: '', slots: emptySlots() } };

    // ── closet
    case 'ADD_GARMENTS': return { ...state, garments: [...a.garments.map((g) => ({ ...g, ownerId: g.ownerId || me, demo: false, addedAt: g.addedAt || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) })), ...state.garments] };
    case 'ADD_GARMENT': return { ...state, garments: [{ ...a.garment, ownerId: a.garment.ownerId || me, demo: false, addedAt: a.garment.addedAt || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10) }, ...state.garments] };
    case 'UPDATE_GARMENT': return { ...state, garments: state.garments.map((g) => (g.id === a.garment.id ? { ...g, ...a.garment } : g)) };
    case 'DELETE_GARMENT': {
      const clear = (slots) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(slots).map(([k, v]) => [k, v === a.id ? null : v]));
      return { ...state, garments: state.garments.filter((g) => g.id !== a.id), draft: { ...state.draft, slots: clear(state.draft.slots) } };
    }
    case 'LOAD_DEMO': {
      const b = demoBundle(state);
      const have = new Set(state.garments.map((g) => g.id));
      return {
        ...state,
        onboarded: true,
        profiles: [...state.profiles, ...b.profiles],
        garments: [...state.garments, ...b.garments.filter((g) => !have.has(g.id))],
        outfits: [...b.outfits.filter((o) => !state.outfits.some((x) => x.id === o.id)), ...state.outfits],
        catalogs: [...b.catalogs.filter((c) => !state.catalogs.some((x) => x.id === c.id)), ...state.catalogs],
        inspirations: [...b.inspirations.filter((i) => !state.inspirations.some((x) => x.id === i.id)), ...state.inspirations],
      };
    }
    case 'REMOVE_DEMO': {
      const demoProfiles = new Set(state.profiles.filter((p) => p.demo).map((p) => p.id));
      const garments = state.garments.filter((g) => !g.demo && !demoProfiles.has(g.ownerId));
      const keep = new Set(garments.map((g) => g.id));
      const clean = (slots) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(slots || {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, keep.has(v) ? v : null]));
      const outfits = state.outfits.filter((o) => !o.demo && !demoProfiles.has(o.profileId));
      const outfitIds = new Set(outfits.map((o) => o.id));
      return {
        ...state,
        profiles: state.profiles.filter((p) => !p.demo),
        activeProfile: demoProfiles.has(state.activeProfile) ? ME : state.activeProfile,
        garments,
        outfits,
        catalogs: state.catalogs.filter((c) => !c.demo).map((c) => ({ ...c, outfitIds: c.outfitIds.filter((x) => outfitIds.has(x)) })),
        ensembles: state.ensembles.filter((e) => !e.demo).map((e) => ({ ...e, members: e.members.filter((m) => !demoProfiles.has(m.profileId)) })).filter((e) => e.members.length),
        inspirations: state.inspirations.filter((i) => !i.demo),
        history: state.history.filter((h) => !demoProfiles.has(h.profileId) && Object.values(h.slots || {}).every((v) => !v || keep.has(v))),
        plans: state.plans.filter((p) => !demoProfiles.has(p.profileId) && Object.values(p.slots || {}).every((v) => !v || keep.has(v))),
        trips: state.trips.filter((t) => !demoProfiles.has(t.profileId)),
        wardrobes: state.wardrobes.filter((w) => !demoProfiles.has(w.profileId)),
        draft: { ...state.draft, slots: clean(state.draft.slots) },
      };
    }

    // ── builder draft
    case 'SET_SLOT': {
      const slots = { ...state.draft.slots, [a.slot]: a.id };
      if (a.slot === 'top' && a.isDress) slots.bottom = null;           // a dress replaces the bottom
      if (a.slot === 'bottom' && a.id && a.topIsDress) slots.top = null; // choosing a bottom drops the dress
      return { ...state, draft: { ...state.draft, slots } };
    }
    case 'LOAD_DRAFT': return { ...state, draft: { ...state.draft, ...a.draft, slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...(a.draft.slots || state.draft.slots) } } };
    case 'SET_CONTEXT': return { ...state, draft: { ...state.draft, context: a.context, choice: a.choice || state.draft.choice } };
    case 'SET_STAGE': return { ...state, draft: { ...state.draft, stage: { ...state.draft.stage, ...a.stage } } };
    case 'SET_NAME': return { ...state, draft: { ...state.draft, name: a.name } };

    // ── saved outfits
    case 'SAVE_OUTFIT': {
      const src = a.outfit || { slots: state.draft.slots, context: state.draft.context, stage: state.draft.stage };
      const outfit = {
        id: src.id || uid('o'), name: a.name || src.name || 'Untitled look', ...(src.wardrobe ? { wardrobe: src.wardrobe } : {}), profileId: a.profileId || me, favorite: !!(a.favorite || src.favorite),
        ...(src.why ? { why: src.why } : {}), ...(src.source ? { source: src.source } : {}), ...(src.aiLook ? { aiLook: src.aiLook } : {}),
        slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...src.slots }, context: src.context || state.draft.context,
        stage: { ...(src.stage || { mode: 'flatlay' }), mode: src.stage?.mode === 'person' ? 'flatlay' : (src.stage?.mode || 'flatlay'), customBg: null, personPhoto: null },
        createdAt: Date.now(),
      };
      let catalogs = state.catalogs;
      if (a.newCatalog) catalogs = [{ id: uid('c'), outfitIds: [outfit.id], profileId: me, createdAt: Date.now(), ...a.newCatalog }, ...catalogs];
      else if (a.catalogId) catalogs = catalogs.map((c) => (c.id === a.catalogId ? { ...c, outfitIds: [...c.outfitIds, outfit.id] } : c));
      return { ...state, outfits: [outfit, ...state.outfits], catalogs, lastSavedId: outfit.id };
    }
    case 'SET_OUTFIT_LOOK': return { ...state, outfits: state.outfits.map((o) => (o.id === a.id ? { ...o, aiLook: a.look || undefined } : o)) };
    case 'TOGGLE_FAVORITE': return { ...state, outfits: state.outfits.map((o) => (o.id === a.id ? { ...o, favorite: !o.favorite } : o)) };
    case 'RENAME_OUTFIT': return { ...state, outfits: state.outfits.map((o) => (o.id === a.id ? { ...o, name: a.name } : o)) };
    case 'DELETE_OUTFIT': return {
      ...state,
      outfits: state.outfits.filter((o) => o.id !== a.id),
      catalogs: state.catalogs.map((c) => ({ ...c, outfitIds: c.outfitIds.filter((x) => x !== a.id) })),
    };
    case 'CREATE_CATALOG': return { ...state, catalogs: [{ id: uid('c'), outfitIds: [], profileId: me, createdAt: Date.now(), ...a.catalog }, ...state.catalogs] };
    case 'DELETE_CATALOG': return { ...state, catalogs: state.catalogs.filter((c) => c.id !== a.id) };
    case 'ADD_TO_CATALOG': return { ...state, catalogs: state.catalogs.map((c) => (c.id === a.catalogId && !c.outfitIds.includes(a.outfitId) ? { ...c, outfitIds: [...c.outfitIds, a.outfitId] } : c)) };
    case 'REMOVE_FROM_CATALOG': return { ...state, catalogs: state.catalogs.map((c) => (c.id === a.catalogId ? { ...c, outfitIds: c.outfitIds.filter((x) => x !== a.outfitId) } : c)) };

    // ── family looks
    case 'SAVE_ENSEMBLE': return { ...state, ensembles: [{ id: uid('e'), createdAt: Date.now(), ...a.ensemble }, ...state.ensembles] };
    case 'DELETE_ENSEMBLE': return { ...state, ensembles: state.ensembles.filter((e) => e.id !== a.id) };

    case 'CACHE_CRITIQUE': {
      const entries = Object.entries({ ...(state.aiCritiques || {}), [a.key]: a.critique }).slice(-30);
      return { ...state, aiCritiques: Object.fromEntries(entries) };
    }
    case 'ADD_INSPIRATION': return { ...state, inspirations: [{ profileId: me, ...a.inspiration }, ...state.inspirations] };
    case 'DELETE_INSPIRATION': return { ...state, inspirations: state.inspirations.filter((i) => i.id !== a.id) };
    // ── where clothes are kept
    case 'ADD_LOCATION': return state.locations.some((l) => l.name.toLowerCase() === a.location.name.toLowerCase()) ? state : { ...state, locations: [...state.locations, a.location] };
    case 'RENAME_LOCATION': return { ...state, locations: state.locations.map((l) => (l.id === a.id ? { ...l, name: a.name } : l)) };
    case 'DELETE_LOCATION': return { ...state, locations: state.locations.filter((l) => l.id !== a.id), garments: state.garments.map((g) => (g.location === a.id ? { ...g, location: null } : g)) };

    // ── outfit history & feedback
    case 'LOG_WEAR': return { ...state, history: [{ id: uid('w'), profileId: a.entry.profileId || me, date: a.entry.date || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...a.entry.slots }, context: a.entry.context || null, outfitId: a.entry.outfitId || null, createdAt: Date.now() }, ...state.history] };
    case 'RATE_WEAR': return { ...state, history: state.history.map((h) => (h.id === a.id ? { ...h, rating: a.rating ?? h.rating, tags: a.tags ?? h.tags, note: a.note ?? h.note } : h)) };
    case 'UPDATE_WEAR_DATE': return { ...state, history: state.history.map((h) => (h.id === a.id ? { ...h, date: a.date } : h)).sort((x, y) => (y.date || '').localeCompare(x.date || '')) };
    case 'DELETE_WEAR': return { ...state, history: state.history.filter((h) => h.id !== a.id) };

    // ── wardrobes (groups of saved outfits, per person)
    case 'ADD_WARDROBE': return { ...state, wardrobes: [...state.wardrobes, { id: a.wardrobe.id || uid('wr'), profileId: a.wardrobe.profileId || me, name: a.wardrobe.name }] };
    case 'RENAME_WARDROBE': return { ...state, wardrobes: state.wardrobes.map((w) => (w.id === a.id ? { ...w, name: a.name } : w)) };
    case 'DELETE_WARDROBE': return { ...state, wardrobes: state.wardrobes.filter((w) => w.id !== a.id), outfits: state.outfits.map((o) => (o.wardrobe === a.id ? { ...o, wardrobe: null } : o)) };
    case 'SET_OUTFIT_WARDROBE': return { ...state, outfits: state.outfits.map((o) => (o.id === a.id ? { ...o, wardrobe: a.wardrobe || null } : o)) };

    // ── calendar plans & trips
    case 'PLAN_DAY': {
      const others = state.plans.filter((p) => !(p.profileId === (a.plan.profileId || me) && p.date === a.plan.date));
      return { ...state, plans: [...others, { id: uid('pl'), profileId: a.plan.profileId || me, date: a.plan.date, slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...a.plan.slots }, name: a.plan.name || 'Planned outfit', outfitId: a.plan.outfitId || null, tripId: a.plan.tripId || null, context: a.plan.context || null }] };
    }
    case 'UNPLAN_DAY': return { ...state, plans: state.plans.filter((p) => !(p.profileId === (a.profileId || me) && p.date === a.date)) };
    case 'SAVE_TRIP': {
      const trip = { id: a.trip.id || uid('tr'), profileId: a.trip.profileId || me, name: a.trip.name, start: a.trip.start, end: a.trip.end, occasion: a.trip.occasion, weather: a.trip.weather };
      const days = new Set(a.days.map((d) => d.date));
      const kept = state.plans.filter((p) => !(p.profileId === trip.profileId && days.has(p.date)));
      const plans = a.days.map((d) => ({ id: uid('pl'), profileId: trip.profileId, date: d.date, slots: { ...emptySlots(), ...d.slots }, name: d.name, tripId: trip.id, context: d.context || null }));
      return { ...state, trips: [...state.trips.filter((t) => t.id !== trip.id), trip], plans: [...kept, ...plans] };
    }
    case 'DELETE_TRIP': return { ...state, trips: state.trips.filter((t) => t.id !== a.id), plans: state.plans.filter((p) => p.tripId !== a.id) };

    case 'DELETE_ALL': return { ...initialState(), onboarded: true };
    case 'RESET': return { ...initialState(), onboarded: true };
    case 'RESTORE': return a.state; // the same state with its photos filled in from this device
    case 'HYDRATE': return { ...migrate(a.state), onboarded: true };
    default: return state;
  }
}

const Ctx = createContext(null);

export function StoreProvider({ children }) {
  const [state, dispatch] = useReducer(reducer, undefined, load);
  // Photos are saved in IndexedDB (see photos.js). Until they are read back, the app waits (a moment).
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(() => refsIn(state).size === 0);
  const [deviceSave, setDeviceSave] = useState('ok'); // ok | full
  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) return;
    fromDevice(state).then((s) => { dispatch({ type: 'RESTORE', state: s }); setReady(true); });
  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  // Save to this device. One save at a time; if the closet changes meanwhile, the newest version is saved next.
  const queued = useRef(null), running = useRef(false), lastPrune = useRef(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready) return;
    queued.current = state;
    if (running.current) return;
    running.current = true;
    (async () => {
      while (queued.current) {
        const next = queued.current; queued.current = null;
        const saved = await forDevice(next);
        try { localStorage.setItem(KEY, JSON.stringify(saved)); setDeviceSave('ok'); }
        catch { setDeviceSave('full'); } // quota or private mode: kept in memory for now
        const used = refsIn(saved);
        if (!used.size) await local.clear().catch(() => {}); // nothing uses any photo (e.g. signed out, deleted)
        else if (Date.now() - lastPrune.current > 10 * 60e3) { lastPrune.current = Date.now(); await local.prune(used).catch(() => {}); }
      }
      running.current = false;
    })();
  }, [state, ready]);

  const api = useMemo(() => {
    const index = new Map(state.garments.map((g) => [g.id, g]));
    const get = (id) => (id ? index.get(id) || null : null);
    const resolve = (slots) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(slots || {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, get(v)]));
    const profile = state.profiles.find((p) => p.id === state.activeProfile) || state.profiles[0];
    const closetOf = (pid) => state.garments.filter((g) => g.ownerId === pid);
    const owned = closetOf(profile.id);           // the active member's closet
    const pool = owned;                           // recommendations only ever use what she owns
    const outfitsOf = (pid) => state.outfits.filter((o) => o.profileId === pid);
    const scoreOf = (outfit) => critique(resolve(outfit.slots), outfit.context).overall;
    const hasDemo = state.garments.some((g) => g.demo) || state.profiles.some((p) => p.demo);
    const historyOf = (pid) => state.history.filter((h) => h.profileId === pid);
    const personal = preferenceProfile(historyOf(profile.id), get);
    return { state, dispatch, get, resolve, owned, pool, profile, closetOf, outfitsOf, scoreOf, hasDemo, historyOf, personal, deviceSave };
  }, [state, deviceSave]);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={api}>{ready ? children : <div className="boot" aria-busy="true">Opening your closet…</div>}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

export const useStore = () => useContext(Ctx);

// Accounts (Supabase Auth) + cloud sync of the whole wardrobe: one row in public.wearwise_data per user,
// with the photos as separate private files in Supabase Storage (see photos.js).

// Keep people signed in across the rename: move the old sign-in key once.
function authKey() {
  const NEW = 'wearwise.auth', OLD = 'unique-ava.auth';
  try { if (!localStorage.getItem(NEW) && localStorage.getItem(OLD)) { localStorage.setItem(NEW, localStorage.getItem(OLD)); localStorage.removeItem(OLD); } } catch { /* ignore */ }
  return NEW;
}
import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { CONFIG } from './config.js';
import { useStore } from './store.jsx';
import { useToast } from './components/common.jsx';
import { forCloud, fromCloud, pruneCloud } from './photos.js';

const configured = !!(CONFIG.SUPABASE_URL && CONFIG.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY);
export const supabase = configured
  ? createClient(CONFIG.SUPABASE_URL, CONFIG.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, { auth: { persistSession: true, storageKey: authKey() } })
  : null;

/** Inside the claude.ai preview, the page can't reach other servers, so accounts only work on the live site. */
export const inClaudePreview = typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.claude && window.top !== window;

const Ctx = createContext(null);
export const useAuth = () => useContext(Ctx);

let currentSession = null;
/** Used by the AI function: the signed-in user's access token, or null. */
export const getAccessToken = () => currentSession?.access_token || null;
const authListeners = new Set();
export const onAuthChange = (f) => { authListeners.add(f); return () => authListeners.delete(f); };

function niceError(e) {
  const m = String(e?.message || e || '');
  if (/failed to fetch|networkerror|load failed/i.test(m)) return inClaudePreview
    ? 'Accounts work on the live site (GitHub Pages). The claude.ai preview can\'t reach Supabase.'
    : 'Couldn\'t reach the server. Check your connection and try again.';
  if (/invalid login credentials/i.test(m)) return 'That email and password don\'t match.';
  if (/email not confirmed/i.test(m)) return 'Please confirm your email first (check your inbox).';
  if (/already registered/i.test(m)) return 'That email already has an account. Try signing in.';
  if (/password should be at least/i.test(m)) return 'Use a password with at least 6 characters.';
  return m || 'Something went wrong.';
}

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [session, setSession] = useState(null);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(!configured);
  const [recovery, setRecovery] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) return;
    supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data }) => { setSession(data.session); setReady(true); }).catch(() => setReady(true));
    const { data } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((event, s) => { setSession(s); if (event === 'PASSWORD_RECOVERY') setRecovery(true); });
    return () => data.subscription.unsubscribe();
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => { currentSession = session; authListeners.forEach((f) => f(session)); }, [session]);

  const api = useMemo(() => {
    // Email links return to the address already approved in Supabase (unique-ava.html), which forwards
    // to wearwise.html keeping the sign-in token in the URL hash.
    const redirect = location.href.split('#')[0].split('?')[0].replace(/wearwise\.html$/, 'unique-ava.html');
    const wrap = (fn) => async (...a) => { try { return await fn(...a); } catch (e) { throw new Error(niceError(e)); } };
    return {
      configured,
      ready,
      recovery,
      endRecovery: () => setRecovery(false),
      user: session?.user || null,
      signIn: wrap(async (email, password) => {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({ email: email.trim().toLowerCase(), password });
        if (error) throw error;
      }),
      signUp: wrap(async (name, email, password) => {
        const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.signUp({ email: email.trim().toLowerCase(), password, options: { data: { name }, emailRedirectTo: redirect } });
        if (error) throw error;
        return { needsConfirm: !data.session };
      }),
      resetPassword: wrap(async (email) => {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email.trim().toLowerCase(), { redirectTo: redirect });
        if (error) throw error;
      }),
      updatePassword: wrap(async (password) => {
        const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password });
        if (error) throw error;
      }),
      signOut: wrap(async () => { await supabase.auth.signOut(); }),
    };
  }, [session, ready, recovery]);

  return <Ctx.Provider value={api}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>;
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────── cloud sync
const slim = (state) => ({
  ...state,
  draft: { ...state.draft, stage: { ...state.draft.stage, customBg: null, personPhoto: null } },
});

// Each signed-in user's closet lives in one row of public.wearwise_data (see supabase/wearwise.sql).
export async function cloudTable() {
  return 'wearwise_data';
}

// Set while "Delete all my data" runs, so nothing is re-uploaded in the meantime.
export const syncControl = { paused: false };

/**
 * Keeps the signed-in user's wardrobe in Supabase.
 * - On sign-in the account copy wins (or is created from this device).
 * - Saves are debounced and CONDITIONAL: a save only goes through if the account copy is still the version this
 *   device last saw. If another device saved in between, nothing is overwritten: she chooses which version to keep.
 * - When this tab comes back into view, newer changes from another device are loaded (if this device has none pending).
 * - A failed save (e.g. no connection) is retried when the connection returns, and leaving with unsaved changes warns.
 * Returns { status, message, lastSaved, conflict, useCloudVersion, keepThisVersion }.
 */
export function useCloudSync() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('off'); // off | loading | synced | saving | error | offline | conflict | needs-setup
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [lastSaved, setLastSaved] = useState(null);
  const [conflict, setConflict] = useState(null); // { data, at }
  const readyFor = useRef(null);
  const timer = useRef(null);
  const prevUser = useRef(null);
  const seen = useRef(null);       // updated_at of the account copy this device last loaded or saved
  const dirty = useRef(false);     // local changes not yet saved
  const skipNext = useRef(false);  // don't echo a just-loaded cloud copy straight back
  const latest = useRef(state);
  latest.current = state;

  const fail = useCallback((error) => {
    const m = String(error?.message || error?.code || '');
    if (/does not exist|PGRST205|42P01|schema cache|bucket not found/i.test(m + (error?.code || ''))) {
      setStatus('needs-setup'); setMessage('Cloud storage isn\'t set up yet. Run supabase/wearwise.sql once in Supabase.');
    } else if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && navigator.onLine === false || /Failed to fetch|NetworkError|network/i.test(m)) {
      setStatus('offline'); setMessage('No connection. Your changes are kept on this device and will be saved when you\'re back online.');
    } else { setStatus('error'); setMessage(niceError(error)); }
  }, []);

  const busy = useRef(false), again = useRef(false), changes = useRef(0), lastPrune = useRef(0);
  const save = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!supabase || !user || readyFor.current !== user.id || syncControl.paused) return;
    if (busy.current) { again.current = true; return; } // one save at a time (two at once would look like a conflict)
    busy.current = true;
    try { await saveOnce(); } finally { busy.current = false; }
    if (again.current) { again.current = false; if (dirty.current) save(); }
  }, [user, fail]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const saveOnce = async () => {
    const T = await cloudTable();
    setStatus('saving');
    const at = changes.current;
    let data;
    try { data = await forCloud(supabase, user.id, slim(latest.current)); } // photos first, then the row that mentions them
    catch (e) { fail(e); return; }
    const payload = { data, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() };
    let res = seen.current
      ? await supabase.from(T).update(payload).eq('user_id', user.id).eq('updated_at', seen.current).select('updated_at')
      : await supabase.from(T).upsert({ user_id: user.id, ...payload }).select('updated_at');
    if (res.error) { fail(res.error); return; }
    if (!res.data?.length) {
      // Nothing matched: the account copy changed on another device, or was deleted.
      const cur = await supabase.from(T).select('data, updated_at').eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle();
      if (cur.error) { fail(cur.error); return; }
      if (!cur.data) {
        res = await supabase.from(T).upsert({ user_id: user.id, ...payload }).select('updated_at');
        if (res.error) { fail(res.error); return; }
      } else {
        setConflict({ data: cur.data.data, at: cur.data.updated_at });
        setStatus('conflict'); setMessage('Your closet was changed on another device.');
        return;
      }
    }
    seen.current = res.data[0].updated_at;
    dirty.current = changes.current !== at; // changed again while saving → the next save is already scheduled
    setStatus(dirty.current ? 'saving' : 'synced'); setLastSaved(new Date()); setMessage('');
    if (Date.now() - lastPrune.current > 10 * 60e3) { // now and then, remove account photos nothing uses any more
      lastPrune.current = Date.now();
      pruneCloud(supabase, user.id, data).catch(() => {});
    }
  };

  // On sign-in: load the account copy (it wins), or create it from this device.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase) return;
    if (!user) {
      if (prevUser.current) dispatch({ type: 'RESET' }); // signed out: don't leave this closet for the next person
      prevUser.current = null; readyFor.current = null; seen.current = null; dirty.current = false; setConflict(null); setStatus('off');
      return;
    }
    if (prevUser.current === user.id) return;
    prevUser.current = user.id;
    setStatus('loading');
    (async () => {
      const T = await cloudTable();
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from(T).select('data, updated_at').eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle();
      if (error) { fail(error); return; }
      if (data?.data && Object.keys(data.data).length) {
        let full;
        try { full = await fromCloud(supabase, user.id, data.data); } catch (e) { fail(e); return; }
        skipNext.current = true;
        dispatch({ type: 'HYDRATE', state: full });
        seen.current = data.updated_at;
        toast('Your wardrobe is loaded from your account');
        readyFor.current = user.id;
        setStatus('synced'); setLastSaved(new Date()); setMessage('');
      } else {
        readyFor.current = user.id;
        seen.current = data?.updated_at || null;
        await save();
        toast('Your closet is now saved to your account');
      }
    })();
  }, [user]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  // After that: save changes (debounced).
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase || !user || readyFor.current !== user.id) return;
    if (skipNext.current) { skipNext.current = false; return; }
    if (conflict) return; // wait for her choice
    dirty.current = true; changes.current++;
    clearTimeout(timer.current);
    setStatus('saving');
    timer.current = setTimeout(save, 1500);
    return () => clearTimeout(timer.current);
  }, [state]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  // Back online → retry. Back to this tab → pick up changes made on another device.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!supabase || !user) return undefined;
    const online = () => { if (dirty.current) save(); };
    const visible = async () => {
      if (document.visibilityState !== 'visible' || readyFor.current !== user.id || conflict || syncControl.paused) return;
      const T = await cloudTable();
      const cur = await supabase.from(T).select('data, updated_at').eq('user_id', user.id).maybeSingle();
      if (cur.error || !cur.data || cur.data.updated_at === seen.current) return;
      if (dirty.current) { setConflict({ data: cur.data.data, at: cur.data.updated_at }); setStatus('conflict'); setMessage('Your closet was changed on another device.'); return; }
      let full;
      try { full = await fromCloud(supabase, user.id, cur.data.data); } catch { return; } // try again next time
      if (dirty.current) return; // she changed something while it loaded: the next save will show the choice
      skipNext.current = true;
      dispatch({ type: 'HYDRATE', state: full });
      seen.current = cur.data.updated_at;
      setLastSaved(new Date());
      toast('Updated with changes from your other device');
    };
    const leaving = (e) => { if (dirty.current) { e.preventDefault(); e.returnValue = ''; } };
    window.addEventListener('online', online);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', visible);
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', leaving);
    return () => { window.removeEventListener('online', online); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', visible); window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', leaving); };
  }, [user, conflict, save]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const useCloudVersion = async () => {
    if (!conflict) return;
    let full;
    try { full = await fromCloud(supabase, user.id, conflict.data); } catch (e) { toast('Couldn\'t load the other version: ' + niceError(e)); return; }
    skipNext.current = true;
    dispatch({ type: 'HYDRATE', state: full });
    seen.current = conflict.at; dirty.current = false;
    setConflict(null); setStatus('synced'); setMessage(''); setLastSaved(new Date());
    toast('Loaded the version from your other device');
  };
  const keepThisVersion = async () => {
    if (!conflict) return;
    seen.current = conflict.at;
    setConflict(null);
    await save();
    toast('Kept this device\'s version');
  };

  return { status, message, lastSaved, conflict, useCloudVersion, keepThisVersion };
}

// Claude connection layer. One interface, two transports, graceful fallback.
//   provider.json(prompt, { images?: Blob[], tier?: 'quick'|'default'|'complex' }) → parsed JSON
// See src/config.js for how each transport is switched on.
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { CONFIG } from '../config.js';
import { getAccessToken, onAuthChange } from '../auth.jsx';

let providerPromise = null;
let disabledReason = null; // set when the viewer declines, so we stop asking
let notSetUp = false;      // the Supabase function answered "no AI key yet"

/** Tolerant JSON parse: whole text, a ```fence```, or first {/[ … last }/]. */
export function parseJsonLoose(text) {
  const t = String(text || '').trim();
  try { return JSON.parse(t); } catch { /* keep trying */ }
  const fence = t.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/);
  if (fence) { try { return JSON.parse(fence[1]); } catch { /* keep trying */ } }
  const start = t.search(/[[{]/);
  const end = Math.max(t.lastIndexOf('}'), t.lastIndexOf(']'));
  if (start >= 0 && end > start) return JSON.parse(t.slice(start, end + 1));
  throw Object.assign(new Error('Claude did not return JSON'), { code: 'invalid_json' });
}

async function blobToBase64(blob) {
  const buf = new Uint8Array(await blob.arrayBuffer());
  let s = '';
  for (let i = 0; i < buf.length; i += 0x8000) s += String.fromCharCode(...buf.subarray(i, i + 0x8000));
  return btoa(s);
}

async function fromClaudeAi() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !window.claude?.use) return null;
  const sample = await window.claude.use('sample').catch(() => null);
  if (!sample) return null;
  const limits = await sample.limits().catch(() => null);
  return {
    name: 'Claude (your claude.ai account)',
    images: !!limits?.images,
    async json(prompt, { images, tier = 'default', signal } = {}) {
      const opts = { modelTier: tier };
      if (signal) opts.signal = signal;
      if (images?.length && limits?.images) opts.images = images;
      return sample.json(prompt, opts);
    },
  };
}

// ── Gemini through Puter.js: no API key anywhere. The user signs in to a free Puter
// account (a popup the first time) and her AI use runs on her own Puter allowance.
const PUTER_SRC = 'https://js.puter.com/v2/';
const PUTER_MODELS = {
  default: ['google/gemini-3.5-flash', 'google/gemini-2.5-flash'],
  quick: ['google/gemini-3.5-flash-lite', 'google/gemini-3.5-flash', 'google/gemini-2.5-flash'],
};
let puterPromise = null;
export function loadPuter() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return Promise.resolve(null);
  if (window.puter) return Promise.resolve(window.puter);
  if (!puterPromise) {
    puterPromise = new Promise((resolve) => {
      const s = document.createElement('script');
      s.src = PUTER_SRC;
      s.async = true;
      s.onload = () => resolve(window.puter || null);
      s.onerror = () => resolve(null);
      document.head.appendChild(s);
      setTimeout(() => resolve(window.puter || null), 15000);
    }).then((p) => { if (!p) puterPromise = null; return p; });
  }
  return puterPromise;
}

const blobToDataUrl = (b) => new Promise((res, rej) => { const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = () => res(r.result); r.onerror = rej; r.readAsDataURL(b); });

function textOf(r) {
  if (r == null) return '';
  if (typeof r === 'string') return r;
  const c = r.message?.content ?? r.content ?? r.text;
  if (typeof c === 'string') return c;
  if (Array.isArray(c)) return c.map((p) => (typeof p === 'string' ? p : p?.text || '')).join('');
  return String(r);
}

export function puterError(e) {
  const msg = String(e?.error?.message || e?.message || e?.error || e || '');
  const code = String(e?.error?.code || e?.code || '');
  if (/insufficient|funds|credit|allowance|quota|limit/i.test(msg + code)) return Object.assign(new Error(msg), { code: 'rate_limited', serverMessage: 'Your free AI allowance on Puter is used up for now. It refreshes monthly, or you can top up on puter.com.' });
  if (/auth|sign.?in|log.?in|cancel|closed|denied|401/i.test(msg + code)) return Object.assign(new Error(msg), { code: 'not_signed_in' });
  if (/model/i.test(msg) && /not|unknown|unsupported|invalid/i.test(msg)) return Object.assign(new Error(msg), { code: 'bad_model' });
  return Object.assign(new Error(msg || 'Puter error'), { code: 'upstream_error' });
}

function fromPuter() {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return null;
  return {
    name: 'Gemini',
    via: 'puter',
    images: true,
    async json(prompt, { images, tier = 'default' } = {}) {
      const puter = await loadPuter();
      if (!puter) throw Object.assign(new Error('Could not load Puter'), { code: 'upstream_error', serverMessage: 'The AI service (Puter) could not be reached.' });
      // Never let a background request pop up Puter's dialog: sign-in only happens when she clicks "Sign in".
      let signedIn = false;
      try { signedIn = await puter.auth.isSignedIn(); } catch { /* treat as signed out */ }
      if (!signedIn) throw Object.assign(new Error('Not signed in to Puter'), { code: 'not_signed_in' });
      const img = images?.[0] || null;
      const dataUrl = img ? await blobToDataUrl(img) : null;
      let lastErr = null;
      for (const model of PUTER_MODELS[tier] || PUTER_MODELS.default) {
        try {
          let r;
          if (!img) r = await puter.ai.chat(prompt, { model });
          else {
            try { r = await puter.ai.chat(prompt, dataUrl, { model }); }
            catch (e) {
              const pe = puterError(e);
              if (pe.code === 'not_signed_in' || pe.code === 'rate_limited' || pe.code === 'bad_model') throw e;
              // Fallback: put the cut-out in the user's own Puter storage and send it from there.
              const ext = (img.type.split('/')[1] || 'png').replace('jpeg', 'jpg');
              const file = await puter.fs.write(`wearwise/ai-${Date.now()}.${ext}`, img, { createMissingParents: true });
              r = await puter.ai.chat([{ role: 'user', content: [{ type: 'file', puter_path: file.path }, { type: 'text', text: prompt }] }], { model });
              puter.fs.delete(file.path).catch(() => {});
            }
          }
          if (r && r.success === false) throw r;
          const text = textOf(r);
          if (!text.trim()) throw Object.assign(new Error('Empty answer'), { code: 'upstream_error' });
          return parseJsonLoose(text);
        } catch (e) {
          lastErr = e?.code === 'invalid_json' ? e : puterError(e);
          if (lastErr.code !== 'bad_model') throw lastErr; // only a missing model is worth trying the next one
        }
      }
      throw lastErr;
    },
  };
}

/** Puter sign-in state, for the header badge. */
export async function puterUser() {
  if (!window.puter) return null;
  try { return (await window.puter.auth.isSignedIn()) ? await window.puter.auth.getUser() : null; } catch { return null; }
}
/** Must be called straight from a click (it opens Puter's sign-in pop-up). */
export function puterSignIn() {
  if (!window.puter?.auth?.signIn) return Promise.reject(new Error('Puter is still loading'));
  return window.puter.auth.signIn().then((u) => { notify(); return u; });
}
export function puterSignOut() { try { window.puter?.auth?.signOut(); } catch { /* ignore */ } notify(); }

// Optional backup: the unique-ava-ai Supabase function (needs a key on the server; off by default).
async function fromProxy() {
  if (!CONFIG.AI_PROXY_URL) return null;
  let status = null;
  try {
    const r = await fetch(CONFIG.AI_PROXY_URL, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', apikey: CONFIG.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY }, body: JSON.stringify({ task: 'status' }) });
    status = r.ok ? await r.json() : null;
  } catch { status = null; }
  if (!status) return null;                       // unreachable (e.g. inside the claude.ai preview)
  if (!status.ready) { notSetUp = true; return null; } // reachable, but no AI key added yet
  return {
    name: status.provider || 'Gemini',
    images: true,
    needsLogin: true,
    async json(prompt, { images, tier = 'default', signal } = {}) {
      const token = getAccessToken();
      if (!token) throw Object.assign(new Error('Sign in to use the AI stylist'), { code: 'not_signed_in' });
      const imgs = [];
      for (const b of images || []) imgs.push({ media_type: b.type || 'image/png', data: await blobToBase64(b) });
      const res = await fetch(CONFIG.AI_PROXY_URL, {
        method: 'POST',
        signal,
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          apikey: CONFIG.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({ prompt, images: imgs, tier }),
      });
      const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
      if (!res.ok) throw Object.assign(new Error(body.error || `AI error ${res.status}`), { code: res.status === 429 ? 'rate_limited' : res.status === 401 ? 'not_signed_in' : 'upstream_error', serverMessage: body.error });
      return parseJsonLoose(body.text);
    },
  };
}

/** Tests only: force a provider (undefined = back to normal detection). */
export function __setProviderForTests(p) { providerPromise = p === undefined ? null : Promise.resolve(p); }

/** Resolves the best available provider, or null (→ rules engine). */
export function getClaude() {
  if (disabledReason) return Promise.resolve(null);
  // Live site: Gemini through Puter (no key). Inside the claude.ai preview, which blocks outside
  // scripts, Claude stands in.
  const inClaudeFrame = typeof window !== 'undefined' && !!window.claude && window.top !== window;
  if (!providerPromise) providerPromise = (async () => (inClaudeFrame ? await fromClaudeAi() : null) || (await fromProxy()) || fromPuter())();
  return providerPromise;
}

/** Call when a request fails, so the UI can fall back and stop asking if it's permanent. */
export function noteFailure(e) {
  const permanent = ['not_granted', 'sampling_disabled', 'not_declared', 'capability_disabled', 'capability_removed'];
  if (permanent.includes(e?.code)) { disabledReason = e.code; providerPromise = Promise.resolve(null); notify(); }
  return friendlyError(e);
}

export function friendlyError(e) {
  switch (e?.code) {
    case 'not_granted': return 'AI access was declined, so Wearwise is using its built-in rules engine.';
    case 'rate_limited': return e.serverMessage || 'The AI is busy or the daily limit was reached. Try again later.';
    case 'session_expired': return 'Please sign in again.';
    case 'image_rejected': return 'The AI could not read that image. Try another photo.';
    case 'invalid_json': return 'The AI answered in an unexpected format. Try again.';
    case 'cancelled': return '';
    case 'not_signed_in': return 'Gemini is off because you\'re not signed in to Puter (free). These outfits come from the built-in rules engine.';
    default: return (e?.serverMessage ? e.serverMessage + ' ' : '') + 'Showing the built-in rules engine instead.';
  }
}

// ── tiny subscription so the header badge updates
const listeners = new Set();
const notify = () => listeners.forEach((f) => f());
onAuthChange(() => notify()); // proxy availability depends on being signed in

/** React hook: { status: 'checking'|'ready'|'offline', name } */
export function useClaudeStatus() {
  const [s, setS] = useState({ status: 'checking', name: '' });
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    const check = () => getClaude().then((p) => {
      if (!alive) return;
      if (p && p.needsLogin && !getAccessToken()) setS({ status: 'signin', name: p.name });
      else if (p?.via === 'puter') {
        setS({ status: 'checking', name: p.name, via: 'puter', images: true });
        loadPuter().then(async (pt) => {
          let ok = false;
          try { ok = !!pt && (await pt.auth.isSignedIn()); } catch { /* signed out */ }
          if (alive) setS(pt ? { status: ok ? 'ready' : 'signin', name: p.name, images: true, via: 'puter' } : { status: 'offline', name: '' });
        });
      } else setS(p ? { status: 'ready', name: p.name, images: p.images, via: p.via } : { status: notSetUp ? 'notsetup' : 'offline', name: disabledReason ? 'declined' : '' });
      if (p?.via === 'puter') loadPuter(); // warm up in the background so sign-in is instant
    });
    check();
    listeners.add(check);
    return () => { alive = false; listeners.delete(check); };
  }, []);
  return s;
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/color.js

// Color helpers used by the critic, the tagger and the UI.

export const NAMED_COLORS = [
  { name: 'black', hex: '#1E1E1E' },
  { name: 'charcoal', hex: '#3C3D40' },
  { name: 'gray', hex: '#8E8F92' },
  { name: 'light gray', hex: '#C9C9C9' },
  { name: 'white', hex: '#F7F6F2' },
  { name: 'off-white', hex: '#EDE7DA' },
  { name: 'cream', hex: '#E8DCC2' },
  { name: 'beige', hex: '#D4C2A1' },
  { name: 'camel', hex: '#B88A55' },
  { name: 'brown', hex: '#6B4A33' },
  { name: 'navy', hex: '#1F2A44' },
  { name: 'denim blue', hex: '#3E5C82' },
  { name: 'light blue', hex: '#A9C3DB' },
  { name: 'olive', hex: '#5B5F3A' },
  { name: 'khaki', hex: '#A79B6E' },
  { name: 'green', hex: '#2F6B4A' },
  { name: 'burgundy', hex: '#6E1F2E' },
  { name: 'red', hex: '#C2272D' },
  { name: 'rust', hex: '#A4492A' },
  { name: 'mustard', hex: '#C9A13B' },
  { name: 'pink', hex: '#E3A7B3' },
  { name: 'lavender', hex: '#B5A8CF' },
];

// Colors that behave as neutrals (light blue = the classic oxford-shirt neutral) when styling (they pair with almost anything).
export const NEUTRALS = new Set([
  'black', 'charcoal', 'gray', 'light gray', 'white', 'off-white', 'cream', 'beige', 'camel',
  'brown', 'navy', 'denim blue', 'light blue', 'khaki',
]);

export function hexToRgb(hex) {
  const h = hex.replace('#', '');
  const n = parseInt(h.length === 3 ? h.split('').map((c) => c + c).join('') : h, 16);
  return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255];
}

export function rgbToHex([r, g, b]) {
  return '#' + [r, g, b].map((v) => Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(v))).toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('');
}

export function rgbToHsl([r, g, b]) {
  r /= 255; g /= 255; b /= 255;
  const max = Math.max(r, g, b), min = Math.min(r, g, b);
  let h = 0, s = 0;
  const l = (max + min) / 2;
  if (max !== min) {
    const d = max - min;
    s = l > 0.5 ? d / (2 - max - min) : d / (max + min);
    if (max === r) h = (g - b) / d + (g < b ? 6 : 0);
    else if (max === g) h = (b - r) / d + 2;
    else h = (r - g) / d + 4;
    h *= 60;
  }
  return [h, s, l];
}

export const hexToHsl = (hex) => rgbToHsl(hexToRgb(hex));

/** Lighten (amt > 0) or darken (amt < 0) a hex color. amt in -1..1 */
export function shade(hex, amt) {
  const rgb = hexToRgb(hex);
  return rgbToHex(rgb.map((v) => (amt < 0 ? v * (1 + amt) : v + (255 - v) * amt)));
}

export function nearestNamedColor(rgb) {
  let best = NAMED_COLORS[0], bestD = Infinity;
  for (const c of NAMED_COLORS) {
    const [r, g, b] = hexToRgb(c.hex);
    // weighted RGB distance (rough perceptual)
    const d = 2 * (r - rgb[0]) ** 2 + 4 * (g - rgb[1]) ** 2 + 3 * (b - rgb[2]) ** 2;
    if (d < bestD) { bestD = d; best = c; }
  }
  return best;
}

export const isNeutral = (colorName) => NEUTRALS.has(colorName);

/** Smallest angle between two hues (0–180). */
export function hueDistance(a, b) {
  const d = Math.abs(a - b) % 360;
  return d > 180 ? 360 - d : d;
}

/** 'warm' | 'cool' | 'neutral' for an accent color. */
export function temperature(hex) {
  const [h, s] = hexToHsl(hex);
  if (s < 0.15) return 'neutral';
  if (h < 70 || h >= 330) return 'warm';
  if (h >= 160 && h < 300) return 'cool';
  return 'neutral';
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/context.js
====================================================================================================

// Turns a free-text situation ("Rainy Tuesday coffee meeting, 16°C") into structured context.
// In production, vague prompts fall back to an optional AI Edge Function (task: "parse-context").

const OCCASIONS = [
  { id: 'wedding', label: 'Wedding', formality: 4.5, re: /wedding|gala|black tie|ceremony|formal/ },
  { id: 'church', label: 'Church', formality: 3.5, re: /church|mass|service|sunday worship/ },
  { id: 'school', label: 'School', formality: 2, re: /school|class|campus|university|college/ },
  { id: 'party', label: 'Party', formality: 3, re: /party|birthday|celebration|club/ },
  { id: 'sports', label: 'Sports', formality: 1, re: /gym|sport|run|jog|workout|tennis|game/ },
  { id: 'special', label: 'Interview', formality: 4, re: /interview|pitch|client|board/ },
  { id: 'work', label: 'Work', formality: 3.5, re: /office|work|meeting|presentation|conference|business/ },
  { id: 'date', label: 'Date', formality: 3, re: /dinner|date|restaurant|theatre|theater|bar|drinks/ },
  { id: 'coffee', label: 'Coffee / brunch', formality: 2.5, re: /coffee|caf[eé]|brunch|lunch/ },
  { id: 'vacation', label: 'Vacation', formality: 1.5, re: /vacation|holiday|trip|travel|sightseeing|temple|museum|tour|airport|flight/ },
  { id: 'weekend', label: 'Weekend errands', formality: 1.5, re: /weekend|errand|market|grocer|casual|home|lazy/ },
  { id: 'outdoor', label: 'Beach / outdoors', formality: 1, re: /beach|park|hike|picnic|pool|festival|garden/ },
];

const CITIES = ['tokyo', 'kyoto', 'osaka', 'seoul', 'manila', 'paris', 'london', 'new york', 'milan', 'copenhagen', 'singapore', 'bangkok', 'sydney', 'berlin', 'lisbon'];

export function seasonForTemp(t) {
  if (t >= 25) return 'summer';
  if (t >= 16) return 'spring';
  if (t >= 8) return 'autumn';
  return 'winter';
}

export function parseContext(prompt = '') {
  const text = prompt.toLowerCase();

  // Temperature
  let tempC = null;
  const m = text.match(/(-?\d{1,3}(?:\.\d)?)\s*°?\s*(c|f)\b/) || text.match(/(-?\d{1,3})\s*°/);
  if (m) {
    tempC = parseFloat(m[1]);
    if (m[2] === 'f') tempC = Math.round(((tempC - 32) * 5) / 9);
  }
  if (tempC === null) {
    if (/snow|freezing|winter/.test(text)) tempC = 3;
    else if (/beach|tropical|summer|heat|hot|humid/.test(text)) tempC = 30;
    else if (/autumn|fall|crisp/.test(text)) tempC = 14;
    else if (/spring/.test(text)) tempC = 18;
    else if (/cold|chilly/.test(text)) tempC = 9;
    else tempC = 21;
  }

  // Rain
  const rain = /rain|drizzle|shower|wet|storm|monsoon|typhoon/.test(text) ? 0.8 : /cloud|overcast|grey|gray/.test(text) ? 0.3 : 0.05;
  const wind = /wind|breezy|gust/.test(text);

  // Occasion
  const occ = OCCASIONS.find((o) => o.re.test(text)) || { id: 'everyday', label: 'Everyday', formality: 2 };
  const style = STYLE_WORDS.find(([, re]) => re.test(text))?.[0] || null;

  // Place
  const city = CITIES.find((c) => text.includes(c));

  const weather = rain >= 0.6 ? 'Rainy' : tempC >= 27 ? 'Hot' : tempC >= 20 ? 'Mild' : tempC >= 12 ? 'Cool' : 'Cold';

  return {
    prompt,
    tempC,
    rain,
    wind,
    occasion: occ.id,
    occasionLabel: occ.label,
    formality: occ.formality,
    city: city ? city.replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase()) : null,
    season: seasonForTemp(tempC),
    weather,
    style,
    prefs: [],
  };
}

const STYLE_WORDS = [
  ['elegant', /elegant|chic|glam/], ['minimalist', /minimal/], ['streetwear', /street/], ['preppy', /preppy/],
  ['sporty', /sporty|athleisure/], ['feminine', /feminine|girly|romantic/], ['edgy', /edgy|rock|grunge/],
  ['business', /business casual|smart casual/], ['classic', /classic|timeless/],
];

// ── Quick choices (Create an Outfit chips) → context
export const WEATHER_CHOICES = [
  { id: 'hot', label: 'Hot', tempC: 31, rain: 0.05 },
  { id: 'warm', label: 'Warm', tempC: 25, rain: 0.05 },
  { id: 'cool', label: 'Cool', tempC: 15, rain: 0.1 },
  { id: 'cold', label: 'Cold', tempC: 5, rain: 0.1 },
  { id: 'rainy', label: 'Rainy', tempC: 17, rain: 0.8 },
];
export const PREF_CHOICES = [
  { id: 'comfortable', label: 'Comfortable' },
  { id: 'formal', label: 'More formal' },
  { id: 'colorful', label: 'More colorful' },
  { id: 'simple', label: 'Simple' },
  { id: 'standout', label: 'Stand out' },
];

/**
 * Build a context from the quick choices. Free text (optional) can add details like a
 * city or an exact temperature; chips always win for the fields they set.
 */
export function buildContext({ occasion = 'everyday', style = null, weather = 'warm', prefs = [], text = '' }, occasions) {
  const base = parseContext(text || '');
  const occ = occasions.find((o) => o.id === occasion) || occasions[0];
  const w = WEATHER_CHOICES.find((x) => x.id === weather) || WEATHER_CHOICES[1];
  const exactTemp = /-?\d+\s*°?\s*[cf]\b|\d+\s*°/i.test(text || '');
  const tempC = exactTemp ? base.tempC : w.tempC;
  const rain = weather === 'rainy' ? 0.8 : exactTemp && base.rain >= 0.6 ? base.rain : w.rain;
  const formality = Math.min(5, occ.formality + (prefs.includes('formal') ? 1 : 0));
  const label = [occ.label, w.label.toLowerCase(), style ? STYLE_LABEL[style] : null].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  return {
    ...base,
    prompt: text?.trim() ? `${label} · ${text.trim()}` : label,
    tempC, rain,
    occasion: occ.id, occasionLabel: occ.label, formality,
    season: seasonForTemp(tempC),
    weather: rain >= 0.6 ? 'Rainy' : tempC >= 27 ? 'Hot' : tempC >= 20 ? 'Warm' : tempC >= 12 ? 'Cool' : 'Cold',
    style: style || base.style, prefs,
  };
}
const STYLE_LABEL = { casual: 'casual', minimalist: 'minimalist', elegant: 'elegant', classic: 'classic', streetwear: 'streetwear', preppy: 'preppy', sporty: 'sporty', feminine: 'feminine', edgy: 'edgy', business: 'business casual' };

export const DEFAULT_CONTEXT = parseContext('Rainy Tuesday coffee meeting, 16°C');
export const EXAMPLE_PROMPTS_OLD = [
  'Rainy Tuesday coffee meeting, 16°C',
  'Sunny beach café, 30°C',
  'Office presentation, 22°C',
  'Kyoto temples in autumn, 12°C',
  'Winter dinner date in Seoul, 2°C',
];

export const EXAMPLE_PROMPTS = [
  'Rainy Tuesday coffee meeting, 16°C',
  'Sunday church, warm',
  'Office presentation, 22°C',
  'Dinner date, 18°C',
  'Beach vacation, 30°C',
];


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/critic.js
====================================================================================================

// The Stylist Critic: a deterministic rules engine (no AI) covering the brief's eight quality checks:
// color harmony, proportion, occasion, weather, style consistency, footwear, accessories, comfort.
// It produces scores + specific findings. The AI stylist (Gemini) may add taste on top, but is
// told not to contradict these facts. See docs/ARCHITECTURE.md §4.2.

import { hexToHsl, hueDistance, isNeutral, temperature } from './color.js';
import { slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';

const NOUN = {
  tee: 'tee', turtleneck: 'turtleneck', shirt: 'shirt', stripe: 'striped tee', knit: 'sweater',
  trousers: 'trousers', jeans: 'jeans', shorts: 'shorts', skirt: 'skirt',
  trench: 'trench', blazer: 'blazer', coat: 'coat', puffer: 'down jacket', jacket: 'jacket', cardigan: 'cardigan',
  sneaker: 'sneakers', loafer: 'loafers', boot: 'boots', sandal: 'sandals',
  tote: 'tote', scarf: 'scarf', crossbody: 'crossbody bag', hat: 'hat', necklace: 'necklace', belt: 'belt',
  dress: 'dress', generic: 'piece',
};
const VOLUME = { fitted: -1, regular: 0, relaxed: 1, oversized: 2 };
const FORMALITY_LABEL = ['', 'Lounge', 'Casual', 'Smart casual', 'Business', 'Formal'];

/** Short, human label: "olive trousers" */
export function short(item) {
  if (!item) return '';
  const noun = NOUN[item.art] || item.category;
  let color = item.colors?.[0]?.name || '';
  if (item.art === 'jeans' && /denim/.test(color)) color = 'blue';
  return `${color} ${noun}`.trim();
}

const cap = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);
const PLURAL = new Set(['trousers', 'jeans', 'shorts', 'sneaker', 'loafer', 'boot', 'sandal']);
const plural = (i) => PLURAL.has(i?.art);
const isAre = (items) => (items.length > 1 || plural(items[0]) ? 'are' : 'is');
/** "a date", "church", "work" … */
export const occ = (ctx) => {
  const l = (ctx.occasionLabel || 'today').toLowerCase();
  return ['date', 'party', 'wedding', 'special occasion', 'interview'].includes(l) ? `${aAn(l)} ${l}` : l;
};
const aAn = (w) => (/^[aeiou]/i.test(w) ? 'an' : 'a');
const clamp = (v, lo = 0, hi = 100) => Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, Math.round(v)));
const pieces = (o) => ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
export const isDress = (item) => item?.category === 'dress';
/** True when the outfit has the essentials: (top + bottom) or a dress, plus shoes. */
export const isComplete = (o) => !!(o.top && (o.bottom || isDress(o.top)) && o.shoes);

// ───────────────────────────────── Pillar 1: Proportions & Silhouette
function proportions(o) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  let s = 74;
  const { top, bottom, outer, shoes } = o;
  if (isDress(top)) {
    s = 82;
    pros.push(`The ${short(top)} gives one clean, unbroken line.`);
    if (outer?.length === 'long') { s += 4; pros.push(`A long ${short(outer)} over the dress keeps the long, elegant line.`); }
    if (outer?.length === 'cropped') { s += 3; pros.push(`A cropped ${short(outer)} marks the waist over the dress.`); }
    if (shoes?.profile === 'chunky' && top.formality >= 4) { s -= 6; cons.push(`Chunky ${short(shoes)} feel heavy under a dressy ${short(top)}. Sleeker shoes would suit it better.`); }
    return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons };
  }
  if (!top || !bottom) {
    return { score: 50, pros, cons: ['Add both a top and a bottom (or a dress) so the silhouette can be judged.'] };
  }
  const tv = VOLUME[top.fit] ?? 0, bv = VOLUME[bottom.fit] ?? 0;

  if (tv >= 1 && bv >= 1) {
    if (tv + bv >= 3) { s -= 22; cons.push(`Volume on volume: the ${top.fit} ${short(top)} and ${bottom.fit} ${short(bottom)} swallow your frame. Tuck the top, or swap in a slimmer bottom.`); }
    else { s -= 8; cons.push(`Two relaxed pieces can read slouchy. A half-tuck of the ${short(top)} would define the waist.`); }
  } else if ((tv >= 1 && bv <= 0) || (bv >= 1 && tv <= 0)) {
    s += 14;
    const loose = tv >= 1 ? top : bottom, neat = tv >= 1 ? bottom : top;
    pros.push(`The ${loose.fit} ${short(loose)} ${plural(loose) ? 'are' : 'is'} balanced by the ${neat.fit === 'fitted' ? 'slimmer' : 'neater'} ${short(neat)}.`);
  } else if (tv < 0 && bv < 0) {
    s += 4;
    pros.push('Streamlined, close-to-body line from top to hem.');
    if (!outer) cons.push('All-fitted can feel a little severe. A relaxed outer layer would add ease.');
  } else {
    s += 6;
    pros.push('Easy, classic proportions that flatter without trying too hard.');
  }

  if (outer) {
    if (outer.length === 'long' && bottom.art === 'shorts') { s -= 12; cons.push(`A long ${short(outer)} over shorts cuts the leg at an awkward point.`); }
    else if (outer.length === 'long' && bv >= 1) { s += 6; pros.push(`The long ${short(outer)} over wide ${short(bottom)} makes one long, elegant column.`); }
    else if (outer.length === 'cropped' && tv >= 2) { s -= 8; cons.push(`The cropped ${short(outer)} lets the oversized ${short(top)} hang out unevenly below it.`); }
    else if (outer.length === 'cropped' && bv >= 1) { s += 6; pros.push(`A cropped ${short(outer)} over wide ${short(bottom)} lengthens the leg line.`); }
  }

  if (shoes) {
    if (shoes.profile === 'chunky' && bv >= 1) { s += 4; pros.push(`The substantial ${short(shoes)} anchor the wide trouser leg.`); }
    else if (shoes.profile === 'sleek' && bv < 0) { s += 4; pros.push(`Sleek ${short(shoes)} continue the slim line of the ${short(bottom)}.`); }
    else if (shoes.profile === 'chunky' && bottom.art === 'skirt') { pros.push(`Chunky ${short(shoes)} under a midi skirt is a modern, grounded contrast.`); }
  }
  return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Pillar 2: Fabric & Weather Practicality
export function warmthOf(o) {
  return (o.top?.warmth || 0) + (isDress(o.top) ? 0.5 * (o.top?.warmth || 0) : 0) + (o.outer?.warmth || 0) + 0.5 * (o.bottom?.warmth || 0) + 0.3 * (o.shoes?.warmth || 0) + (o.accessory?.warmth || 0);
}
export function warmthNeeded(tempC) {
  return Math.max(1.5, Math.min(12, 3 + (22 - tempC) / 2.5));
}

function fabricWeather(o, ctx) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  let s = 78;
  const t = ctx.tempC;
  const have = warmthOf(o), need = warmthNeeded(t);
  const diff = have - need;

  if (diff < -2.5) { s -= 28; cons.push(`Underdressed for ${t}°C. This look is about ${Math.round(have)} warmth points against roughly ${Math.round(need)} needed. Add a layer${o.outer ? ' or a heavier knit' : ', like a coat or a knit'}.`); }
  else if (diff < -1.2) { s -= 12; cons.push(`Slightly light for ${t}°C. You may feel the chill once the sun goes.`); }
  else if (diff > 3) { s -= 22; cons.push(`Overheated at ${t}°C: ${o.outer ? `the ${short(o.outer)} is too much layering` : 'this is too much fabric'} for the conditions.`); }
  else if (diff > 1.5) { s -= 6; cons.push(`May run warm indoors at ${t}°C. Choose a layer that's easy to take off.`); }
  else { s += 10; pros.push(`Just the right warmth for ${t}°C.`); }

  if (ctx.rain >= 0.6) {
    const sh = o.shoes;
    if (sh && sh.water <= 1) { s -= 18; cons.push(`Wearing ${(sh.material || 'these').toLowerCase()} ${NOUN[sh.art] || 'shoes'} in the rain is risky, because water marks and stains them. Choose leather boots or rubber soles.`); }
    else if (sh && sh.art === 'sandal') { s -= 18; cons.push('Open sandals in the rain mean wet feet. Choose closed shoes.'); }
    else if (sh && sh.water >= 4) { s += 8; pros.push(`The ${short(sh)}${sh.material ? ` (${sh.material.toLowerCase()})` : ''} will shrug off puddles.`); }
    if (o.outer && o.outer.water >= 4) { s += 10; pros.push(`The water-repellent ${short(o.outer)} handles the drizzle.`); }
    else if (!o.outer) { s -= 12; cons.push('There\'s no rain-ready layer. A trench or mac would keep the look intact.'); }
    else { s -= 6; cons.push(`The ${short(o.outer)} isn't water-resistant, so you'll want an umbrella.`); }
  }

  if (o.shoes?.art === 'sandal' && t < 18) { s -= 18; cons.push(`Sandals at ${t}°C will leave your feet cold. Closed shoes are the move.`); }

  const items = pieces(o);
  if (t >= 27 && o.shoes?.art === 'boot') { s -= 10; cons.push(`The ${short(o.shoes)} will be hot at ${t}°C.`); }
  if (t >= 27 && o.top && (o.top.warmth || 0) >= 3) { s -= 15; cons.push(`The ${short(o.top)} is too warm a top for ${t}°C. Choose something light and breathable.`); }
  if (t >= 27) {
    const heavy = items.find((i) => i.weight === 'heavy');
    if (heavy) { s -= 10; cons.push(`The heavy ${short(heavy)} will trap heat.`); }
    const linen = items.find((i) => /linen/i.test(i.material || ''));
    if (linen) { s += 6; pros.push(`Breathable linen in the ${short(linen)} is exactly right for the heat.`); }
  }

  const light = items.find((i) => /linen/i.test(i.material || '') && i.category !== 'accessory');
  const heavyClash = items.find((i) => i.weight === 'heavy' && i.category !== 'shoes');
  if (light && heavyClash) { s -= 8; cons.push(`Fabric weight clash: airy ${short(light)} next to a heavy ${short(heavyClash)} reads seasonally confused.`); }

  const offSeason = items.filter((i) => i.seasons && !i.seasons.includes(ctx.season) && i.category !== 'accessory').slice(0, 2);
  for (const i of offSeason) { s -= 6; cons.push(`The ${short(i)} is meant for ${i.seasons.join('/')}, so it looks out of place in ${ctx.season}.`); }

  // Comfort & practicality (weighted more when she asked for comfort, or it's sports/vacation)
  const wantsComfort = ctx.prefs?.includes('comfortable') || ['sports', 'vacation'].includes(ctx.occasion);
  const stiff = items.filter((i) => comfortOf(i) <= 1);
  const easy = items.filter((i) => comfortOf(i) >= 3 && i.category !== 'accessory');
  if (wantsComfort) {
    if (stiff.length) { s -= 8 * stiff.length; cons.push(`For comfort, the ${stiff.map(short).join(' and ')} ${isAre(stiff)} on the stiff side.`); }
    else if (easy.length >= 2) { s += 6; pros.push(`Comfortable all day: the ${easy.slice(0, 2).map(short).join(' and ')} move with you.`); }
  }
  if (ctx.occasion === 'sports' && o.shoes && o.shoes.art !== 'sneaker') { s -= 14; cons.push(`You'll want sneakers for sports, not ${short(o.shoes)}.`); }
  return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Pillar 3: Color Harmony
function colorHarmony(o, ctx = {}) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  const items = pieces(o);
  if (!items.length) return { score: 0, pros, cons: ['Add pieces to see color feedback.'] };

  const accents = [];
  for (const it of items) for (const c of it.colors || []) if (!isNeutral(c.name)) accents.push({ ...c, item: it });
  const uniqueAccents = [...new Map(accents.map((a) => [a.name, a])).values()];
  let s;

  if (uniqueAccents.length === 0) {
    const ls = items.map((i) => hexToHsl(i.colors[0].hex)[2]);
    if (ls.every((l) => l < 0.3)) { s = 86; pros.push('A tonal dark palette that reads sleek and intentional.'); }
    else if (ls.every((l) => l > 0.75)) { s = 86; pros.push('A fresh, light tonal palette that works well in daylight.'); }
    else { s = 84; pros.push('An all-neutral palette: calm, versatile, and almost impossible to clash.'); }
