    if (items.length >= 4 && !o.accessory) { s -= 3; cons.push('This is a safe palette. One colored accessory would give it a point of interest.'); }
  } else if (uniqueAccents.length === 1) {
    const a = uniqueAccents[0];
    s = 92; pros.push(`The ${short(a.item)} ${plural(a.item) ? 'are' : 'is'} the one pop of color against calm neutrals.`);
  } else if (uniqueAccents.length === 2) {
    const [a, b] = uniqueAccents;
    const hd = hueDistance(hexToHsl(a.hex)[0], hexToHsl(b.hex)[0]);
    if (hd < 40) { s = 86; pros.push(`${cap(a.name)} and ${b.name} are analogous hues, so they harmonize quietly.`); }
    else if (hd >= 150) { s = 80; pros.push(`${cap(a.name)} with ${b.name} is a complementary pairing: bold but controlled.`); }
    else {
      s = 62;
      const warmCool = temperature(a.hex) !== temperature(b.hex) ? ' (one warm, one cool)' : '';
      cons.push(`${cap(a.name)} and ${b.name} compete for attention${warmCool}. Keep one and swap the other for a neutral.`);
    }
  } else {
    s = 50; cons.push(`Too many focal colors (${uniqueAccents.map((a) => a.name).join(', ')}). Let one lead.`);
  }

  // Pattern compatibility: one pattern leads; two need a shared color and different scale
  const patterned = items.filter((i) => i.pattern && i.pattern !== 'solid');
  if (patterned.length >= 2) {
    const shareColor = patterned[0].colors.some((c) => patterned[1].colors.some((d) => d.name === c.name));
    if (patterned[0].pattern === patterned[1].pattern) { s -= 14; cons.push(`Two ${patterned[0].pattern} patterns (${short(patterned[0])} and ${short(patterned[1])}) fight each other. Keep one and go plain with the other.`); }
    else if (shareColor) { s -= 2; pros.push(`Brave pattern mix, held together by a shared color.`); }
    else { s -= 10; cons.push(`The ${patterned[0].pattern} ${short(patterned[0])} and ${patterned[1].pattern} ${short(patterned[1])} don't share a color, so they clash.`); }
  } else if (patterned.length === 1) {
    pros.push(`The ${patterned[0].pattern} ${short(patterned[0])} leads; the plain pieces let it breathe.`);
  }

  // Her preference: simple vs colorful / stand out
  const prefs = ctx.prefs || [];
  if (prefs.includes('simple')) {
    if (uniqueAccents.length + patterned.length >= 2) { s -= 10; cons.push('You asked for simple: there\'s a lot going on in color and pattern here.'); }
    else { s += 4; pros.push('Clean and simple, as you asked.'); }
  }
  if (prefs.includes('colorful') || prefs.includes('standout')) {
    if (uniqueAccents.length === 0 && patterned.length === 0) { s -= 12; cons.push(prefs.includes('colorful') ? 'You wanted more color, but this look is all neutrals.' : 'You wanted to stand out, but this look plays it very safe.'); }
    else { s += 6; pros.push(prefs.includes('colorful') ? 'Brings in the color you asked for.' : 'Has the eye-catching point you asked for.'); }
  }

  // Flat contrast: two different, non-neutral colors at the same depth meeting at the waist look muddy.
  const tp = isDress(o.top) ? null : o.top, bt = isDress(o.top) ? null : o.bottom;
  if (tp && bt && tp.colors?.[0] && bt.colors?.[0] && !isNeutral(tp.colors[0].name) && !isNeutral(bt.colors[0].name) && tp.colors[0].name !== bt.colors[0].name) {
    const [h1, , l1] = hexToHsl(tp.colors[0].hex), [h2, , l2] = hexToHsl(bt.colors[0].hex);
    const dh = Math.min(Math.abs(h1 - h2), 360 - Math.abs(h1 - h2));
    if (Math.abs(l1 - l2) < 0.08 && dh > 40) { s -= 6; cons.push(`The ${short(tp)} and ${short(bt)} are the same depth of color, so they compete. A lighter or darker partner for one of them would look sharper.`); }
  }

  // Classic near-miss: black right next to navy as separate pieces.
  const names = items.map((i) => i.colors[0].name);
  if (names.includes('black') && names.includes('navy')) {
    s -= 6; cons.push('Black right next to navy can read as a near-miss. Make the contrast obviously intentional, or pick one.');
  }
  return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Pillar 4: Occasion Fit
function occasionFit(o, ctx) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  const core = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  if (!core.length) return { score: 0, pros, cons: ['Add pieces to judge the occasion fit.'] };
  const f = core.map((i) => i.formality);
  const avg = f.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / f.length;
  const diff = avg - ctx.formality;
  let s;
  const low = core.reduce((a, b) => (b.formality < a.formality ? b : a));
  const high = core.reduce((a, b) => (b.formality > a.formality ? b : a));

  if (Math.abs(diff) <= 0.6) { s = 90; pros.push(`The formality sits right for ${occ(ctx)}.`); }
  else if (diff < 0) { s = clamp(90 - 26 * Math.abs(diff)); cons.push(`Too casual for ${occ(ctx)}. The ${short(low)} ${plural(low) ? 'pull' : 'pulls'} the look down.`); }
  else if (diff <= 1.25) { s = 82; pros.push(`A touch polished for ${occ(ctx)}, which reads intentional rather than overdone.`); }
  else { s = clamp(92 - 20 * diff); cons.push(`Overdressed for ${occ(ctx)}. Relax it by swapping the ${short(high)}.`); }

  if (high.formality - low.formality >= 3) {
    if (low.category === 'shoes' && low.art === 'sneaker' && low.profile !== 'chunky' && !['sporty', 'streetwear'].includes(low.style)) { s += 4; pros.push(`High-low mix: ${short(low)} with the ${short(high)} is a deliberate, modern touch.`); }
    else { s -= 10; cons.push(`Formality gap: the ${short(low)} and ${short(high)} come from different worlds.`); }
  }
  if (o.shoes && ctx.formality >= 3.5 && (o.shoes.formality || 2) <= ctx.formality - 1.5 && o.shoes.art !== 'sneaker') { s -= 14; cons.push(`The ${short(o.shoes)} ${plural(o.shoes) ? 'are' : 'is'} too casual for ${occ(ctx)}.`); }
  if (!o.shoes) { s -= 10; cons.push('Add shoes to complete the look.'); }
  if (o.top && !isDress(o.top) && !o.bottom) { s -= 15; cons.push('Add a bottom (or choose a dress) to complete the look.'); }
  return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons, avg };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Comfort helper (0 stiff … 4 very comfortable)
export function comfortOf(i) {
  if (!i) return 2;
  const m = (i.material || '').toLowerCase();
  let c = 2;
  if (/jersey|knit|fleece|stretch|linen|cotton|modal|down/.test(m)) c += 1;
  if (/satin|patent|gabardine|cashmere/.test(m)) c -= 1;
  if (i.fit === 'relaxed' || i.fit === 'oversized') c += 1;
  if (i.fit === 'fitted' && /denim/.test(m)) c -= 1;
  if (i.art === 'sneaker' || i.art === 'sandal') c += 1;
  if (i.art === 'loafer' && i.formality >= 4) c -= 1;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(4, c));
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Pillar 5: Style, Shoes, Accessories & Balance
// Style families: pieces within a family (or neighbouring families) look like they belong together.
const STYLE_FAMILY = {
  casual: 'easy', minimalist: 'clean', classic: 'clean', preppy: 'clean', business: 'polished', elegant: 'polished',
  feminine: 'soft', sporty: 'sport', streetwear: 'street', edgy: 'street',
};
const CLASHES = [['sport', 'polished'], ['street', 'polished'], ['sport', 'soft'], ['street', 'soft']];
const styleName = (x) => (x === 'business' ? 'business-casual' : x);
function finish(o, ctx) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  let s = 80;
  const core = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  const styled = core.filter((i) => i.style);
  const fams = [...new Set(styled.map((i) => STYLE_FAMILY[i.style]).filter(Boolean))];
  const clash = CLASHES.find(([a, b]) => fams.includes(a) && fams.includes(b));
  if (clash) {
    const A = styled.find((i) => STYLE_FAMILY[i.style] === clash[0]), B = styled.find((i) => STYLE_FAMILY[i.style] === clash[1]);
    s -= 22; cons.push(`Mixed messages: the ${styleName(A.style)} ${short(A)} and the ${styleName(B.style)} ${short(B)} pull in different directions.`);
  } else if (styled.length >= 3 && fams.length <= 2) {
    s += 8; { const mood = [...new Set(styled.map((i) => styleName(i.style)))].slice(0, 2).join(' / '); pros.push(`Every piece speaks the same ${mood.replace(' / ', '-and-')} language.`); }
  }
  if (ctx.style && styled.length) {
    const match = styled.filter((i) => i.style === ctx.style || STYLE_FAMILY[i.style] === STYLE_FAMILY[ctx.style]).length / styled.length;
    if (match >= 0.6) { s += 6; pros.push(`Fits the ${styleName(ctx.style)} style you picked.`); }
    else if (match < 0.3) { s -= 10; cons.push(`Not very ${styleName(ctx.style)}: most pieces lean another way.`); }
  }

  // Footwear: do the shoes suit the rest?
  const sh = o.shoes;
  if (sh) {
    const rest = core.filter((i) => i !== sh);
    const avg = rest.length ? rest.reduce((a, i) => a + i.formality, 0) / rest.length : sh.formality;
    const be = plural(sh) ? 'are' : 'is';
    if (sh.formality - avg >= 2) { s -= 10; cons.push(`The ${short(sh)} ${be} dressier than the rest of the outfit.`); }
    else if (avg - sh.formality >= 2 && sh.art !== 'sneaker') { s -= 10; cons.push(`The ${short(sh)} ${be} too casual for the rest of the outfit.`); }
    else if (avg - sh.formality >= 2 && sh.art === 'sneaker' && (ctx.formality >= 4 || ctx.style === 'elegant')) { s -= 10; cons.push(`The ${short(sh)} are too casual for an ${ctx.style === 'elegant' ? 'elegant' : 'dressy'} look.`); }
    else if (avg - sh.formality >= 2 && sh.art === 'sneaker' && (sh.profile === 'chunky' || ['sporty', 'streetwear'].includes(sh.style))) { s -= 8; cons.push(`The ${short(sh)} ${be} too sporty for the rest of the outfit.`); }
    else if (avg - sh.formality >= 2 && sh.art === 'sneaker') { pros.push(`The ${short(sh)} relax a smart outfit, a modern touch.`); }
    else { s += 4; const tone = avg >= 3.3 ? 'keep it polished' : avg <= 1.8 ? 'keep it easy-going' : 'pull it together'; pros.push(`The ${short(sh)} ${tone}.`); }
    if (sh.art === 'sandal' && o.outer?.weight === 'heavy') { s -= 10; cons.push(`Sandals with a heavy ${short(o.outer)} mix two seasons.`); }
  }

  // Accessories: add, don't clutter
  const extras = [o.bag, o.accessory].filter(Boolean);
  const loud = extras.filter((i) => !isNeutral(i.colors[0].name));
  const before = cons.length;
  if (loud.length >= 2) { s -= 8; cons.push(`Two bold accessories (${loud.map(short).join(', ')}) compete. Keep one.`); }
  if (o.bag && (/straw|woven|raffia|wicker/i.test(`${o.bag.name} ${o.bag.material || ''}`) && ctx.tempC < 15 || /straw|woven|raffia|wicker|canvas/i.test(`${o.bag.name} ${o.bag.material || ''}`) && ctx.rain >= 0.6)) { s -= 8; cons.push(ctx.rain >= 0.6 ? `A ${short(o.bag)} will soak up the rain.` : `A ${short(o.bag)} is a summer piece; it looks out of season at ${ctx.tempC}°C.`); }
  if (o.accessory?.art === 'scarf' && ctx.tempC >= 25) { s -= 10; cons.push(`A ${short(o.accessory)} is too warm for ${ctx.tempC}°C.`); }
  if (o.accessory?.art === 'necklace' && core.some((i) => i.style === 'sporty')) { s -= 6; cons.push(`The ${short(o.accessory)} feels out of place with sporty pieces.`); }
  const coreAvg = core.length ? core.reduce((a, i) => a + (i.formality || 2), 0) / core.length : 2;
  if (o.bag && o.bag.formality <= 1 && (ctx.formality >= 3 || coreAvg >= 3.5)) { s -= 6; cons.push(`A ${short(o.bag)} is too casual for ${(ctx.occasionLabel || 'this occasion').toLowerCase()}.`); }
  if (cons.length === before) {
    if (extras.length && !loud.length) { s += 3; pros.push(`The ${extras.map(short).join(' and ')} ${extras.length > 1 ? 'finish' : 'finishes'} the look without crowding it.`); }
    else if (loud.length === 1) { s += 4; pros.push(`The ${short(loud[0])} adds a point of interest.`); }
  }

  // Fabric & texture: only judged when the fabric is known (never guessed from nothing).
  const tex = (i) => {
    const m = `${i.material || ''}`.toLowerCase();
    if (!m || (i.unverified || []).includes('material')) return null;
    if (/silk|satin|velvet|cashmere|sequin|lace|chiffon|organza/.test(m)) return 'luxe';
    if (/fleece|sweat|jersey|tech|mesh|spandex|polyester knit|nylon/.test(m)) return 'sport';
    if (/denim|canvas|leather|suede|corduroy|tweed|waxed/.test(m)) return 'rugged';
    if (/knit|wool|merino|cable|mohair|boucl/.test(m)) return 'knit';
    if (/linen|cotton|poplin|oxford|twill|chino|gabardine|crepe|suiting/.test(m)) return 'crisp';
    return null;
  };
  const worn = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  const textures = worn.map((i) => [i, tex(i)]).filter(([, t]) => t);
  const luxe = textures.find(([, t]) => t === 'luxe'), sport = textures.find(([, t]) => t === 'sport');
  if (luxe && sport) { s -= 12; cons.push(`The ${(luxe[0].material || '').toLowerCase()} ${NOUN[luxe[0].art] || 'piece'} and the ${(sport[0].material || '').toLowerCase()} ${NOUN[sport[0].art] || 'piece'} are fabrics from different worlds; one of them makes the other look accidental.`); }
  else if (luxe && textures.some(([, t]) => t === 'rugged' || t === 'knit')) { s += 4; pros.push(`Smooth ${(luxe[0].material || '').toLowerCase()} against a textured piece gives the outfit depth.`); }
  const denims = !isDress(o.top) ? [o.top, o.bottom].filter((i) => i && /denim/i.test(`${i.material || ''} ${i.art}`) && !(i.unverified || []).includes('material')) : [];
  if (denims.length === 2) {
    if (denims[0].colors?.[0]?.name === denims[1].colors?.[0]?.name) { s -= 8; cons.push('Denim on denim in the same wash looks like a uniform. Pick two clearly different washes.'); }
    else { s += 3; pros.push('Double denim in two different washes, done right.'); }
  }

  // Overall visual balance: where the eye lands (light vs dark, heavy vs light pieces)
  const upper = o.outer || o.top, lower = isDress(o.top) ? o.top : o.bottom;
  if (upper && lower && o.shoes) {
    const L = (i) => hexToHsl(i.colors[0].hex)[2];
    const heavyTop = (o.outer?.weight === 'heavy' || o.top?.fit === 'oversized') && lower.art === 'shorts';
    if (heavyTop) { s -= 8; cons.push(`Top-heavy: the bulky ${short(upper)} overwhelms the ${short(lower)}.`); }
    if (L(upper) < 0.3 && L(lower) > 0.75 && L(o.shoes) > 0.75) { s -= 4; cons.push('Dark on top and pale below. Darker shoes would ground the look.'); }
    else if (Math.abs(L(upper) - L(lower)) > 0.25 && Math.abs(L(o.shoes) - L(lower)) < 0.35) { s += 3; pros.push('Good light-and-dark balance from top to toe.'); }
  }
  return { score: clamp(s), pros, cons };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Honest verdict
/**
 * A plain, honest verdict for any outfit, never flattering: what a frank friend with good taste would say.
 * @returns {{ label, tone: 'great'|'good'|'okay'|'no', line }}
 */
export function verdictOf(c) {
  const worst = c.pillars.slice().sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score)[0];
  const firstCon = worst?.cons?.[0];
  if (c.dealBreakers?.length) return { label: "Doesn't work", tone: 'no', line: c.dealBreakers[0] };
  if (c.overall >= 88) return { label: 'Great look', tone: 'great', line: firstCon ? `Small note: ${firstCon.charAt(0).toLowerCase()}${firstCon.slice(1)}` : 'Nothing to fix.' };
  if (c.overall >= 78) return { label: 'Good outfit', tone: 'good', line: firstCon || 'Solid and easy to wear.' };
  if (c.overall >= 68) return { label: 'Okay, not exciting', tone: 'okay', line: firstCon ? `Wearable, but ${firstCon.charAt(0).toLowerCase()}${firstCon.slice(1)}` : 'Wearable, but it won\'t turn heads.' };
  return { label: 'Not recommended', tone: 'no', line: firstCon || 'Several things are working against each other here.' };
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Deal-breakers
// Things a stylist would never let you leave the house in. Each one names the pillar it belongs to.
const ATHLETIC = (i) => i && i.art === 'sneaker' && (i.style === 'sporty' || /running|trainer|athletic/i.test(i.name || ''));
export function dealBreakers(o, ctx) {
  const out = [];
  const add = (pillar, text) => out.push({ pillar, text });
  const core = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  if (!o.top || !o.shoes) return out;
  const sh = o.shoes;
  // weather
  if (ctx.rain >= 0.6 && (sh.art === 'sandal' || (sh.water || 1) <= 1)) add('fabric', `The ${short(sh)} won't survive the rain. Wear closed, water-resistant shoes.`);
  const diff = warmthOf(o) - warmthNeeded(ctx.tempC);
  if (diff < -2.5) add('fabric', `Far too light for ${ctx.tempC}°C. You'd be cold within minutes.`);
  if (diff > 3.5) add('fabric', `Far too warm for ${ctx.tempC}°C.`);
  if (sh.art === 'sandal' && ctx.tempC < 18 && !(sh.formality >= 4 && ctx.formality >= 4)) add('fabric', `Open ${short(sh)} at ${ctx.tempC}°C means cold feet.`);
  // shoes that cancel the outfit
  const dressy = core.filter((i) => i !== sh && (i.formality || 2) >= 3.5);
  if (ATHLETIC(sh) && (dressy.length || ctx.formality >= 3.5)) add('finish', `Athletic ${short(sh)} cancel out ${dressy.length ? `the ${short(dressy[0])}` : `a ${ctx.occasionLabel.toLowerCase()} look`}. Pick flats, loafers or boots.`);
  if (sh.formality >= 4.5 && ctx.formality <= 1.5) add('finish', `${short(sh).charAt(0).toUpperCase() + short(sh).slice(1)} are impractical for ${occ(ctx)}.`);
  // style families that never work together
  const fam = (i) => STYLE_FAMILY[i.style];
  const sporty = core.find((i) => (fam(i) === 'sport' && i !== sh) || (fam(i) === 'street' && ctx.formality >= 3.5 && (i.formality || 2) <= 2));
  const polished = core.find((i) => fam(i) === 'polished' && (i.formality || 2) >= 4);
  if (sporty && polished) add('finish', `The ${short(sporty)} and the ${short(polished)} belong to different worlds.`);
  // occasion
  const avg = core.reduce((a, i) => a + (i.formality || 2), 0) / core.length;
  const tooCasual = ['top', 'bottom'].map((k) => o[k]).find((i) => i && (i.formality || 2) <= ctx.formality - 2.4);
  if (avg - ctx.formality <= -1.5) add('occasion', `Much too casual for ${occ(ctx)}.`);
  else if (tooCasual) add('occasion', `The ${short(tooCasual)} ${plural(tooCasual) ? 'are' : 'is'} too casual for ${occ(ctx)}, even under a smarter layer.`);
  const tooDressy = ['top', 'bottom'].map((k) => o[k]).find((i) => i && ((i.formality || 2) >= ctx.formality + 2.5 || ((i.formality || 2) >= 5 && ctx.formality < 4)));
  if (avg - ctx.formality >= 2.2) add('occasion', `Much too dressy for ${occ(ctx)}.`);
  else if (tooDressy) add('occasion', `The ${short(tooDressy)} ${plural(tooDressy) ? 'are' : 'is'} far too dressy for ${occ(ctx)}.`);
  if (ctx.occasion === 'sports') {
    const lower = isDress(o.top) ? o.top : o.bottom;
    const canMove = lower && (lower.style === 'sporty' || lower.art === 'shorts' || /jogger|legging|track|sweat/i.test(lower.name || ''));
    if (!canMove) add('occasion', `The ${short(lower || o.top)} ${lower && plural(lower) ? 'are' : 'is'} hard to move in for sports.`);
    if (sh.art !== 'sneaker') add('fabric', `You'll want sneakers for sports, not ${short(sh)}.`);
  }
  return out;
}

// ───────────────────────────────── Public API
export const PILLARS = [
  { id: 'proportions', label: 'Proportions & Silhouette', weight: 0.17 },
  { id: 'fabric', label: 'Weather & Comfort', weight: 0.25 },
  { id: 'color', label: 'Color & Pattern', weight: 0.2 },
  { id: 'occasion', label: 'Occasion & Formality', weight: 0.2 },
  { id: 'finish', label: 'Style, Shoes & Accessories', weight: 0.18 },
];

export function critique(outfit, ctx) {
  const results = {
    proportions: proportions(outfit),
    fabric: fabricWeather(outfit, ctx),
    color: colorHarmony(outfit, ctx),
    occasion: occasionFit(outfit, ctx),
    finish: finish(outfit, ctx),
  };
  // Deal-breakers: serious flaws cap the score instead of being averaged away by good pillars.
  const breakers = dealBreakers(outfit, ctx);
  for (const b of breakers) {
    const r = results[b.pillar];
    r.score = clamp(r.score - 12);
    if (!r.cons.includes(b.text)) r.cons.unshift(b.text);
  }
  let overall = clamp(PILLARS.reduce((sum, p) => sum + results[p.id].score * p.weight, 0));
  if (breakers.length) overall = Math.min(overall, breakers.length > 1 ? 60 : 68);
  const items = pieces(outfit);
  const seasons = ['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter'].filter((s) => items.filter((i) => i.category !== 'accessory').every((i) => !i.seasons || i.seasons.includes(s)));
  const avgF = results.occasion.avg || 0;

  return {
    overall,
    dealBreakers: breakers.map((b) => b.text),
    pillars: PILLARS.map((p) => ({ ...p, ...results[p.id] })),
    seasons,
    weatherMeter: Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(results.fabric.score / 20))),
    formalityMeter: Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(avgF))),
    formalityLabel: FORMALITY_LABEL[Math.max(1, Math.min(5, Math.round(avgF)))] || '—',
    warmth: { have: Math.round(warmthOf(outfit) * 10) / 10, need: Math.round(warmthNeeded(ctx.tempC) * 10) / 10 },
  };
}

/** The single swap (or added/removed layer) from HER closet that improves the score most. */
export function suggestSwap(outfit, ctx, pool) {
  const base = critique(outfit, ctx).overall;
  let best = null;
  for (const slot of ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes']) {
    if (slot === 'bottom' && isDress(outfit.top)) continue;
    const candidates = pool.filter((g) => slotOf(g) === slot && g.id !== outfit[slot]?.id && (slot !== 'top' || isDress(g) === isDress(outfit.top)));
    for (const c of candidates) {
      const score = critique({ ...outfit, [slot]: c }, ctx).overall;
      if (!best || score > best.score) best = { slot, to: c, from: outfit[slot] || null, score };
    }
    if (slot === 'outer' && outfit.outer) {
      const score = critique({ ...outfit, outer: null }, ctx).overall;
      if (!best || score > best.score) best = { slot, to: null, from: outfit.outer, score };
    }
  }
  if (!best || best.score - base < 3) return null;
  const gain = best.score - base;
  let text;
  if (!best.to) text = `Drop the ${short(best.from)}. You don't need the layer today.`;
  else if (!best.from) text = `Add your ${short(best.to)}.`;
  else text = `Swap the ${short(best.from)} for your ${short(best.to)}.`;
  return { ...best, gain, text };
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/generator.js
====================================================================================================

// Outfit generation: deterministic search scored by the critic (no AI involved here).
// Used directly when the AI stylist isn't available, and to VALIDATE the AI's picks when it is.
//
//   generateOutfits(pool, ctx, anchor)      → { outfits: [3 distinct], missing: [] }
//   styleWithThis(pool, anchor, ctx)        → 3+ styling directions around one garment
//   alternatives(outfit, ctx, pool)         → "different shoes / add a jacket / more formal …" (owned items only)
//   explain(outfit, critique, ctx)          → plain-language "why this works"
//   familyLook(members, ctx)                → one coordinated outfit per family member

import { critique, isDress, isComplete, short, comfortOf, occ } from './critic.js';
import { isNeutral, hexToHsl, hueDistance } from './color.js';
import { slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';

export const isAvailable = (g) => !g.status || g.status === 'available';
const CORE = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'];
const ALL_SLOTS = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'];
const key = (o) => ALL_SLOTS.map((k) => o[k]?.id || '-').join('|');

// ── what's missing for a complete outfit?
export function missingForOutfit(pool) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const has = (fn) => avail.some(fn);
  const missing = [];
  const tops = has((g) => slotOf(g) === 'top' && !isDress(g));
  const dresses = has(isDress);
  const bottoms = has((g) => slotOf(g) === 'bottom');
  if (!dresses && !(tops && bottoms)) {
    if (!tops) missing.push('a top');
    if (!bottoms) missing.push('a bottom');
    if (!tops && !bottoms) missing.splice(0, 2, 'a top and a bottom (or a dress)');
  }
  if (!has((g) => slotOf(g) === 'shoes')) missing.push('shoes');
  return missing;
}

// ── single-piece suitability, used to keep the search small and sensible
function pieceFit(g, ctx) {
  let s = 0;
  s -= Math.abs((g.formality ?? 2) - ctx.formality) * 1.4;
  if (g.seasons && !g.seasons.includes(ctx.season)) s -= 2;
  if (g.occasions?.includes(ctx.occasion)) s += 1.5;
  if (ctx.style && g.style === ctx.style) s += 1.5;
  if (ctx.rain >= 0.6 && slotOf(g) === 'shoes') s += (g.water || 1) >= 3 ? 1.5 : -2;
  if (ctx.tempC >= 27 && g.weight === 'heavy') s -= 2;
  if (ctx.tempC <= 10 && g.weight === 'light' && g.category !== 'accessory') s -= 1;
  if (ctx.prefs?.includes('comfortable')) s += (comfortOf(g) - 2) * 0.8;
  return s;
}
const topN = (items, ctx, n, keep) => {
  const ranked = [...items].sort((a, b) => pieceFit(b, ctx) - pieceFit(a, ctx)).slice(0, n);
  if (keep && items.includes(keep) && !ranked.includes(keep)) ranked.push(keep);
  return ranked;
};

/** Best bag + accessory (or none) for an outfit, from her closet. */
function accessorize(o, ctx, pool, anchor) {
  let best = { ...o };
  for (const slot of ['bag', 'accessory']) {
    if (anchor && slotOf(anchor) === slot) { best[slot] = anchor; continue; }
    const options = [null, ...pool.filter((g) => slotOf(g) === slot)];
    let bestScore = -1, pick = null;
    for (const c of options) {
      const sc = critique({ ...best, [slot]: c }, ctx).overall + (c ? 0.5 : 0); // a tie goes to the finished look
      if (sc > bestScore) { bestScore = sc; pick = c; }
    }
    best = { ...best, [slot]: pick };
  }
  return best;
}

/**
 * Ranking score: the critic's overall score, plus
 *  - a hard push-down for practical problems (rain-unsafe shoes, too hot/cold), which must never win;
 *  - extra weight for the style she picked.
 */
function rank(o, ctx) {
  const c = critique(o, ctx);
  let s = c.overall;
  const weather = c.pillars.find((p) => p.id === 'fabric').score;
  if (weather < 65) s -= (65 - weather) * 1.5;
  // Deal-breakers: never let these win, whatever else scores well
  const sh = o.shoes;
  if (ctx.rain >= 0.6 && sh && ((sh.water || 1) <= 1 || sh.art === 'sandal')) s -= 30;
  if (ctx.tempC < 15 && sh?.art === 'sandal') s -= 20;
  if (ctx.tempC >= 27 && CORE.some((k) => o[k]?.weight === 'heavy' && k !== 'shoes')) s -= 20;
  if (ctx.style) {
    const core = CORE.map((k) => o[k]).filter((i) => i?.style);
    const match = core.length ? core.filter((i) => i.style === ctx.style).length / core.length : 0;
    s += match * 10;
  }
  if (ctx.personal) s += personalBonus(o, ctx.personal);
  return s;
}

/**
 * Learned taste (from her ratings in Outfit History) nudges the ranking, within ±8 points,
 * so it can choose between good outfits but never rescue a bad one (deal-breakers still cap the score).
 */
export function personalBonus(o, p) {
  if (!p) return 0;
  let b = 0;
  for (const k of [...CORE, 'bag', 'accessory']) {
    const i = o[k];
    if (!i) continue;
    b += p.items?.[i.id] || 0;
    if (k !== 'bag' && k !== 'accessory') {
      b += (p.colors?.[i.colors?.[0]?.name] || 0) * 0.5;
      b += (p.styles?.[i.style] || 0) * 0.5;
    }
    if (p.recent?.includes(i.id) && k !== 'shoes' && k !== 'bag') b -= 1.5; // worn in the last few days: a little variety
  }
  return Math.max(-8, Math.min(8, b));
}

/** All reasonable complete outfits, best first. */
export function candidates(pool, ctx, anchor, limit = 60) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const aSlot = anchor ? slotOf(anchor) : null;
  const of = (slot, filter = () => true) => avail.filter((g) => slotOf(g) === slot && filter(g));
  const tops = aSlot === 'top' ? [anchor] : topN(of('top'), ctx, 9);
  const bottoms = aSlot === 'bottom' ? [anchor] : topN(of('bottom'), ctx, 8);
  const shoes = aSlot === 'shoes' ? [anchor] : topN(of('shoes'), ctx, 6);
  const outers = aSlot === 'outer' ? [anchor] : [null, ...topN(of('outer'), ctx, 5)];
  const scored = [];
  for (const top of tops) {
    const bots = isDress(top) ? [null] : (aSlot === 'bottom' ? bottoms : bottoms);
    if (isDress(top) && aSlot === 'bottom') continue; // anchor bottom can't go with a dress
    for (const bottom of bots) {
      if (!isDress(top) && !bottom) continue;
      for (const sh of shoes) for (const outer of outers) {
        const o = { top, bottom, outer, shoes: sh, bag: aSlot === 'bag' ? anchor : null, accessory: aSlot === 'accessory' ? anchor : null };
        scored.push({ o, score: rank(o, ctx) });
      }
    }
  }
  scored.sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
  return scored.slice(0, limit);
}

/** Describe an outfit's character in a few words, e.g. "Polished · Classic". */
const TC = (t) => t.replace(/\b([a-z])/g, (m) => m.toUpperCase());

/** A memorable, descriptive name from the two pieces that define the look, e.g. "Olive Trousers & Black Turtleneck". */
export function describe(o) {
  const hero = (i) => i && (i.pattern && i.pattern !== 'solid' ? 3 : 0) + (isNeutralItem(i) ? 0 : 2) + (i.category === 'dress' ? 2 : 0);
  const pieces = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer'].map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  if (!pieces.length) return 'New look';
  const [a, ...rest] = pieces;
  const b = rest.sort((x, y) => hero(y) - hero(x))[0];
  const second = b || o.shoes;
  return second ? `${TC(short(a))} & ${TC(short(second))}` : TC(short(a));
}
const NEUTRALS = /^(black|white|off-white|ivory|cream|gray|grey|charcoal|beige|camel|navy|khaki|brown|taupe|stone)/;
const isNeutralItem = (i) => NEUTRALS.test(i.colors?.[0]?.name || '');

/** The outfit's mood, e.g. "Polished · Classic" (shown under the name). */
export function vibeOf(o) {
  const core = CORE.map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean);
  const f = core.reduce((a, i) => a + (i.formality || 2), 0) / (core.length || 1);
  const level = f >= 3.4 ? 'Polished' : f >= 2.3 ? 'Smart' : 'Relaxed';
  const counts = {};
  core.forEach((i) => { if (i.style) counts[i.style] = (counts[i.style] || 0) + 1; });
  const top = Object.entries(counts).sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])[0]?.[0];
  const LABEL = { business: 'Business casual', streetwear: 'Street', casual: 'Easy' };
  return top ? `${level} · ${LABEL[top] || top.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + top.slice(1)}` : level;
}

/** Plain-language explanation (no jargon) built from the strongest findings. */
export function explain(o, c, ctx) {
  // Written like a stylist: color story → shape → why it suits the day → one thing to watch.
  const P = Object.fromEntries(c.pillars.map((p) => [p.id, p]));
  const cons = c.pillars.filter((p) => p.cons.length).sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score).flatMap((p) => p.cons);
  const lower = (t) => t.charAt(0).toLowerCase() + t.slice(1).replace(/\.$/, '');
  const parts = [];
  const color = P.color?.pros[0];
  const shape = P.proportions?.pros.find((t) => /column|anchor|balanced|line/.test(t)) || P.proportions?.pros[0];
  if (color) parts.push(color.replace(/\.$/, '') + '.');
  if (shape && parts.length < 2) {
    const m = shape.match(/^The (\w+) (.+?) (are|is) balanced by (.+)$/);
    const again = m && color && color.includes(m[2]);
    parts.push(again ? `${m[3] === 'are' ? 'Their' : 'Its'} ${m[1]} cut is balanced by ${m[4].replace(/\.$/, '')}.` : shape.replace(/\.$/, '') + '.');
  }
  const practical = [...(P.fabric?.pros || [])].filter((t) => /rain|puddle|drizzle|breath|cool|warm enough|layer/i.test(t))[0];
  const fit = (P.occasion?.score ?? 0) >= 80 ? `Right for ${occ(ctx)}` : `Works for ${occ(ctx)}`;
  parts.push(`${fit} at ${ctx.tempC}°C${practical ? `, and ${lower(practical).replace(/\s*\([^)]*\)/g, '')}` : ''}.`);
  const said = parts.join(' ');
  const finish = (P.finish?.pros || []).find((t) => /finishes|point of interest|keep it|pull it together/.test(t) && !said.includes((t.match(/^The (.+?) (adds|finishes|keep|pull)/) || [])[1] || '§'));
  if (finish && parts.length < 4) parts.push(finish.replace(/\.$/, '') + '.');
  if (cons.length) parts.push(`Watch: ${lower(cons[0])}.`);
  return parts.join(' ');
}

function personalNote(o, ctx) {
  const liked = ctx.personal ? [...CORE, 'bag', 'accessory'].map((k) => o[k]).filter((i) => i && (ctx.personal.items?.[i.id] || 0) >= 2) : [];
  return liked.length ? ` Includes ${liked.length > 1 ? 'pieces' : 'a piece'} you rated highly before (the ${liked.slice(0, 2).map(short).join(' and ')}).` : '';
}

const shared = (a, b) => CORE.filter((k) => a[k] && b[k] && a[k].id === b[k].id).length;

/**
 * Three distinct, genuinely good outfits from HER available clothes.
 * @returns {{ outfits: Array<{ name, outfit, critique, why }>, missing: string[] }}
 */
/** The quality bar for a suggestion to be shown as a recommendation (the "Good outfit" verdict starts at 78). */
export const GOOD = 76;

export function generateOutfits(pool, ctx, anchor = null) {
  const missing = missingForOutfit(pool);
  if (missing.length) return { outfits: [], missing };
  if (anchor && !isAvailable(anchor)) return { outfits: [], missing: [], unavailable: anchor };
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const list = candidates(avail, ctx, anchor);
  const chosen = [];
  for (const { o } of list) {
    if (chosen.length >= 3) break;
    // distinct: share at most one core piece with any outfit already chosen (the anchor doesn't count)
    if (chosen.some((c) => shared(c.o, o) - (anchor && slotOf(anchor) !== 'bag' && slotOf(anchor) !== 'accessory' ? 1 : 0) > 1)) continue;
    chosen.push({ o });
  }
  // a small closet may not allow 3 fully distinct looks: relax the rule rather than return fewer
  for (const { o } of list) {
    if (chosen.length >= 3) break;
    if (!chosen.some((c) => key(c.o) === key(o))) chosen.push({ o });
  }
  const outfits = chosen.map(({ o }) => {
    const full = accessorize(o, ctx, avail, anchor);
    const c = critique(full, ctx);
    return { name: describe(full), vibe: vibeOf(full), outfit: full, critique: c, why: explain(full, c, ctx) + personalNote(full, ctx), weak: c.dealBreakers.length > 0 };
  });
  // Never pad with mediocre outfits: show only good ones (no deal-breaker, score ≥ GOOD). If none reach the bar,
  // show the single closest option, clearly labelled.
  const strong = outfits.filter((x) => !x.weak && x.critique.overall >= GOOD);
  const shown = (strong.length ? strong : outfits.slice(0, 1)).sort((a, b) => b.critique.overall - a.critique.overall);
  const note = shown.length < 3
    ? (strong.length
      ? `Only ${strong.length} good outfit${strong.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} can be made from this closet for ${occ(ctx)}, so that's all you're shown. Adding pieces would give more choice.`
      : `This closet can't make a really good outfit for ${occ(ctx)} yet. Here's the closest option, with what to watch.`)
    : '';
  return { outfits: shown, missing: [], note };
}

// ── Style me with this: several styling DIRECTIONS around one garment
export const DIRECTIONS = [
  { id: 'casual', name: 'Casual', ctx: { formality: 2, style: 'casual', prefs: ['comfortable'] } },
  { id: 'elegant', name: 'Elegant', ctx: { formality: 4, style: 'elegant', prefs: [] } },
  { id: 'minimal', name: 'Minimalist', ctx: { style: 'minimalist', prefs: ['simple'] } },
  { id: 'trendy', name: 'Trendy', ctx: { style: 'streetwear', prefs: ['standout'] } },
];
export function styleWithThis(pool, anchor, ctx) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  if (!anchor || !avail.includes(anchor)) return { outfits: [], missing: [], unavailable: anchor };
  const missing = missingForOutfit(avail);
  if (missing.length) return { outfits: [], missing };
  const dirs = [{ id: 'occasion', name: `For ${(ctx.occasionLabel || 'today').toLowerCase()}`, ctx: {} }, ...DIRECTIONS];
  const out = [];
  for (const d of dirs) {
    const dctx = { ...ctx, ...d.ctx, prefs: [...(ctx.prefs || []).filter((p) => !['simple', 'standout'].includes(p)), ...(d.ctx.prefs || [])] };
    const list = candidates(avail, dctx, anchor, 25);
    const pick = list.find(({ o }) => !out.some((x) => key(x.raw) === key(o) || shared(x.raw, o) >= 3));
    if (!pick) continue;
    const full = accessorize(pick.o, dctx, avail, anchor);
    const c = critique(full, dctx);
    if (c.overall < 55 && out.length >= 3) continue;   // only keep weak directions if we'd have fewer than 3
    out.push({ raw: pick.o, name: d.name, direction: d.id, outfit: full, critique: c, why: explain(full, c, dctx), ctx: dctx });
    if (out.length >= 4) break;
  }
  return { outfits: out.map(({ raw, ...r }) => r), missing: [] };
}

// ── Alternatives she owns
const formalityOf = (o) => { const c = CORE.map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean); return c.reduce((a, i) => a + (i.formality || 2), 0) / (c.length || 1); };
const accentsOf = (o) => ALL_SLOTS.map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean).flatMap((i) => i.colors).filter((c) => !isNeutral(c.name)).length;

export function alternatives(o, ctx, pool) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const base = critique(o, ctx).overall;
  const out = [];
  const bestSwap = (slots, filter, scoreFn, minScore) => {
    let best = null;
    for (const slot of slots) {
      for (const g of avail.filter((x) => slotOf(x) === slot && x.id !== o[slot]?.id && filter(x, slot))) {
        const next = { ...o, [slot]: g };
        if (slot === 'top' && isDress(g)) next.bottom = null;
        if (!isComplete(next)) continue;
        const sc = critique(next, ctx).overall;
        if (sc < minScore) continue;
        const v = scoreFn(next, sc);
        if (!best || v > best.v) best = { v, next, sc, piece: g };
      }
    }
    return best;
  };
  const push = (id, label, r) => { if (r) out.push({ id, label: label(r.piece), outfit: r.next, score: r.sc, delta: r.sc - base }); };
  push('shoes', (p) => `Different shoes: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(['shoes'], () => true, (_, sc) => sc, base - 10));
  push('top', (p) => `Replace the ${isDress(o.top) ? 'dress' : 'top'}: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(['top'], (x) => isDress(x) === isDress(o.top), (_, sc) => sc, base - 10));
  if (!o.outer) push('jacket', (p) => `Add a jacket: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(['outer'], () => true, (_, sc) => sc, base - 8));
  const f0 = formalityOf(o);
  push('formal', (p) => `More formal: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'], (x, slot) => (x.formality || 2) > (o[slot]?.formality ?? 0), (n, sc) => (formalityOf(n) - f0) * 12 + sc, base - 14));
  push('casual', (p) => `More casual: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'], (x, slot) => (x.formality || 2) < (o[slot]?.formality ?? 9), (n, sc) => (f0 - formalityOf(n)) * 12 + sc, base - 14));
  if (!o.bag || !o.accessory) push('accessory', (p) => `Add ${p.category === 'accessory' ? 'an accessory' : 'a piece'}: ${p.name}`, bestSwap([!o.bag ? 'bag' : null, !o.accessory ? 'accessory' : null].filter(Boolean), () => true, (_, sc) => sc, base - 5));
  const a0 = accentsOf(o);
  push('colorful', (p) => `More colorful: ${p.name}`, bestSwap(ALL_SLOTS, (x) => x.colors.some((c) => !isNeutral(c.name)), (n, sc) => (accentsOf(n) > a0 ? 20 : 0) + sc, base - 12));
  // de-duplicate (two goals can land on the same change)
  const seen = new Set();
  return out.filter((a) => (a.delta >= -14) && !seen.has(key(a.outfit)) && seen.add(key(a.outfit)));
}

// ── Family looks: one outfit per member, coordinated
function paletteOf(o) {
  return ALL_SLOTS.map((k) => o[k]).filter(Boolean).flatMap((i) => i.colors).filter((c) => !isNeutral(c.name));
}
export function coordination(outfits) {
  const pros = [], cons = [];
  let s = 0;
  const palettes = outfits.map(paletteOf);
  const names = palettes.map((p) => new Set(p.map((c) => c.name)));
  const all = [...new Set(palettes.flat().map((c) => c.name))];
  // shared accent across most of the family
  const sharedAccent = all.find((n) => names.filter((set) => set.has(n)).length >= Math.max(2, Math.ceil(outfits.length * 0.6)));
  if (sharedAccent) { s += 14; pros.push(`A shared thread of ${sharedAccent} ties everyone together.`); }
  if (all.length === 0) { s += 3; pros.push('An all-neutral family palette looks calm and coordinated.'); }
  else if (all.length === 1 && !sharedAccent) { s += 2; pros.push(`One splash of ${all[0]} brightens an otherwise neutral family palette.`); }
  if (all.length >= 4) { s -= 6 * (all.length - 3); cons.push(`Too many different colors across the family (${all.slice(0, 5).join(', ')}).`); }
  // accent hues should harmonise (analogous or complementary)
  const hexes = [...new Map(palettes.flat().map((c) => [c.name, c.hex])).values()];
  for (let i = 0; i < hexes.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < hexes.length; j++) {
    const d = hueDistance(hexToHsl(hexes[i])[0], hexToHsl(hexes[j])[0]);
    if (d > 45 && d < 140) { s -= 5; }
  }
  // same level of dressiness
  const f = outfits.map(formalityOf);
  const spread = Math.max(...f) - Math.min(...f);
  if (spread <= 1) { s += 8; pros.push('Everyone is dressed to the same level.'); }
  else if (spread > 1.6) { s -= 12; cons.push('Dressiness is uneven: one person looks much more formal than the others.'); }
  // not identical uniforms
  for (let i = 0; i < outfits.length; i++) for (let j = i + 1; j < outfits.length; j++) {
    const a = outfits[i].top, b = outfits[j].top;
    if (a && b && a.colors[0].name === b.colors[0].name && a.art === b.art) { s -= 8; cons.push('Two people would be wearing near-identical tops. Coordinated beats matching.'); }
  }
  return { score: s, pros, cons };
}

/**
 * @param members [{ profile, pool }] each member's OWN closet
 * @returns {{ members: [{ profile, outfit, critique }], coordination, why, missing: [{ name, missing }] }}
 */
export function familyLook(members, ctx) {
  const missing = members.map((m) => ({ name: m.profile.name, missing: missingForOutfit(m.pool) })).filter((m) => m.missing.length);
  if (missing.length) return { members: [], missing };
  const lists = members.map((m) => {
    const mctx = m.profile.kind === 'child' ? { ...ctx, formality: Math.max(1, ctx.formality - 0.5) } : ctx;
    return candidates(m.pool, mctx, null, 30).filter((x, i, arr) => arr.findIndex((y) => key(y.o) === key(x.o)) === i).slice(0, members.length > 3 ? 4 : 6).map((x) => ({ ...x, ctx: mctx }));
  });
  let best = null;
  const walk = (i, picked) => {
    if (i === lists.length) {
      const outfits = picked.map((p) => p.o);
      const co = coordination(outfits);
      const total = picked.reduce((a, p) => a + p.score, 0) / picked.length + co.score * 0.7;
      if (!best || total > best.total) best = { total, picked, co };
      return;
    }
    for (const c of lists[i]) walk(i + 1, [...picked, c]);
  };
  walk(0, []);
  const result = members.map((m, i) => {
    const o = accessorize(best.picked[i].o, best.picked[i].ctx, m.pool.filter(isAvailable), null);
    return { profile: m.profile, outfit: o, critique: critique(o, best.picked[i].ctx) };
  });
  const co = coordination(result.map((r) => r.outfit));
  const why = [co.pros[0], co.pros[1]].filter(Boolean).join(' ') + (co.cons[0] ? ` One thing to watch: ${co.cons[0].charAt(0).toLowerCase()}${co.cons[0].slice(1)}` : '');
  return { members: result, coordination: co, why: why || 'Each outfit suits the occasion on its own, and the palettes sit well together.', missing: [] };
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/imagine.js
====================================================================================================

// Realistic AI photos, made with the user's own Puter account (Puter's image models; the user covers the cost,
// usually from Puter's free monthly allowance). Every image made here is labelled as AI wherever it is shown, and it
// NEVER replaces a photo the user took: it is stored separately (garment.aiImage, outfit.aiLook).
import { loadPuter, puterError } from './claude.js';
import { CATEGORY_LABEL } from '../data/closet.js';

// Tried in order; only a missing/unsupported model moves on to the next one.
const MODELS = ['google/gemini-2.5-flash-image', 'black-forest-labs/flux-1.1-pro', 'black-forest-labs/flux-schnell'];

const colorWords = (item) => (item.colors || []).map((c) => c.name).filter(Boolean).join(' and ') || '';
const kindOf = (item) => item.art || item.subtype || CATEGORY_LABEL?.[item.category] || item.category || 'garment';

/** Words that describe one piece, from its saved details only. */
export function describePiece(item) {
  const bits = [colorWords(item), item.pattern && item.pattern !== 'solid' ? `${item.pattern} pattern` : '', item.material, kindOf(item)];
  return `${bits.filter(Boolean).join(' ')} ("${item.name}")`;
}

/** Product photo of a single piece, matching the look of the demo closet photos. */
export function piecePrompt(item) {
  const hex = (item.colors || []).map((c) => `${c.name}${c.hex ? ` (${c.hex})` : ''}`).join(' and ');
  return `${item.name}: ${hex || 'neutral'}${item.material ? `, ${item.material.toLowerCase()}` : ''}${item.fit ? `, ${item.fit} fit` : ''}`
    + `${item.pattern && item.pattern !== 'solid' ? `, ${item.pattern} pattern` : ''}, ${kindOf(item)}. `
    + 'Neatly laid flat, front view (or invisible ghost-mannequin style). Professional e-commerce product photograph, centered, '
    + 'seamless light gray (#F4F4F4) studio background, soft diffused lighting from the upper left, subtle natural contact shadow, '
    + 'true-to-life fabric texture and color, no person, no props, no text, no logos, portrait 4:5.';
}

/** A styled photo of a whole outfit. Neck-down, so it never pretends to be the user. */
export function outfitPrompt(pieces, { withReference } = {}) {
  const list = pieces.map(describePiece).join('; ');
  return 'Photorealistic full-length fashion photograph of an outfit worn by a model, framed from the neck down (no face), '
    + `standing naturally, plain warm light-gray studio background, soft daylight. The outfit is exactly: ${list}. `
    + (withReference ? 'The reference image shows these exact pieces: keep their colors, patterns and details as shown. ' : '')
    + 'Do not add other garments, bags or jewelry. No text, no logos.';
}

const loadImg = (src) => new Promise((res, rej) => { const i = new Image(); i.crossOrigin = 'anonymous'; i.onload = () => res(i); i.onerror = rej; i.src = src; });

/** Shrink to a sensible size and save as WebP (a few dozen KB), so it syncs quickly. */
async function compress(src, max = 900) {
  let img;
  try { const b = await (await fetch(src)).blob(); img = await loadImg(URL.createObjectURL(b)); }
  catch { img = await loadImg(src); }
  const k = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight));
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * k); c.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * k);
  c.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  return c.toDataURL('image/webp', 0.86);
}

/** One reference image with the pieces' own photos side by side (so the AI sees the real clothes). */
export async function referenceSheet(photos) {
  const list = photos.filter(Boolean).slice(0, 6);
  if (!list.length) return null;
  const cols = Math.min(3, list.length), rows = Math.ceil(list.length / cols), cw = 320, ch = 400;
  const c = document.createElement('canvas'); c.width = cols * cw; c.height = rows * ch;
  const x = c.getContext('2d'); x.fillStyle = '#ffffff'; x.fillRect(0, 0, c.width, c.height);
  for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
    try {
      const im = await loadImg(list[i]);
      const k = Math.min((cw - 20) / im.naturalWidth, (ch - 20) / im.naturalHeight);
      const w = im.naturalWidth * k, h = im.naturalHeight * k;
      x.drawImage(im, (i % cols) * cw + (cw - w) / 2, Math.floor(i / cols) * ch + (ch - h) / 2, w, h);
    } catch { /* skip a photo that won't load */ }
  }
  return c.toDataURL('image/jpeg', 0.85);
}

/**
 * Make an image. Returns { image (WebP data URL), model, usedReference }.
 * Errors carry .code: not_signed_in | rate_limited | upstream_error (see claude.js).
 */
export async function makeImage(prompt, { reference } = {}) {
  const puter = await loadPuter();
  if (!puter) throw Object.assign(new Error('Could not load Puter'), { code: 'upstream_error', serverMessage: 'The AI service (Puter) could not be reached.' });
  let signedIn = false;
  try { signedIn = await puter.auth.isSignedIn(); } catch { /* treat as signed out */ }
  if (!signedIn) throw Object.assign(new Error('Not signed in to Puter'), { code: 'not_signed_in' });
  let lastErr = null;
  for (const model of MODELS) {
    for (const ref of reference ? [reference, null] : [null]) { // if a model won't take the reference, try without it
      try {
        const el = await puter.ai.txt2img(prompt, { model, ...(ref ? { input_image: ref } : {}) });
        const src = el?.src || (typeof el === 'string' ? el : null);
        if (!src) throw Object.assign(new Error('No image came back'), { code: 'upstream_error' });
        return { image: await compress(src), model, usedReference: !!ref };
      } catch (e) {
        lastErr = puterError(e);
        if (lastErr.code === 'not_signed_in' || lastErr.code === 'rate_limited') throw lastErr;
        if (lastErr.code === 'bad_model') break; // next model
      }
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/insights.js
====================================================================================================

// Phase 2: wardrobe versatility, Rediscover My Closet, outfit history stats, learned preferences.
// Pure functions over her own data; no AI needed, so everything here works offline and is testable.
import { critique } from './critic.js';
import { candidates, isAvailable, styleWithThis, generateOutfits } from './generator.js';
import { buildContext } from './context.js';
import { OCCASIONS, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';

const DAY = 86400000;
const daysBetween = (a, b) => Math.round((new Date(b) - new Date(a)) / DAY);
export const today = () => new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);

// The everyday situations a versatile piece should handle.
export const VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS = [
  { occasion: 'everyday', weather: 'warm' }, { occasion: 'everyday', weather: 'cool' },
  { occasion: 'work', weather: 'cool' }, { occasion: 'date', weather: 'warm' },
  { occasion: 'church', weather: 'warm' }, { occasion: 'vacation', weather: 'hot' },
].map((c) => buildContext(c, OCCASIONS));

const strong = (o, ctx) => { const c = critique(o, ctx); return c.overall >= 75 && !c.dealBreakers.length; };

/** Wear counts and last-worn dates per item, from Outfit History. */
export function wearStats(history) {
  const stats = {};
  for (const h of history) {
    for (const id of Object.values(h.slots || {})) {
      if (!id) continue;
      const s = (stats[id] ||= { wears: 0, last: null });
      s.wears++;
      if (!s.last || h.date > s.last) s.last = h.date;
    }
  }
  return stats;
}

/**
 * Versatility of every available piece: how many strong, distinct outfits it is part of
 * across everyday situations, and in which situations it works.
 * @returns {{ items: [{ item, combos, contexts: string[] }], score: number (0-100), hardToStyle: [] }}
 */
function versatilityOf(item, avail, contexts) {
  const partners = new Set();
  let combos = 0;
  const where = [];
  for (const ctx of contexts) {
    const list = candidates(avail, ctx, item, 400).filter(({ o }) => strong(o, ctx));
    combos += list.length;
    for (const { o } of list) for (const k of ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes']) if (o[k] && o[k].id !== item.id) partners.add(o[k].id);
    if (list.length) where.push(ctx.occasionLabel + (ctx.weather === 'Hot' || ctx.weather === 'Cool' ? ` (${ctx.weather.toLowerCase()})` : ''));
  }
  return { item, combos, partners: partners.size, contexts: [...new Set(where)] };
}
const coreOf = (pool) => pool.filter(isAvailable).filter((g) => ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].includes(slotOf(g)));
function summarize(rows) {
  const items = rows.sort((a, b) => b.partners - a.partners || b.combos - a.combos);
  const working = items.filter((x) => x.contexts.length >= 2 && x.partners >= 3).length;
  const score = items.length ? Math.round((working / items.length) * 100) : 0;
  return { items, score, hardToStyle: items.filter((x) => x.contexts.length === 0) };
}

/**
 * Versatility of every available piece: how many other pieces it makes strong outfits with, and in which
 * everyday situations. @returns {{ items: [{ item, combos, partners, contexts }], score: 0-100, hardToStyle }}
 */
export function versatility(pool, contexts = VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  return summarize(coreOf(pool).map((item) => versatilityOf(item, avail, contexts)));
}

/** Same result, computed in small batches so a big closet never freezes the page. onProgress(done, total). */
export async function versatilityAsync(pool, onProgress = () => {}, contexts = VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS, isCancelled = () => false) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const core = coreOf(pool);
  const rows = [];
  let t = performance.now();
  for (let i = 0; i < core.length; i++) {
    if (isCancelled()) return null;
    rows.push(versatilityOf(core[i], avail, contexts));
    if (performance.now() - t > 30) { onProgress(i + 1, core.length); await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)); t = performance.now(); }
  }
  onProgress(core.length, core.length);
  return summarize(rows);
}

/** Why a piece is hard to style: the slot that has nothing suitable to pair with it. */
export function whyHardToStyle(item, pool) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const s = slotOf(item);
  const need = s === 'shoes' ? ['top', 'bottom'] : s === 'outer' ? ['top', 'shoes'] : s === 'top' && item.category === 'dress' ? ['shoes'] : s === 'top' ? ['bottom', 'shoes'] : ['top', 'shoes'];
  const lacking = need.filter((slot) => {
    const options = avail.filter((g) => slotOf(g) === slot);
    return !options.some((g) => Math.abs((g.formality || 2) - (item.formality || 2)) <= 1.5);
  });
  if (lacking.length) return `Nothing in the closet ${lacking.length > 1 ? `(${lacking.join(', ')})` : `(${lacking[0]})`} sits at its level of dressiness.`;
  return 'Its colors or style clash with most of the other pieces.';
}

/**
 * Rediscover My Closet: available pieces she hasn't worn in a while (or ever), each with a strong outfit built around it.
 * Items added in the last 14 days are skipped (they're not forgotten, they're new).
 */
export function rediscover(pool, history, { now = today(), ctx = null, contexts = null, limit = 4, minDays = 30 } = {}) {
  const ctxs = contexts || (ctx ? [ctx] : VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS.slice(0, 2));
  const stats = wearStats(history);
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const forgotten = avail
    .filter((g) => ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].includes(slotOf(g)))
    .filter((g) => !g.addedAt || daysBetween(g.addedAt, now) >= 14)
    .map((g) => ({ item: g, last: stats[g.id]?.last || null, wears: stats[g.id]?.wears || 0 }))
    .filter((x) => !x.last || daysBetween(x.last, now) >= minDays)
    .sort((a, b) => (a.last || '0000') .localeCompare(b.last || '0000'));
  const out = [];
  const usedPartners = new Map(); // keep the suggestions varied: penalise partners already shown
  for (const f of forgotten) {
    if (out.length >= limit) break;
    let best = null;
    for (const c of ctxs) {
      const options = [...styleWithThis(avail, f.item, c).outfits, ...generateOutfits(avail, c, f.item).outfits]
        .filter((o) => !o.critique.dealBreakers.length && o.critique.overall >= 75);
      for (const o of options) {
        const repeat = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'].reduce((a, k) => a + (o.outfit[k] && o.outfit[k].id !== f.item.id ? usedPartners.get(o.outfit[k].id) || 0 : 0), 0);
        const val = o.critique.overall - repeat * 4;
        if (!best || val > best.val) best = { ...o, val, ctx: o.ctx || c };
      }
    }
    if (!best) continue;
    for (const g of Object.values(best.outfit)) if (g && g.id !== f.item.id) usedPartners.set(g.id, (usedPartners.get(g.id) || 0) + 1);
    out.push({ ...f, daysSince: f.last ? daysBetween(f.last, now) : null, outfit: best.outfit, critique: best.critique, why: best.why, ctx: best.ctx });
  }
  return out;
}

export const FEEDBACK_TAGS = [
  { id: 'loved', label: 'Loved it', w: 2 },
  { id: 'compliments', label: 'Got compliments', w: 2 },
  { id: 'comfortable', label: 'Comfortable', w: 1 },
  { id: 'uncomfortable', label: 'Uncomfortable', w: -2 },
  { id: 'too-warm', label: 'Too warm', w: -1 },
  { id: 'too-cold', label: 'Too cold', w: -1 },
  { id: 'too-formal', label: 'Too formal', w: -1 },
  { id: 'too-casual', label: 'Too casual', w: -1 },
  { id: 'not-me', label: "Didn't feel like me", w: -2 },
];

/**
 * Learned taste from rated wears (only the profile's own history).
 * Ratings 1–5 → -2…+2; tags add or subtract. Returns small per-item/color/style nudges for the ranking.
 */
export function preferenceProfile(history, garmentsById, { now = today() } = {}) {
  const items = {}, colors = {}, styles = {};
  const counts = { items: {}, colors: {}, styles: {} };
  let rated = 0;
  for (const h of history) {
    const tagW = (h.tags || []).reduce((a, t) => a + (FEEDBACK_TAGS.find((x) => x.id === t)?.w || 0), 0);
    if (!h.rating && !tagW) continue;
    rated++;
    const v = (h.rating ? h.rating - 3 : 0) + tagW * 0.5;
    for (const id of Object.values(h.slots || {})) {
      const g = id && garmentsById(id);
      if (!g) continue;
      items[id] = (items[id] || 0) + v; counts.items[id] = (counts.items[id] || 0) + 1;
      const col = g.colors?.[0]?.name;
      if (col) { colors[col] = (colors[col] || 0) + v; counts.colors[col] = (counts.colors[col] || 0) + 1; }
      if (g.style) { styles[g.style] = (styles[g.style] || 0) + v; counts.styles[g.style] = (counts.styles[g.style] || 0) + 1; }
    }
  }
  const avg = (sum, n) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(sum).map(([k, s]) => [k, Math.max(-3, Math.min(3, s / Math.max(1, n[k] ** 0.5)))]));
  const recent = history.filter((h) => daysBetween(h.date, now) <= 3).flatMap((h) => Object.values(h.slots || {})).filter(Boolean);
  return { items: avg(items, counts.items), colors: avg(colors, counts.colors), styles: avg(styles, counts.styles), recent, rated };
}

/** Plain-language summary of what has been learned, for the History page. */
export function describePreferences(p, garmentsById) {
  const top = (obj, sign) => Object.entries(obj).filter(([, v]) => sign * v >= 1).sort((a, b) => sign * (b[1] - a[1])).slice(0, 3).map(([k]) => k);
  const lines = [];
  const likedItems = top(p.items, 1).map((id) => garmentsById(id)?.name).filter(Boolean);
  const dislikedItems = top(p.items, -1).map((id) => garmentsById(id)?.name).filter(Boolean);
  if (likedItems.length) lines.push(`Favourites: ${likedItems.join(', ')}.`);
  if (top(p.colors, 1).length) lines.push(`Colors that work for you: ${top(p.colors, 1).join(', ')}.`);
  if (top(p.styles, 1).length) lines.push(`Styles you rate highly: ${top(p.styles, 1).join(', ')}.`);
  if (dislikedItems.length) lines.push(`Shown less often: ${dislikedItems.join(', ')}.`);
  return lines;
}


====================================================================================================

// Fashion Inspiration Bridge (prototype).
// Production: Gemini vision decomposes a user-supplied photo/link into items, and pgvector
// matches them to the closet. Here, a look is described in text and decomposed by keywords.

import { NAMED_COLORS, hexToHsl, isNeutral, hueDistance } from './color.js';

// Words people actually use → [category, closest garment type]. Longer phrases are checked first.
const NOUNS = [
  ['long sleeve', 'top', 'tee'], ['t-shirt', 'top', 'tee'], ['tshirt', 'top', 'tee'], ['tank', 'top', 'tee'], ['cami', 'top', 'tee'], ['crop top', 'top', 'tee'], ['tee', 'top', 'tee'], ['top', 'top', 'tee'],
  ['stripe', 'top', 'stripe'], ['breton', 'top', 'stripe'], ['turtleneck', 'top', 'turtleneck'], ['mock neck', 'top', 'turtleneck'], ['polo', 'top', 'shirt'],
  ['blouse', 'top', 'shirt'], ['button-down', 'top', 'shirt'], ['button down', 'top', 'shirt'], ['oxford', 'top', 'shirt'], ['shirt', 'top', 'shirt'],
  ['sweatshirt', 'top', 'knit'], ['hoodie', 'top', 'knit'], ['sweater', 'top', 'knit'], ['knit', 'top', 'knit'], ['jumper', 'top', 'knit'], ['pullover', 'top', 'knit'],
  ['jeans', 'bottom', 'jeans'], ['denim', 'bottom', 'jeans'], ['shorts', 'bottom', 'shorts'], ['skirt', 'bottom', 'skirt'], ['skort', 'bottom', 'skirt'],
  ['trousers', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['pants', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['chinos', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['slacks', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['culottes', 'bottom', 'trousers'],
  ['joggers', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['leggings', 'bottom', 'trousers'], ['cargo', 'bottom', 'trousers'],
  ['dress', 'dress', 'dress'], ['gown', 'dress', 'dress'], ['jumpsuit', 'dress', 'dress'],
  ['trench', 'outer', 'trench'], ['raincoat', 'outer', 'trench'], ['mac', 'outer', 'trench'], ['blazer', 'outer', 'blazer'], ['suit jacket', 'outer', 'blazer'],
  ['puffer', 'outer', 'puffer'], ['down jacket', 'outer', 'puffer'], ['parka', 'outer', 'coat'], ['overcoat', 'outer', 'coat'], ['coat', 'outer', 'coat'],
  ['cardigan', 'outer', 'cardigan'], ['shacket', 'outer', 'jacket'], ['bomber', 'outer', 'jacket'], ['jacket', 'outer', 'jacket'], ['vest', 'outer', 'jacket'],
  ['sneakers', 'shoes', 'sneaker'], ['trainers', 'shoes', 'sneaker'], ['sneaker', 'shoes', 'sneaker'], ['loafers', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['loafer', 'shoes', 'loafer'],
  ['flats', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['ballet', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['mules', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['oxfords', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['pumps', 'shoes', 'loafer'], ['heels', 'shoes', 'sandal'],
  ['boots', 'shoes', 'boot'], ['boot', 'shoes', 'boot'], ['booties', 'shoes', 'boot'], ['sandals', 'shoes', 'sandal'], ['slides', 'shoes', 'sandal'], ['flip flops', 'shoes', 'sandal'], ['espadrilles', 'shoes', 'sandal'],
  ['crossbody', 'accessory', 'crossbody'], ['shoulder bag', 'accessory', 'crossbody'], ['clutch', 'accessory', 'crossbody'], ['purse', 'accessory', 'crossbody'], ['handbag', 'accessory', 'crossbody'],
  ['tote', 'accessory', 'tote'], ['backpack', 'accessory', 'tote'], ['bag', 'accessory', 'crossbody'],
  ['scarf', 'accessory', 'scarf'], ['hat', 'accessory', 'hat'], ['cap', 'accessory', 'hat'], ['beret', 'accessory', 'hat'], ['beanie', 'accessory', 'hat'],
  ['necklace', 'accessory', 'necklace'], ['pendant', 'accessory', 'necklace'], ['chain', 'accessory', 'necklace'], ['belt', 'accessory', 'belt'],
];
const NOUNS_SORTED = NOUNS.slice().sort((a, b) => b[0].length - a[0].length);

// Everyday color words → the app's named colors.
const COLOR_WORDS = [
  ['off white', 'off-white'], ['ivory', 'cream'], ['ecru', 'cream'], ['oatmeal', 'beige'], ['sand', 'beige'], ['nude', 'beige'], ['stone', 'beige'], ['tan', 'camel'], ['cognac', 'brown'], ['chocolate', 'brown'],
  ['grey', 'gray'], ['silver', 'light gray'], ['heather', 'gray'], ['slate', 'charcoal'], ['jet', 'black'],
  ['indigo', 'denim blue'], ['blue jeans', 'denim blue'], ['cobalt', 'denim blue'], ['royal blue', 'denim blue'], ['sky', 'light blue'], ['baby blue', 'light blue'], ['powder blue', 'light blue'], ['midnight', 'navy'], ['blue', 'denim blue'],
  ['sage', 'olive'], ['army', 'olive'], ['khaki green', 'olive'], ['forest', 'green'], ['emerald', 'green'], ['mint', 'green'],
  ['maroon', 'burgundy'], ['wine', 'burgundy'], ['oxblood', 'burgundy'], ['plum', 'burgundy'], ['scarlet', 'red'], ['cherry', 'red'],
  ['terracotta', 'rust'], ['orange', 'rust'], ['brick', 'rust'], ['copper', 'rust'], ['yellow', 'mustard'], ['gold', 'mustard'], ['ochre', 'mustard'],
  ['blush', 'pink'], ['rose', 'pink'], ['fuchsia', 'pink'], ['lilac', 'lavender'], ['purple', 'lavender'], ['violet', 'lavender'],
];
const byName = (n) => NAMED_COLORS.find((c) => c.name === n);
function colorIn(p, art) {
  const direct = NAMED_COLORS.slice().sort((a, b) => b.name.length - a.name.length).find((c) => new RegExp(`\\b${c.name}\\b`).test(p));
  if (direct) return direct;
  const syn = COLOR_WORDS.slice().sort((a, b) => b[0].length - a[0].length).find(([w]) => new RegExp(`\\b${w}\\b`).test(p));
  if (syn) return byName(syn[1]);
  if (art === 'jeans') return byName('denim blue');
  return null;
}

/** "black turtleneck, olive trousers, white sneakers" → [{label, category, art, color}] (keyword matching, no AI). */
export function decomposeLook(text) {
  const parts = text.toLowerCase().replace(/[.!?]/g, ',').split(/,|\+|\band\b|\bwith\b|;|\n|\//).map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  const items = [];
  for (const p of parts) {
    const noun = NOUNS_SORTED.find(([w]) => new RegExp(`\\b${w}`).test(p));
    if (!noun) continue;
    const color = colorIn(p, noun[2]);
    items.push({ label: p, category: noun[1], art: noun[2], color: color ? { name: color.name, hex: color.hex } : null });
  }
  return items;
}

// Garment types that can stand in for each other.
const FAMILY = { tee: 'casual-top', stripe: 'casual-top', knit: 'warm-top', turtleneck: 'warm-top', shirt: 'shirt', coat: 'long-layer', trench: 'long-layer', blazer: 'tailored', jacket: 'jacket', puffer: 'jacket', cardigan: 'warm-top', loafer: 'smart-shoe', boot: 'boot', sneaker: 'sneaker', sandal: 'open-shoe', tote: 'bag', crossbody: 'bag' };
const slotCat = (c) => (c === 'dress' ? 'dress' : c);

function colorScore(want, g) {
  if (!want.color) return 0.75; // the look didn't say a color
  let best = 0;
  for (const c of g.colors || []) {
    let cs;
    if (c.name === want.color.name) cs = 1;
    else if (isNeutral(c.name) && isNeutral(want.color.name)) cs = Math.abs(hexToHsl(c.hex)[2] - hexToHsl(want.color.hex)[2]) < 0.15 ? 0.7 : 0.35;
    else if (!isNeutral(c.name) && !isNeutral(want.color.name)) cs = hueDistance(hexToHsl(c.hex)[0], hexToHsl(want.color.hex)[0]) < 30 ? 0.65 : 0.15;
    else cs = 0.15;
    best = Math.max(best, cs);
  }
  return best;
}

/** How well one closet piece stands in for one wanted piece: { score, level: exact | close | approximate | null, why } */
export function similarity(want, g) {
  const sameCat = slotCat(g.category) === slotCat(want.category) || (want.art === 'cardigan' && g.art === 'cardigan');
  if (!sameCat) return { score: 0, level: null };
  const cs = colorScore(want, g);
  const shape = g.art === want.art ? 1 : FAMILY[g.art] && FAMILY[g.art] === FAMILY[want.art] ? 0.75 : 0.45;
  const score = 0.6 * cs + 0.4 * shape;
  const level = shape === 1 && cs >= 0.99 ? 'exact' : shape >= 0.75 && cs >= 0.65 ? 'close' : score >= 0.55 ? 'approximate' : null;
  const why = level === 'exact' ? 'Same type and color'
    : [shape < 1 ? `a ${g.art} instead of a ${want.art}` : '', cs < 0.99 && want.color ? `${g.colors?.[0]?.name || 'another color'} instead of ${want.color.name}` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ');
  return { score, level, why };
}

/**
 * Map a decomposed look onto the closet.
 * Each wanted piece becomes an exact / close / approximate match, or is missing (with the closest thing she owns, if any).
 * Coverage counts exact and close matches fully and approximate ones as half.
 */
export function matchLook(items, closet) {
  const used = new Set();
  const matches = [], missing = [];
  for (const want of items) {
    let best = null;
    for (const g of closet) {
      if (used.has(g.id)) continue;
      const s = similarity(want, g);
      if (s.level && (!best || s.score > best.s.score)) best = { g, s };
    }
    if (best) { used.add(best.g.id); matches.push({ want, garment: best.g, score: best.s.score, level: best.s.level, exact: best.s.level === 'exact', why: best.s.why }); }
    else {
      const nearest = closet.filter((g) => slotCat(g.category) === slotCat(want.category)).map((g) => ({ g, s: similarity(want, g) })).sort((a, b) => b.s.score - a.s.score)[0];
      missing.push({ ...want, reason: nearest ? `Nothing close enough. Nearest: ${nearest.g.name} (${nearest.s.why || 'different'})` : `No ${CATEGORY_WORD[slotCat(want.category)] || want.category} in this closet yet` });
    }
  }
  const points = matches.reduce((a, m) => a + (m.level === 'approximate' ? 0.5 : 1), 0);
  const coverage = items.length ? Math.round((points / items.length) * 100) : 0;
  return { coverage, matches, missing };
}
const CATEGORY_WORD = { top: 'tops', bottom: 'bottoms', dress: 'dresses', outer: 'outerwear', shoes: 'shoes', accessory: 'accessories of that kind' };

  },
  {
    id: 'insp-2', title: 'Parisian Weekend', source: 'Sample street-style look', mood: '#C9B79C',

    text: 'cream knit sweater, black trousers, charcoal coat, black boots, burgundy scarf',
  },
  {
    id: 'insp-4', title: 'Seaside Linen', source: 'Sample street-style look', mood: '#BFD3DB',
    text: 'off-white linen shirt, beige shorts, brown sandals, tote',
  },

====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/planner.js
