// Trip planner: one strong outfit per day, all different, re-using pieces so the suitcase stays small.
import { critique } from './critic.js';
import { candidates, isAvailable, describe, explain, missingForOutfit } from './generator.js';

const DAY = 86400000;
export const iso = (d) => new Date(d).toISOString().slice(0, 10);
export function daysBetween(start, end) {
  const out = [];
  for (let t = new Date(start + 'T12:00:00').getTime(); t <= new Date(end + 'T12:00:00').getTime() && out.length < 31; t += DAY) out.push(iso(t));
  return out;
}

const PACK_KEYS = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'];
const coreKey = (o) => ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes'].map((k) => o[k]?.id || '-').join('|');
const coreKeyOf = (x) => coreKey(x.outfit || x);

/**
 * Plan a trip.
 * @returns {{ days: [{ date, outfit, critique, name, why }], packing: [{ item, days }], missing: string[], note }}
 */
export function planTrip(pool, ctx, dates, avoid = []) {
  const avoidSet = new Set(avoid.map(coreKeyOf));
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const missing = missingForOutfit(avail);
  if (missing.length) return { days: [], packing: [], missing, note: '' };
  const options = candidates(avail, ctx, null, 300)
    .map(({ o }) => ({ o, c: critique(o, ctx) }))
    .filter(({ c }) => !c.dealBreakers.length && c.overall >= 72);
  if (!options.length) return { days: [], packing: [], missing: [], note: `Nothing in this closet makes a strong outfit for ${ctx.occasionLabel.toLowerCase()} in ${ctx.weather.toLowerCase()} weather.` };

  const packed = new Map(); // item id → count of days used
  const usedOutfits = new Set();
  const days = [];
  let prev = null;
  for (const date of dates) {
    let best = null;
    for (const { o, c } of options) {
      const k = coreKey(o);
      let s = c.overall;
      if (usedOutfits.has(k)) s -= 40;                      // every day looks different
      if (avoidSet.has(k)) s -= 12;                         // "Re-plan": prefer outfits not in the previous plan
      if (prev && o.top?.id === prev.top?.id) s -= 25;      // never the same top two days running
      for (const key of PACK_KEYS) {
        const g = o[key];
        if (!g) continue;
        const n = packed.get(g.id) || 0;
        if (n > 0) s += key === 'top' ? 2 : 7;              // re-wearing bottoms, shoes and layers keeps the bag light
        if (key === 'top' && n >= 2) s -= 12;               // but a top shouldn't be worn on 3+ days
      }
      if (!best || s > best.s) best = { o, c, s, k };
    }
    usedOutfits.add(best.k);
    for (const key of PACK_KEYS) if (best.o[key]) packed.set(best.o[key].id, (packed.get(best.o[key].id) || 0) + 1);
    days.push({ date, outfit: best.o, critique: best.c, name: describe(best.o), why: explain(best.o, best.c, ctx) });
    prev = best.o;
  }
  const byId = new Map(avail.map((g) => [g.id, g]));
  const packing = [...packed.entries()].map(([id, n]) => ({ item: byId.get(id), days: n })).filter((x) => x.item)
    .sort((a, b) => PACK_KEYS.indexOf(slot(a.item)) - PACK_KEYS.indexOf(slot(b.item)) || b.days - a.days);
  const distinct = new Set(days.map((d) => coreKey(d.outfit))).size;
  const note = distinct < days.length ? `Your closet has ${distinct} strong different outfit${distinct > 1 ? 's' : ''} for this trip, so some days repeat. Adding pieces would give more variety.` : '';
  return { days, packing, missing: [], note };
}
const slot = (g) => (g.category === 'dress' ? 'top' : g.category === 'accessory' ? (['tote', 'crossbody'].includes(g.art) ? 'bag' : 'accessory') : g.category);


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/stylist.js
====================================================================================================

// AI stylist features (Gemini via Puter on the live site). Every function returns
// { error } / { offline } instead of throwing, so callers fall back to the rules engine.
// Whatever the AI suggests is VALIDATED by the rules engine before it is shown.
import { getClaude, noteFailure } from './claude.js';
import { NAMED_COLORS } from './color.js';
import { short, critique, isComplete, isDress } from './critic.js';
import { generateOutfits, explain, describe, isAvailable, GOOD } from './generator.js';
import { ARTS, STYLES, SLEEVES, OCCASIONS, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';

const CATS = Object.keys(ARTS);
const STYLE_IDS = STYLES.map((s) => s.id);
const OCC_IDS = OCCASIONS.map((o) => o.id);
const COLOR_NAMES = NAMED_COLORS.map((c) => c.name);
const clampInt = (v, lo, hi, d) => { const n = Math.round(Number(v)); return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.max(lo, Math.min(hi, n)) : d; };
const oneOf = (v, list, d) => (list.includes(v) ? v : d);
const arr = (v) => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []);
const str = (v, max = 400) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v.trim().slice(0, max) : '');

/** Run an AI call; returns { value, provider } or { error } / { offline } (never throws). */
async function ask(prompt, opts) {
  const ai = await getClaude();
  if (!ai) return { error: null, offline: true };
  if (opts?.images?.length && !ai.images) opts = { ...opts, images: undefined };
  try { return { value: await ai.json(prompt, opts), provider: ai.name }; }
  catch (e) { return { error: noteFailure(e), code: e?.code }; }
}

const garmentLine = (g) => [
  g.id, g.name, slotOf(g) === 'top' && isDress(g) ? 'dress' : slotOf(g), g.colors.map((c) => c.name).join('/'),
  g.pattern && g.pattern !== 'solid' ? g.pattern : '', g.material, g.style ? `style ${g.style}` : '', `fit ${g.fit}`,
  `warmth ${g.warmth}/5`, `rain ${g.water || 1}/5`, `formality ${g.formality}/5`, `seasons ${(g.seasons || []).join('/')}`,
].filter(Boolean).join(' | ');

// ─────────────────────────────────────────── 1. Vision tagging
export async function tagGarment(cutoutBlob, deviceDraft) {
  const prompt = `You are the cataloguing assistant for a digital wardrobe app.
The image is ONE clothing item photographed by its owner, with the background removed.
On-device pixel analysis measured its dominant color as "${deviceDraft.colors[0].name}" (${deviceDraft.colors[0].hex}). Trust that unless the image clearly shows a different main color.

Reply with ONLY a JSON object, no other text:
{
  "name": "short retail-style product name, e.g. 'Olive Wide Chino Trousers'",
  "category": one of ${JSON.stringify(CATS)}  (bags, scarves, hats, jewelry and belts are "accessory"),
  "art": the closest shape for that category: ${JSON.stringify(ARTS)},
  "color": main color, one of ${JSON.stringify(COLOR_NAMES)},
  "secondColor": a second color from the same list if patterned or two-tone, else null,
  "pattern": "solid" | "stripe" | "check" | "floral" | "dots" | "graphic" | "other",
  "material": best guess, e.g. "cotton twill", "merino wool", "suede",
  "style": one of ${JSON.stringify(STYLE_IDS)},
  "sleeve": one of ${JSON.stringify(SLEEVES)} for tops/dresses/outerwear, else null,
  "weight": "light" | "mid" | "heavy",
  "fit": "fitted" | "regular" | "relaxed" | "oversized",
  "warmth": 0-5, "water": 1-5 (rain resistance), "formality": 1-5 (1 lounge … 5 black tie),
  "seasons": array of "spring","summer","autumn","winter",
  "occasions": array from ${JSON.stringify(OCC_IDS)} where it would be appropriate,
  "length": for outerwear "cropped" | "hip" | "long", else null,
  "profile": for shoes "sleek" | "chunky", else null,
  "confidence": 0-1,
  "note": one short sentence about anything you were unsure of, or ""
}
If the image is not a clothing item, set "confidence" to 0.`;
  const r = await ask(prompt, { images: [cutoutBlob], tier: 'default' });
  if (!r.value) return { error: r.error, offline: r.offline };
  return { provider: r.provider, garment: applyTags(deviceDraft, r.value) };
}

/** Validate the AI's tags and merge them into the on-device draft (never trust blindly). */
export function applyTags(draft, v = {}) {
  const category = oneOf(v.category, CATS, draft.category);
  const colorName = oneOf(v.color, COLOR_NAMES, draft.colors[0].name);
  const second = COLOR_NAMES.includes(v.secondColor) ? NAMED_COLORS.find((c) => c.name === v.secondColor) : null;
  const seasons = arr(v.seasons).filter((s) => ['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter'].includes(s));
  return {
    ...draft,
    name: str(v.name, 80) || draft.name,
    category,
    art: oneOf(v.art, ARTS[category], ARTS[category][0]),
    colors: [{ name: colorName, hex: draft.colors[0].hex }, ...(second && second.name !== colorName ? [{ name: second.name, hex: second.hex }] : [])],
    pattern: oneOf(v.pattern, ['solid', 'stripe', 'check', 'floral', 'dots', 'graphic', 'other'], 'solid'),
    material: str(v.material, 60) || draft.material,
    style: oneOf(v.style, STYLE_IDS, draft.style || 'casual'),
    sleeve: oneOf(v.sleeve, SLEEVES, null),
    weight: oneOf(v.weight, ['light', 'mid', 'heavy'], draft.weight),
    fit: oneOf(v.fit, ['fitted', 'regular', 'relaxed', 'oversized'], 'regular'),
    warmth: clampInt(v.warmth, 0, 5, draft.warmth),
    water: clampInt(v.water, 1, 5, 1),
    formality: clampInt(v.formality, 1, 5, draft.formality),
    seasons: seasons.length ? seasons : draft.seasons,
    occasions: arr(v.occasions).filter((o) => OCC_IDS.includes(o)),
    ...(category === 'outer' ? { length: oneOf(v.length, ['cropped', 'hip', 'long'], 'hip') } : {}),
    ...(category === 'shoes' ? { profile: oneOf(v.profile, ['sleek', 'chunky'], 'sleek') } : {}),
    confidence: Math.max(0, Math.min(1, Number(v.confidence) || 0.5)),
    aiNote: str(v.note, 200),
    taggedBy: 'ai',
    tagSource: 'ai',
    // An AI reading of a photo is still a suggestion. Fabric is hard to judge from a picture, so it always stays
    // "not checked" until she confirms it; with low confidence every detail does.
    unverified: (Number(v.confidence) || 0) >= 0.75 ? ['material'] : ['category', 'art', 'pattern', 'material', 'fit', 'warmth', 'formality', 'water', 'style'],
  };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────── 2. Stylist critique
export async function critiqueWithClaude(outfit, ctx, rules) {
  const pieces = ['top', 'bottom', 'outer', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'].filter((k) => outfit[k]).map((k) => `${k.toUpperCase()}: ${garmentLine(outfit[k])}`);
  if (!pieces.length) return null;
  const measured = rules.pillars.map((p) => `${p.label} (rules score ${p.score}): ${[...p.pros.map((x) => '+ ' + x), ...p.cons.map((x) => '- ' + x)].join(' ')}`).join('\n');
  const ids = rules.pillars.map((p) => p.id);
  const prompt = `You are an honest, specific personal stylist. Write for someone with no fashion vocabulary: plain words, no jargon. No flattery; name real trade-offs.

SITUATION: "${ctx.prompt}" → ${ctx.tempC}°C, rain ${ctx.rain >= 0.6 ? 'likely' : 'unlikely'}, occasion: ${ctx.occasionLabel}${ctx.style ? `, wanted style: ${ctx.style}` : ''}${ctx.prefs?.length ? `, preferences: ${ctx.prefs.join(', ')}` : ''}.
OUTFIT:
${pieces.join('\n')}

MEASURED FACTS from the app's rules engine (warmth, water resistance, formality, color and pattern math). Do not contradict these; you may add your own observations:
${measured}

Evaluate on exactly these pillars: ${ids.join(', ')}.
Reply with ONLY this JSON:
{
  "verdict": "one sentence overall take, like a friendly stylist would say it",
  "score": 0-100,
  "pillars": { ${ids.map((id) => `"${id}": { "score": 0-100, "pros": ["…"], "cons": ["…"] }`).join(', ')} },
  "styling_tip": "one concrete tip that needs no new purchase (a tuck, a roll, a swap within this closet)"
}
Each pros/cons list: 0-3 short sentences that name the actual pieces by color and type, never by id.`;
  const r = await ask(prompt, { tier: 'default' });
  if (!r.value) return { error: r.error, offline: r.offline };
  const v = r.value || {};
  const pillars = rules.pillars.map((p) => {
    const c = v.pillars?.[p.id] || {};
    return { ...p, score: clampInt(c.score, 0, 100, p.score), pros: arr(c.pros).map((x) => str(x)).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3), cons: arr(c.cons).map((x) => str(x)).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 3) };
  });
  return {
    provider: r.provider,
    critique: { ...rules, overall: clampInt(v.score, 0, 100, rules.overall), pillars, verdict: str(v.verdict, 300), tip: str(v.styling_tip, 300), by: 'claude', provider: r.provider },
  };
}

const likedLine = (ctx, avail) => {
  const p = ctx.personal;
  if (!p) return '';
  const liked = avail.filter((g) => (p.items?.[g.id] || 0) >= 1).map((g) => g.id);
  const disliked = avail.filter((g) => (p.items?.[g.id] || 0) <= -1).map((g) => g.id);
  return [liked.length ? `She has rated outfits with these pieces highly: ${liked.join(', ')}.` : '', disliked.length ? `She rated outfits with these poorly, so use them only if they clearly work: ${disliked.join(', ')}.` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' ') + (liked.length || disliked.length ? '\n' : '');
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────── 3. Three outfits (AI picks, rules engine validates)
/**
 * @returns {{ outfits: [{ name, outfit, critique, why, source: 'ai'|'rules', note? }], provider, error?, missing? }}
 * Always returns the best available answer: AI picks that pass validation, topped up from the rules engine.
 */
export async function styleWithAI({ ctx, pool, anchor = null, direction = null }) {
  const avail = pool.filter(isAvailable);
  const rules = generateOutfits(avail, ctx, anchor);
  if (rules.missing?.length || rules.unavailable) return { ...rules, outfits: [] };

  const closet = avail.map(garmentLine).join('\n');
  const q = `You are the outfit stylist of a wardrobe app. Build outfits ONLY from the closet below (the person's own, available clothes).

THE SITUATION: occasion ${ctx.occasionLabel} · ${ctx.tempC}°C${ctx.rain >= 0.6 ? ', rain likely' : ''}${ctx.style ? ` · style: ${ctx.style}` : ''}${ctx.prefs?.length ? ` · preferences: ${ctx.prefs.join(', ')}` : ''}${ctx.prompt ? ` · note: "${ctx.prompt}"` : ''}.
${likedLine(ctx, avail)}${anchor ? `MUST INCLUDE in every outfit: ${anchor.id} (${anchor.name}).` : ''}${direction ? `\nSTYLING DIRECTION: ${direction}.` : ''}

CLOSET (id | name | slot | colors | pattern | material | style | fit | warmth | rain | formality | seasons):
${closet}

Build exactly 3 distinct outfits that a stylish person would genuinely want to wear. Each needs: (top + bottom) OR a dress (slot "top", then bottom is null), plus shoes. Outerwear, bag and one accessory are optional (null if not needed).
Check the whole outfit: color harmony, pattern mix, proportions, formality, occasion, weather practicality (no suede/canvas/sandals in rain, nothing heavy in heat, enough warmth in cold), shoes that suit the outfit, accessories that help rather than clutter, and comfort. Don't reuse the same top or bottom across the 3 outfits if the closet allows.

Reply with ONLY this JSON:
{ "outfits": [ { "top": "id", "bottom": "id or null", "outer": "id or null", "shoes": "id", "bag": "id or null", "accessory": "id or null", "why": "1-2 plain sentences: why this works for the situation" } ] }`;
  const r = await ask(q, { tier: 'default' });
  if (!r.value) {
    return { outfits: rules.outfits.map((x) => ({ ...x, source: 'rules' })), note: rules.note, error: r.offline ? null : r.error, offline: r.offline };
  }
  const byId = new Map(avail.map((g) => [g.id, g]));
  const pick = (id, slot) => { const g = byId.get(id); return g && slotOf(g) === slot ? g : null; };
  const seen = new Set();
  const validated = [];
  let rejected = 0;
  for (const raw of arr(r.value?.outfits).slice(0, 3)) {
    const o = { top: pick(raw.top, 'top'), bottom: pick(raw.bottom, 'bottom'), outer: pick(raw.outer, 'outer'), shoes: pick(raw.shoes, 'shoes'), bag: pick(raw.bag, 'bag'), accessory: pick(raw.accessory, 'accessory') };
    if (isDress(o.top)) o.bottom = null;
    if (anchor) o[slotOf(anchor)] = anchor;
    const k = Object.values(o).map((x) => x?.id || '-').join('|');
    const c = isComplete(o) ? critique(o, ctx) : null;
    const weather = c?.pillars.find((p) => p.id === 'fabric').score ?? 0;
    // Independent validation: complete, uses her real available items, practical, and genuinely decent.
    if (!c || seen.has(k) || weather < 60 || c.overall < GOOD || c.dealBreakers.length) { rejected++; continue; }
    seen.add(k);
    validated.push({ name: describe(o), outfit: o, critique: c, why: str(raw.why, 300) || explain(o, c, ctx), source: 'ai' });
  }
  // Top up from the rules engine (never fewer than 3 when her closet allows)
  for (const x of rules.outfits) {
    if (validated.length >= 3) break;
    const k = Object.values(x.outfit).map((g) => g?.id || '-').join('|');
    if (seen.has(k)) continue;
    seen.add(k);
    validated.push({ ...x, source: 'rules' });
  }
  return {
    outfits: validated,
    provider: r.provider,
    note: [rejected ? `The rules engine replaced ${rejected} AI suggestion${rejected > 1 ? 's' : ''} that didn't pass its checks (incomplete, impractical for the weather, or a weak match).` : '', validated.length < 3 ? rules.note : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' '),
  };
}

// ─────────────────────────────────────────── 4. Inspiration decomposition (photo)
export async function decomposeWithClaude(imageBlob) {
  const prompt = `This is a fashion inspiration photo the user saved (street style or a lookbook).
List the visible clothing pieces of the main outfit. Reply with ONLY this JSON:
{ "title": "3-5 word name for the look", "items": [ { "label": "e.g. 'black turtleneck'", "category": one of ${JSON.stringify(CATS)}, "art": closest shape from ${JSON.stringify(ARTS)}, "color": one of ${JSON.stringify(COLOR_NAMES)} } ] }
At most one item per category except accessories (max 2). Do not describe the person.`;
  const r = await ask(prompt, { images: [imageBlob], tier: 'default' });
  if (!r.value) return { error: r.error, offline: r.offline };
  const items = arr(r.value?.items).map((it) => {
    const category = oneOf(it.category, CATS, null);
    if (!category) return null;
    const color = NAMED_COLORS.find((c) => c.name === it.color) || null;
    return { label: str(it.label, 60) || `${color?.name || ''} ${it.art || category}`.trim(), category, art: oneOf(it.art, ARTS[category], ARTS[category][0]), color: color ? { name: color.name, hex: color.hex } : null };
  }).filter(Boolean);
  if (!items.length) return { error: 'The AI could not find clothing in that image.' };
  return { provider: r.provider, title: str(r.value?.title, 60), items };
}

export { short };


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/ai/tagger.js
====================================================================================================

// Closet onboarding pipeline (prototype).
//  REAL, on-device: resize, simple background removal (border flood-fill), dominant color.
//  MOCK: semantic tags (category, material, warmth…), inferred from filename + shape.
// Production swaps in @imgly/background-removal + an optional AI "tag" Edge Function.

import { nearestNamedColor, rgbToHex } from './color.js';

const wait = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

function loadImage(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
    const img = new Image();
    img.onload = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); resolve(img); };
    img.onerror = reject;
    img.src = url;
  });
}

/** Flood-fill from the borders, clearing pixels close to the background color. */
// Perceptual-ish color distance ("redmean"), cheaper than Lab and much better than plain RGB.
function cdist(r1, g1, b1, r2, g2, b2) {
  const rm = (r1 + r2) / 2, dr = r1 - r2, dg = g1 - g2, db = b1 - b2;
  return Math.sqrt((2 + rm / 256) * dr * dr + 4 * dg * dg + (2 + (255 - rm) / 256) * db * db);
}

/**
 * On-device background removal, tuned for messy real photos (beds, floors, shadows, light gradients):
 *  - learns up to 4 background colors from the photo's border (not one average), so a two-tone floor or a shadow works;
 *  - grows the background from the edges, also following smooth gradients pixel to pixel, but never into a sharp edge;
 *  - softens the outline by one pixel so cut-outs don't look jagged.
 * Garment pixels are never recolored. If this fails, refineCutout() keeps the original photo.
 */
function removeBackground(ctx, w, h, tolerance = 34) {
  const data = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  const px = data.data;
  // 1. border palette: simple k-means (k ≤ 4) on border pixels
  const border = [];
  const take = (x, y) => { const i = (y * w + x) * 4; border.push([px[i], px[i + 1], px[i + 2]]); };
  for (let x = 0; x < w; x += 3) { take(x, 0); take(x, 1); take(x, h - 1); take(x, h - 2); }
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y += 3) { take(0, y); take(1, y); take(w - 1, y); take(w - 2, y); }
  let centers = [border[0], border[(border.length / 4) | 0], border[(border.length / 2) | 0], border[((3 * border.length) / 4) | 0]].map((c) => [...c]);
  for (let it = 0; it < 8; it++) {
    const sum = centers.map(() => [0, 0, 0, 0]);
    for (const c of border) {
      let bi = 0, bd = Infinity;
      centers.forEach((k, j) => { const d = cdist(c[0], c[1], c[2], k[0], k[1], k[2]); if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = j; } });
      const s = sum[bi]; s[0] += c[0]; s[1] += c[1]; s[2] += c[2]; s[3]++;
    }
    centers = sum.filter((s) => s[3] > border.length * 0.04).map((s) => [s[0] / s[3], s[1] / s[3], s[2] / s[3]]);
    if (!centers.length) centers = [border[0]];
  }
  const T = tolerance * 3;       // distance to a learned background color
  const STEP = tolerance * 0.9;  // pixel-to-pixel change allowed while following a gradient
  const near = (i) => centers.some((k) => cdist(px[i], px[i + 1], px[i + 2], k[0], k[1], k[2]) < T);
  // 2. grow from the border
  const bg = new Uint8Array(w * h);
  const stack = [];
  const seed = (p) => { if (!bg[p] && near(p * 4)) { bg[p] = 1; stack.push(p); } };
  for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) { seed(x); seed((h - 1) * w + x); }
  for (let y = 0; y < h; y++) { seed(y * w); seed(y * w + w - 1); }
  while (stack.length) {
    const p = stack.pop();
    const i = p * 4;
    const x = p % w, y = (p / w) | 0;
    const nb = [x > 0 ? p - 1 : -1, x < w - 1 ? p + 1 : -1, y > 0 ? p - w : -1, y < h - 1 ? p + w : -1];
    for (const q of nb) {
      if (q < 0 || bg[q]) continue;
      const j = q * 4;
      const step = cdist(px[i], px[i + 1], px[i + 2], px[j], px[j + 1], px[j + 2]);
      // accept: close to a learned background color, or a gentle continuation of the background (shadow / gradient)
      if (near(j) || (step < STEP && centers.some((k) => cdist(px[j], px[j + 1], px[j + 2], k[0], k[1], k[2]) < T * 2.2))) { bg[q] = 1; stack.push(q); }
    }
  }
  // 3. apply, with a one-pixel soft edge
  for (let p = 0; p < w * h; p++) if (bg[p]) px[p * 4 + 3] = 0;
  for (let p = 0; p < w * h; p++) {
    if (bg[p]) continue;
    const x = p % w, y = (p / w) | 0;
    const edge = (x > 0 && bg[p - 1]) || (x < w - 1 && bg[p + 1]) || (y > 0 && bg[p - w]) || (y < h - 1 && bg[p + w]);
    if (edge) px[p * 4 + 3] = 170;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(data, 0, 0);
  return data;
}

/**
 * Clean up the cut-out without touching the garment's own pixels:
 *  - keep only the largest connected piece (drops crumbs, lint, bits of the background),
 *  - crop to the garment with a small margin, so it fills its box in the flat-lay and mannequin,
 *  - if the cut-out looks wrong (almost nothing or almost everything removed), keep the original photo instead.
 * Returns { canvas, data, cut: 'clean' | 'kept-original', aspect }.
 */
export function refineCutout(canvas, original) {
  const w = canvas.width, h = canvas.height;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  const data = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  const px = data.data;
  const N = w * h;
  const label = new Int32Array(N).fill(-1);
  let best = -1, bestSize = 0, comp = 0;
  const stack = [];
  for (let p0 = 0; p0 < N; p0++) {
    if (label[p0] !== -1 || px[p0 * 4 + 3] === 0) continue;
    let size = 0; stack.push(p0); label[p0] = comp;
    while (stack.length) {
      const p = stack.pop(); size++;
      const x = p % w, y = (p / w) | 0;
      const nb = [x > 0 ? p - 1 : -1, x < w - 1 ? p + 1 : -1, y > 0 ? p - w : -1, y < h - 1 ? p + w : -1];
      for (const q of nb) if (q >= 0 && label[q] === -1 && px[q * 4 + 3] !== 0) { label[q] = comp; stack.push(q); }
    }
    if (size > bestSize) { bestSize = size; best = comp; }
    comp++;
  }
  const coverage = bestSize / N;
  if (best < 0 || coverage < 0.015 || coverage > 0.97) {
    // The cut-out failed (garment too close to the background color, or touching every edge). Never lose the garment.
    ctx.putImageData(original, 0, 0);
    return { canvas, data: original, cut: 'kept-original', aspect: h / w };
  }
  let x0 = w, y0 = h, x1 = 0, y1 = 0;
  for (let p = 0; p < N; p++) {
    if (label[p] === best) { const x = p % w, y = (p / w) | 0; if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x; if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y; }
    else px[p * 4 + 3] = 0;
  }
  ctx.putImageData(data, 0, 0);
  const pad = Math.round(Math.max(x1 - x0, y1 - y0) * 0.03);
  x0 = Math.max(0, x0 - pad); y0 = Math.max(0, y0 - pad); x1 = Math.min(w - 1, x1 + pad); y1 = Math.min(h - 1, y1 + pad);
  const cw = x1 - x0 + 1, ch = y1 - y0 + 1;
  const out = document.createElement('canvas');
  out.width = cw; out.height = ch;
  const octx = out.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  octx.drawImage(canvas, x0, y0, cw, ch, 0, 0, cw, ch);
  return { canvas: out, data: octx.getImageData(0, 0, cw, ch), cut: 'clean', aspect: ch / cw };
}

/** Dominant color of the opaque pixels (tiny k-means, k=3). */
function dominantColor(data) {
  const px = data.data, pts = [];
  for (let i = 0; i < px.length; i += 16) if (px[i + 3] > 200) pts.push([px[i], px[i + 1], px[i + 2]]);
  if (!pts.length) return [128, 128, 128];
  let centers = [pts[0], pts[(pts.length / 2) | 0], pts[pts.length - 1]].map((c) => [...c]);
  let counts = [0, 0, 0];
  for (let iter = 0; iter < 8; iter++) {
    const sums = centers.map(() => [0, 0, 0]);
    counts = [0, 0, 0];
    for (const p of pts) {
      let bi = 0, bd = Infinity;
      centers.forEach((c, k) => { const d = (c[0] - p[0]) ** 2 + (c[1] - p[1]) ** 2 + (c[2] - p[2]) ** 2; if (d < bd) { bd = d; bi = k; } });
      counts[bi]++; sums[bi][0] += p[0]; sums[bi][1] += p[1]; sums[bi][2] += p[2];
    }
    centers = centers.map((c, k) => (counts[k] ? sums[k].map((v) => v / counts[k]) : c));
  }
  return centers[counts.indexOf(Math.max(...counts))];
}

const KEYWORDS = [
  { re: /tee|t-shirt|tshirt/, category: 'top', art: 'tee', material: 'Cotton jersey', weight: 'light', warmth: 1, formality: 1 },
  { re: /turtle/, category: 'top', art: 'turtleneck', material: 'Knit', weight: 'mid', warmth: 3, formality: 3 },
  { re: /shirt|blouse|oxford/, category: 'top', art: 'shirt', material: 'Cotton', weight: 'light', warmth: 2, formality: 3 },
  { re: /sweater|knit|jumper|cardigan|hoodie|sweat/, category: 'top', art: 'knit', material: 'Knit', weight: 'mid', warmth: 3, formality: 2 },
  { re: /jean|denim/, category: 'bottom', art: 'jeans', material: 'Denim', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 2 },
  { re: /short/, category: 'bottom', art: 'shorts', material: 'Cotton', weight: 'light', warmth: 1, formality: 1 },
  { re: /skirt/, category: 'bottom', art: 'skirt', material: 'Woven', weight: 'light', warmth: 1, formality: 3 },
  { re: /pant|trouser|chino|slack/, category: 'bottom', art: 'trousers', material: 'Cotton twill', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 3 },
  { re: /trench|mac|raincoat/, category: 'outer', art: 'trench', material: 'Cotton gabardine', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 4, water: 4, length: 'long' },
  { re: /blazer|suit/, category: 'outer', art: 'blazer', material: 'Wool blend', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 4, length: 'hip' },
  { re: /coat/, category: 'outer', art: 'coat', material: 'Wool blend', weight: 'heavy', warmth: 4, formality: 4, length: 'long' },
  { re: /jacket|puffer|parka/, category: 'outer', art: 'jacket', material: 'Nylon', weight: 'mid', warmth: 3, formality: 2, length: 'hip' },
  { re: /sneaker|trainer|runner/, category: 'shoes', art: 'sneaker', material: 'Leather', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 2, profile: 'sleek' },
  { re: /boot/, category: 'shoes', art: 'boot', material: 'Leather', weight: 'heavy', warmth: 3, formality: 3, water: 4, profile: 'chunky' },
  { re: /loafer|oxford shoe|heel|flat/, category: 'shoes', art: 'loafer', material: 'Leather', weight: 'mid', warmth: 2, formality: 4, profile: 'sleek' },
  { re: /sandal|slide/, category: 'shoes', art: 'sandal', material: 'Leather', weight: 'light', warmth: 1, formality: 1, profile: 'sleek' },
  { re: /bag|tote|purse/, category: 'accessory', art: 'tote', material: 'Canvas', weight: 'mid', warmth: 0, formality: 2 },
  { re: /scarf/, category: 'accessory', art: 'scarf', material: 'Knit', weight: 'mid', warmth: 1, formality: 2 },
];

/** Rough silhouette reading of the cut-out: two legs → trousers, wide shoulders/sleeves → top. */
export function shapeHint(data) {
  const { width: w, height: h, data: px } = data;
  const runsAt = (fy) => {
    const y = Math.min(h - 1, Math.round(h * fy));
    let runs = 0, inRun = false, filled = 0;
    for (let x = 0; x < w; x++) {
      const on = px[(y * w + x) * 4 + 3] > 0;
      if (on) filled++;
      if (on && !inRun) runs++;
      inRun = on;
    }
    return { runs, fill: filled / w };
  };
  const low = [0.8, 0.88, 0.94].map(runsAt);
  const high = runsAt(0.22), mid = runsAt(0.5);
  if (low.filter((r) => r.runs >= 2).length >= 2 && high.runs === 1 && high.fill > 0.5) return 'legs';
  if (mid.runs >= 3 || runsAt(0.4).runs >= 3 || (high.fill >= 0.85 && mid.fill < high.fill)) return 'sleeves';
  return null;
}

function guessFromShape(aspect, shape) {
  if (shape === 'legs') return KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.art === (aspect > 1.2 ? 'trousers' : 'shorts'));
  if (shape === 'sleeves') return KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.art === (aspect > 1.15 ? 'knit' : 'tee'));
  // tall & narrow → bottom; wide & short → shoes; otherwise top
  if (aspect > 1.45) return KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.art === 'trousers');
  if (aspect < 0.7) return KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.art === 'sneaker');
  return KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.art === 'tee');
}

/**
 * Processes one photo. Calls onStep(label) as it goes.
 * Returns a draft garment for the user to confirm.
 */
export async function processUpload(file, onStep = () => {}) {
  onStep('Reading photo');
  const img = await loadImage(file);
  const max = 640;
  const scale = Math.min(1, max / Math.max(img.width, img.height));
  const w = Math.round(img.width * scale), h = Math.round(img.height * scale);
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = w; canvas.height = h;
  const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d', { willReadFrequently: true });
  ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, w, h); // drawing to canvas also drops EXIF/GPS metadata
  await wait(250);

  onStep('Removing background');
  const original = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, w, h);
  removeBackground(ctx, w, h);
  const refined = refineCutout(canvas, original);
  const data = refined.data;
  await wait(350);

  onStep('Reading colors');
  const rgb = dominantColor(data);
  const named = nearestNamedColor(rgb);
  await wait(300);

  onStep('Tagging (category, fabric, season)');
  const fname = file.name.toLowerCase();
  const hint = KEYWORDS.find((k) => k.re.test(fname)) || guessFromShape(refined.aspect, refined.cut === 'clean' ? shapeHint(data) : null);
  await wait(500);

  // Honest fallback: the device only *measures* colors. The type is a guess (from the file name or the outline), and
  // fabric, fit, warmth and formality are NOT inferred: they get neutral placeholders and are flagged for her to check.
  const { re, material: _m, water: _w, ...tags } = hint;
  return {
    id: 'u-' + Date.now().toString(36),
    name: `${named.name.replace(/\b\w/g, (c) => c.toUpperCase())} ${tags.art === 'tee' ? 'T-Shirt' : tags.art.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + tags.art.slice(1)}`,
    colors: [{ name: named.name, hex: rgbToHex(rgb) }],
    pattern: 'solid',
    seasons: ['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter'],
    fit: 'regular',
    water: 1,
    ...tags,
    owned: true,
    uploaded: true,
    image: refined.canvas.toDataURL('image/webp', 0.86),
    cutout: refined.cut,
    material: '',
    tagSource: 'device',
    unverified: ['category', 'art', 'pattern', 'material', 'fit', 'warmth', 'formality', 'water', 'style'],
    confidence: KEYWORDS.some((k) => k.re.test(fname)) ? 0.5 : 0.25,
  };
}

// Header account button + sign-in / sign-up / reset modal.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useAuth, useCloudSync, inClaudePreview } from '../auth.jsx';
import { Modal, useToast } from './common.jsx';

function AuthModal({ onClose, initial = 'signin' }) {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [mode, setMode] = useState(initial); // signin | signup | reset
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState('');
  const [info, setInfo] = useState('');

  async function submit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();
    setErr(''); setInfo(''); setBusy(true);
    try {
      if (mode === 'signin') { await auth.signIn(email, password); toast('Welcome back!'); onClose(); }
      else if (mode === 'signup') {
        const r = await auth.signUp(name, email, password);
        if (r.needsConfirm) setInfo('Almost there! We sent a confirmation link to ' + email + '. Open it, then sign in.');
        else { toast('Account created'); onClose(); }
      } else { await auth.resetPassword(email); setInfo('If that email has an account, a reset link is on its way.'); }
    } catch (x) { setErr(x.message); }
    setBusy(false);
  }

  const title = mode === 'signin' ? 'Sign in' : mode === 'signup' ? 'Create your account' : 'Reset your password';
  return (
    <Modal title={title} onClose={onClose}>
      <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
        {mode === 'reset' ? 'Enter your email and we\'ll send you a link.' : 'Save your closet, looks and lookbooks to your account, and see them on every device.'}
      </p>
      {inClaudePreview && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)' }}>Sign-in works on the live site. This claude.ai preview can't reach the account server, so your closet stays in this browser here.</p>}
      <form onSubmit={submit}>
        {mode === 'signup' && <div className="field"><label className="label" htmlFor="au-name">Name</label><input id="au-name" className="input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} autoComplete="name" /></div>}
        <div className="field"><label className="label" htmlFor="au-email">Email</label><input id="au-email" className="input" type="email" required value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} autoComplete="email" autoFocus /></div>
        {mode !== 'reset' && (
          <div className="field"><label className="label" htmlFor="au-pw">Password</label>
            <input id="au-pw" className="input" type="password" required minLength={6} value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} autoComplete={mode === 'signup' ? 'new-password' : 'current-password'} />
          </div>
        )}
        {err && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }}>{err}</p>}
        {info && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--good)' }}>{info}</p>}
        <button className="btn primary block" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'One moment…' : mode === 'signin' ? 'Sign in' : mode === 'signup' ? 'Create account' : 'Send reset link'}</button>
      </form>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 10, marginTop: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        {mode === 'signin' ? (
          <>
            <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={() => { setMode('signup'); setErr(''); setInfo(''); }}>New here? Create an account</button>
            <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={() => { setMode('reset'); setErr(''); setInfo(''); }}>Forgot password?</button>
          </>
        ) : (
          <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={() => { setMode('signin'); setErr(''); setInfo(''); }}>← Back to sign in</button>
        )}
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function NewPasswordModal({ onClose }) {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [pw, setPw] = useState('');
  const [err, setErr] = useState('');
  return (
    <Modal title="Choose a new password" onClose={onClose}>
      <form onSubmit={async (e) => { e.preventDefault(); try { await auth.updatePassword(pw); toast('Password updated'); onClose(); } catch (x) { setErr(x.message); } }}>
        <div className="field"><label className="label" htmlFor="np">New password</label><input id="np" className="input" type="password" minLength={6} required value={pw} onChange={(e) => setPw(e.target.value)} autoFocus /></div>
        {err && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }}>{err}</p>}
        <button className="btn primary block">Save password</button>
      </form>
    </Modal>
  );
}

const ago = (d) => { if (!d) return ''; const s = Math.round((Date.now() - d) / 1000); return s < 10 ? 'just now' : s < 60 ? `${s}s ago` : `${Math.round(s / 60)} min ago`; };

export default function Account() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const sync = useCloudSync();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(null); // 'signin' | 'signup' | null
  const [menu, setMenu] = useState(false);
  const ref = useRef();
  const [, tick] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => { const t = setInterval(() => tick((n) => n + 1), 30000); return () => clearInterval(t); }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menu) return;
    const close = (e) => { if (!ref.current?.contains(e.target)) setMenu(false); };
    document.addEventListener('mousedown', close);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('mousedown', close);
  }, [menu]);

  if (!auth?.configured) return null;
  const u = auth.user;
  const name = u?.user_metadata?.name || u?.email?.split('@')[0] || '';
  const syncText = { loading: 'Loading your closet…', saving: 'Saving…', synced: `☁️ Synced ${ago(sync.lastSaved)}`, error: 'Not synced', offline: 'Offline: saved on this device only', conflict: 'Changed on another device', 'needs-setup': 'Cloud not set up', off: '' }[sync.status];

  return (
    <div className="account" ref={ref}>
      {!u ? (
        <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setOpen('signin')}>Sign in</button>
      ) : (
        <button className="avatar" onClick={() => setMenu(!menu)} aria-expanded={menu} aria-label="Account menu" title={syncText}>
          {name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
          <i className={'sync-dot ' + sync.status} />
        </button>
      )}
      {menu && u && (
        <div className="account-menu" role="menu">
          <div className="small" style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{name}</div>
          <div className="tiny">{u.email}</div>
          <div className="small" style={{ margin: '10px 0', color: sync.status === 'error' || sync.status === 'needs-setup' || sync.status === 'offline' || sync.status === 'conflict' ? 'var(--warn)' : 'var(--ink-2)' }}>{syncText}</div>
          {sync.message && <div className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)', marginBottom: 8 }}>{sync.message}</div>}
          <button className="btn sm block" onClick={async () => { setMenu(false); await auth.signOut(); toast('Signed out'); }}>Sign out</button>
        </div>
      )}
      {sync.conflict && (
        <div className="sync-banner" role="alertdialog" aria-label="Closet changed on another device">
          <div><b>Your closet was changed on another device.</b><span className="small"> Nothing has been overwritten. Which version do you want to keep?</span></div>
          <div className="sync-banner-actions">
            <button className="btn sm primary" onClick={sync.useCloudVersion}>Use the other device's version</button>
            <button className="btn sm" onClick={sync.keepThisVersion}>Keep this device's version</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {sync.status === 'offline' && !menu && <div className="sync-pill" role="status">Offline · your changes are kept on this device</div>}
      {open && <AuthModal initial={open} onClose={() => setOpen(null)} />}
      {auth.recovery && u && <NewPasswordModal onClose={() => auth.endRecovery()} />}
    </div>
  );
}

// Realistic AI photos (Puter). Always labelled as AI, never replace the user's own photos.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useClaudeStatus, puterSignIn } from '../ai/claude.js';
import { makeImage, piecePrompt, outfitPrompt, referenceSheet } from '../ai/imagine.js';
import { usePhotos } from '../data/photos.js';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { useToast } from './common.jsx';

const SLOT_KEYS = ['outer', 'top', 'bottom', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'];
export const lookKey = (pieces) => pieces.map((p) => p.id).sort().join('|');

function errorText(e) {
  if (e?.code === 'not_signed_in') return 'Sign in to Puter (free) to make AI photos.';
  if (e?.code === 'rate_limited') return e.serverMessage || 'Your free Puter allowance for AI images is used up for now.';
  return 'The AI couldn\'t make a photo just now. Try again in a moment.';
}

/** Handles Puter sign-in (from the click itself, so the pop-up is allowed) and errors. */
export function useAiMaker() {
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const run = async (job) => {
    if (busy) return null;
    try { if (ai.status !== 'ready') await puterSignIn(); }
    catch { toast('Sign in to Puter (free) to make AI photos.'); return null; }
    setBusy(true);
    try { return await job(); }
    catch (e) { toast(errorText(e)); return null; }
    finally { setBusy(false); }
  };
  return { busy, run, status: ai.status };
}

/** For one piece without a photo of its own: make / replace / remove its AI sample photo. */
export function PieceAiPhoto({ item }) {
  const { dispatch } = useStore();
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const { busy, run } = useAiMaker();
  if (item.image) return null; // her own photo always wins; nothing to make
  const slot = photos.garments[item.id];
  const make = () => run(async () => {
    const { image } = await makeImage(piecePrompt(item));
    dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: item.id, aiImage: image } });
  });
  return (
    <div className="ai-piece">
      {busy ? <div className="ai-making" style={{ maxWidth: 220 }}>Making a realistic photo from this piece's details…</div> : null}
      <div className="row" style={{ gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', marginTop: 6 }}>
        <button className="btn sm" disabled={busy} onClick={make}>✦ {item.aiImage || slot ? 'Make a new AI photo' : 'Make a realistic AI photo'}</button>
        {item.aiImage && <button className="btn sm ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: item.id, aiImage: null } })}>Remove AI photo</button>}
      </div>
      <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>AI photos are made from this piece's details (name, colors, fabric) with Puter, so they show <i>a</i> piece like it, not your actual one. They're labelled "AI photo". Adding your own photo replaces it.</p>
    </div>
  );
}

/** "Make AI photos for the N pieces without a photo" (one at a time, can be stopped). */
export function BulkAiPhotos({ items }) {
  const { dispatch } = useStore();
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const { busy, run } = useAiMaker();
  const [done, setDone] = useState(0);
  const stop = useRef(false);
  const todo = items.filter((g) => !g.image && !g.aiImage && !photos.garments[g.id]);
  if (!todo.length && !busy) return null;
  const go = () => run(async () => {
    stop.current = false; setDone(0);
    for (const g of todo) {
      if (stop.current) break;
      const { image } = await makeImage(piecePrompt(g));
      dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: g.id, aiImage: image } });
      setDone((n) => n + 1);
    }
  });
  return (
    <div className="ai-bulk tiny">
      {busy
        ? <>Making AI photos… {done} of {todo.length + done} <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => { stop.current = true; }}>Stop</button></>
        : <><button className="btn sm" onClick={go}>✦ Make AI photos for {todo.length} piece{todo.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} without a photo</button> <span>Realistic sample photos from each piece's details, labelled "AI photo". Uses your Puter account.</span></>}
    </div>
  );
}

/** A realistic AI photo of a whole outfit. `pieces` = the outfit's garments; `outfitId` = saved outfit (keeps the photo). */
export function OutfitAiLook({ pieces, outfitId, saved }) {
  const { dispatch } = useStore();
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const { busy, run } = useAiMaker();
  const key = lookKey(pieces);
  const [local, setLocal] = useState(null);
  const look = (saved?.key === key && saved) || (local?.key === key && local) || null;
  // Made before she saved the outfit? Keep it with the saved outfit.
  useEffect(() => { if (outfitId && local?.key === key && !saved) dispatch({ type: 'SET_OUTFIT_LOOK', id: outfitId, look: local }); }, [outfitId]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  if (!pieces.length) return null;
  const make = () => run(async () => {
    const own = pieces.map((p) => p.image || null);
    const anyOwn = own.some(Boolean);
    const reference = anyOwn ? await referenceSheet(pieces.map((p) => p.image || photos.garments[p.id] || p.aiImage)) : null;
    const r = await makeImage(outfitPrompt(pieces, { withReference: !!reference }), { reference });
    const next = { key, image: r.image, usedReference: r.usedReference, at: Date.now() };
    setLocal(next);
    if (outfitId) dispatch({ type: 'SET_OUTFIT_LOOK', id: outfitId, look: next });
  });
  return (
    <div className="ai-look-wrap">
      {busy && <div className="ai-making">Making a realistic photo of this outfit… (about 10–30 seconds)</div>}
      {!busy && look && (
        <figure className="ai-look">
          <img src={look.image} alt="AI-made photo of this outfit" />
          <span className="ai-tag">✦ AI impression</span>
          <figcaption className="ai-look-note">
            Made by AI from {look.usedReference ? 'your photos and details' : 'the details'} of these pieces. Colors, patterns and fit can differ from your real clothes, and it isn't you.
          </figcaption>
        </figure>
      )}
      <div className="row" style={{ gap: 8, marginTop: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <button className="btn sm" disabled={busy} onClick={make}>✦ {look ? 'Another AI photo' : 'Realistic photo (AI)'}</button>
        {look && <button className="btn sm ghost" disabled={busy} onClick={() => { setLocal(null); if (outfitId) dispatch({ type: 'SET_OUTFIT_LOOK', id: outfitId, look: null }); }}>Remove</button>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export const outfitPieces = (resolved) => SLOT_KEYS.map((k) => resolved?.[k]).filter(Boolean);

import { OutfitAiLook, outfitPieces } from './AiPhoto.jsx';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
// Building blocks for "choose → get outfits → compare → save".
import { useState } from 'react';
import Stage from './Stage.jsx';
import { PieceList, ScoreBadge, HeartButton, Verdict, WardrobePicker } from './common.jsx';
import { OCCASIONS, STYLES, SLOTS } from '../data/closet.js';
import { WEATHER_CHOICES, PREF_CHOICES, buildContext } from '../ai/context.js';
import { alternatives } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { critique } from '../ai/critic.js';
import { explain, describe, vibeOf } from '../ai/generator.js';

export const DEFAULT_CHOICE = { occasion: 'everyday', style: null, weather: 'warm', prefs: [], text: '' };
export const contextOf = (choice) => buildContext(choice, OCCASIONS);
export const toSlots = (o) => Object.fromEntries(SLOTS.map((k) => [k, o[k]?.id || null]));

/** Occasion, style, weather, preferences, optional note. Every choice has a sensible default. */
export function ContextPicker({ value, onChange, compact = false, showText = true }) {
  const set = (k, v) => onChange({ ...value, [k]: v });
  const togglePref = (id) => set('prefs', value.prefs.includes(id) ? value.prefs.filter((p) => p !== id) : [...value.prefs, id]);
  return (
    <div className={'ctx-picker' + (compact ? ' compact' : '')}>
      <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Occasion</span>
        <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Occasion">
          {OCCASIONS.map((o) => <button key={o.id} className={'chip' + (value.occasion === o.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('occasion', o.id)}>{o.label}</button>)}
        </div></div>
      <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Weather</span>
        <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Weather">
          {WEATHER_CHOICES.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip' + (value.weather === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('weather', w.id)}>{w.label}</button>)}
        </div></div>
      <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Style</span>
        <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Style">
          <button className={'chip' + (!value.style ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('style', null)}>Any</button>
          {STYLES.map((s) => <button key={s.id} className={'chip' + (value.style === s.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('style', s.id)}>{s.label}</button>)}
        </div></div>
      {!compact && (
        <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Preferences</span>
          <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Preferences">
            {PREF_CHOICES.map((p) => <button key={p.id} className={'chip' + (value.prefs.includes(p.id) ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => togglePref(p.id)}>{p.label}</button>)}
          </div></div>
      )}
      {showText && !compact && (
        <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Anything else? <span className="muted">(optional)</span></span>
          <input className="input" value={value.text} onChange={(e) => set('text', e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. outdoor garden wedding, 18°C · lots of walking" aria-label="Extra details" />
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/** Strengths and weaknesses pulled from the critique, for cards and comparison. */
export function strengthsOf(c, n = 3) {
  return c.pillars.slice().sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score).flatMap((p) => p.pros).slice(0, n);
}
export function weaknessesOf(c, n = 3) {
  return c.pillars.slice().sort((a, b) => a.score - b.score).flatMap((p) => p.cons).slice(0, n);
}
export function suitability(c, ctx) {
  const occ = c.pillars.find((p) => p.id === 'occasion')?.score ?? c.overall;
  const weather = c.pillars.find((p) => p.id === 'fabric')?.score ?? c.overall;
  const word = (s) => (s >= 85 ? 'Excellent' : s >= 72 ? 'Good' : s >= 60 ? 'Fair' : 'Weak');
  return { occasion: `${word(occ)} for ${ctx.occasionLabel.toLowerCase()}`, weather: `${word(weather)} for ${ctx.weather.toLowerCase()} weather (${ctx.tempC}°C)` };
}

const SOURCE = { ai: 'Picked by Gemini · checked by the rules engine', rules: 'Picked by the rules engine' };

/** One suggested outfit with explanation, alternatives and save. */
export function OutfitCard({ result, ctx, pool, index, child = false, onReplace, onSave, onOpen, onWore, saved, savedId }) {
  const savedLook = useStore().state.outfits.find((o) => o.id === savedId)?.aiLook || null;
  const [wore, setWore] = useState(false);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('flatlay');
  const [showAlts, setShowAlts] = useState(false);
  const alts = showAlts ? alternatives(result.outfit, ctx, pool) : [];
  const c = result.critique;
  return (
    <article className="outfit-card" aria-label={`Outfit ${index + 1}`}>
      <div className="outfit-card-stage">
        <Stage outfit={result.outfit} mode={mode} compact child={child} />
        <div className="seg mini-seg">
          <button className={mode === 'flatlay' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setMode('flatlay')}>Flat-lay</button>
          <button className={mode === 'mannequin' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setMode('mannequin')}>Mannequin</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="outfit-card-body">
        <div className="outfit-card-head">
          <div>
            <div className="tiny">Outfit {index + 1}{result.label ? ` · ${result.label}` : ''}{result.vibe || result.outfit ? ` · ${result.vibe || vibeOf(result.outfit)}` : ''}</div>
            <h3 className="h3">{result.name}</h3>
          </div>
          <ScoreBadge score={c.overall} />
        </div>
        <Verdict critique={c} />
        {Object.values(result.outfit).some((g) => g?.unverified?.some((k) => ['category', 'art', 'warmth', 'formality', 'material', 'water'].includes(k))) && (
          <p className="tiny unchecked-note">Uses pieces whose details you haven't checked yet, so this verdict is less certain. Check them in My Closet.</p>
        )}
        {result.source && <span className={'source-tag ' + result.source}>{SOURCE[result.source] || 'Edited by you'}</span>}
        {result.critique.dealBreakers?.length > 0 && <span className="source-tag weak">Weak option · {result.critique.dealBreakers[0]}</span>}
        <p className="small why">{result.why}</p>
        <PieceList outfit={result.outfit} />
        <div className="outfit-card-actions">
          <HeartButton saved={saved} onSave={onSave} />
          <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setShowAlts(!showAlts)} aria-expanded={showAlts}>{showAlts ? 'Hide changes' : 'Improve or change'}</button>
          {onWore && <button className="btn sm ghost" disabled={wore} onClick={() => { onWore(); setWore(true); }}>{wore ? '✓ Logged' : 'I wore this'}</button>}
          {onOpen && <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={onOpen}>Open in builder</button>}
        </div>
        {savedId && <WardrobePicker outfitId={savedId} compact />}
        <OutfitAiLook pieces={outfitPieces(result.outfit)} outfitId={savedId} saved={savedId ? savedLook : null} />
        {showAlts && (
          <div className="alts">
            {alts.length === 0 && <p className="tiny">No other combinations from your available clothes improve or vary this one.</p>}
            {alts.map((a) => (
              <button key={a.id} className="alt" onClick={() => { onReplace(withCritique(a.outfit, ctx, result)); setShowAlts(false); }}>
                <span>{a.label}</span>
                <span className={'tiny ' + (a.delta >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down')}>{a.score} ({a.delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{a.delta})</span>
              </button>
            ))}
            <p className="tiny">Only clothes in this closet that are available are used.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

export function withCritique(outfit, ctx, prev = {}) {
  const c = critique(outfit, ctx);
  return { ...prev, name: describe(outfit), outfit, critique: c, why: explain(outfit, c, ctx), source: prev.source === 'ai' ? 'edited' : prev.source };
}

/** Side by side: previews, items, explanation, strengths, weaknesses, occasion suitability. */
export function Compare({ results, ctx, child = false, onPick, onClose, savedIdx = [] }) {
  const best = results.reduce((b, r, i) => (r.critique.overall > results[b].critique.overall ? i : b), 0);
  return (
    <section className="compare" aria-label="Compare outfits">
      <div className="compare-head">
        <h2 className="h2">Compare side by side</h2>
        <button className="btn sm" onClick={onClose}>Back to cards</button>
      </div>
      <div className="compare-grid" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${results.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}>
        {results.map((r, i) => {
          const s = suitability(r.critique, ctx);
          return (
            <div key={i} className={'compare-col' + (i === best ? ' best' : '')}>
              <div className="tiny">Outfit {i + 1}{i === best ? ' · highest score' : ''}</div>
              <h3 className="h3">{r.name}</h3>
              <Stage outfit={r.outfit} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
              <div className="compare-score"><ScoreBadge score={r.critique.overall} /></div>
              <Verdict critique={r.critique} />
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Pieces</span><PieceList outfit={r.outfit} /></div>
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Why it works</span><p className="small">{r.why}</p></div>
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Strengths</span>
                <ul className="mini-list pro">{strengthsOf(r.critique).map((t, k) => <li key={k}>{t}</li>)}</ul></div>
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Weaknesses</span>
                {weaknessesOf(r.critique).length ? <ul className="mini-list con">{weaknessesOf(r.critique).map((t, k) => <li key={k}>{t}</li>)}</ul> : <p className="tiny">Nothing significant.</p>}</div>
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Suitability</span><p className="small">{s.occasion}<br />{s.weather}</p></div>
              <div className="compare-sec"><span className="label">Pillar scores</span>
                {r.critique.pillars.map((p) => (
                  <div key={p.id} className="mini-bar"><span className="tiny">{p.label}</span><span className={'bar' + (p.score < 65 ? ' low' : '')}><i style={{ width: p.score + '%' }} /></span><span className="tiny">{p.score}</span></div>
                ))}</div>
              <button className={'btn block ' + (savedIdx.includes(i) ? '' : 'accent')} disabled={savedIdx.includes(i)} onClick={() => onPick(i)}>{savedIdx.includes(i) ? '♥ Saved as favorite' : '♥ This one'}</button>
            </div>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

import { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import GarmentArt, { photoOf } from './GarmentArt.jsx';
import { usePhotos } from '../data/photos.js';
import Stage from './Stage.jsx';
import { CATEGORY_LABEL } from '../data/closet.js';
import { verdictOf } from '../ai/critic.js';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';

const STATUS_FLAG = { laundry: 'In laundry', repair: 'Needs repair', stored: 'Stored' };

export function ProductCard({ item, onClick, action, flag, place }) {
  const meta = [item.material, item.weight && `${item.weight[0].toUpperCase()}${item.weight.slice(1)} weight`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const isAi = photoOf(item, usePhotos()).ai;
  return (
    <div className="card">
      <button className="card" onClick={onClick} aria-label={`Open ${item.name}`}>
        <div className="card-stage">
          <GarmentArt item={item} />
          {item.demo && <span className="card-flag basic">Demo</span>}
          {isAi && <span className="card-flag ai" title="AI-made sample photo, not a photo of the real piece">AI photo</span>}
          {item.status && item.status !== 'available' && <span className="card-flag status">{STATUS_FLAG[item.status]}</span>}
          {item.unverified?.length > 0 && <span className="card-flag check" title="Some details are guesses">Check details</span>}
          {flag && <span className="card-flag new">{flag}</span>}
        </div>
        <div className="card-body">
          <div className="tiny">{(CATEGORY_LABEL[item.category] || item.category).toUpperCase()}</div>
          <div className="card-name">{item.name}</div>
          <div className="card-meta">{meta}</div>
          {place && <div className="card-place">⌂ {place}</div>}
          <div className="swatches">{item.colors.map((c) => <span key={c.name} className="swatch" style={{ background: c.hex }} title={c.name} />)}</div>
        </div>
      </button>
      {action && <div className="card-hover">{action}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Meter({ value, max = 5 }) {
  return <span className="meter" role="img" aria-label={`${value} of ${max}`}>{Array.from({ length: max }, (_, i) => <i key={i} className={i < value ? 'on' : ''} />)}</span>;
}

export function Modal({ title, onClose, children, wide }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    const k = (e) => e.key === 'Escape' && onClose();
    window.addEventListener('keydown', k);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', k);
  }, [onClose]);
  return (
    <div className="overlay" onMouseDown={(e) => e.target === e.currentTarget && onClose()}>
      <div className={'modal' + (wide ? ' wide' : '')} role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-label={title}>
        <div className="modal-head"><h2 className="h2">{title}</h2><button className="close" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close">×</button></div>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function OutfitThumb({ outfit, resolve, compactStage = true, child = false }) {
  const o = resolve(outfit.slots);
  return <Stage outfit={o} mode={outfit.stage?.mode || 'flatlay'} sceneId={outfit.stage?.scene} compact={compactStage} child={child} />;
}

/** Plain list of an outfit's pieces. */
export function PieceList({ outfit }) {
  const rows = [['top', outfit.top?.category === 'dress' ? 'Dress' : 'Top'], ['bottom', 'Bottom'], ['outer', 'Outerwear'], ['shoes', 'Shoes'], ['bag', 'Bag'], ['accessory', 'Accessory']];
  return (
    <ul className="piece-list">
      {rows.filter(([k]) => outfit[k]).map(([k, l]) => <li key={k}><span>{l}</span><span>{outfit[k].name}</span></li>)}
    </ul>
  );
}

const ToastCtx = createContext(() => {});
export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
  const [msg, setMsg] = useState(null);
  const show = useCallback((m) => { setMsg(m); clearTimeout(window.__toastT); window.__toastT = setTimeout(() => setMsg(null), 2600); }, []);
  return <ToastCtx.Provider value={show}>{children}{msg && <div className="toast" role="status">{msg}</div>}</ToastCtx.Provider>;
}
export const useToast = () => useContext(ToastCtx);

/** Click once to arm, again to confirm (no browser dialogs). */
export function ConfirmButton({ label, confirmLabel = 'Click again to confirm', onConfirm, className = 'btn ghost sm', style }) {
  const [armed, setArmed] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => { if (!armed) return; const t = setTimeout(() => setArmed(false), 3500); return () => clearTimeout(t); }, [armed]);
  return <button className={className} style={{ ...style, ...(armed ? { color: 'var(--accent)' } : {}) }} onClick={() => (armed ? (setArmed(false), onConfirm()) : setArmed(true))}>{armed ? confirmLabel : label}</button>;
}

/** Score that counts up when it first appears (instantly if the user prefers reduced motion). */
export function ScoreBadge({ score }) {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState(score);
  const first = useRef(true);
  useEffect(() => {
    const reduce = typeof window !== 'undefined' && window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (reduce || typeof score !== 'number') { setShown(score); return; }
    const from = first.current ? Math.max(0, score - 40) : shown;
    first.current = false;
    const t0 = performance.now(), dur = 700;
    let raf;
    const step = (t) => {
      const k = Math.min(1, (t - t0) / dur);
      setShown(Math.round(from + (score - from) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 3))));
      if (k < 1) raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);
    };
    raf = requestAnimationFrame(step);
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf);
  }, [score]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const tier = score >= 85 ? 'great' : score >= 72 ? 'good' : 'fair';
  return (
    <span className={'score-ring ' + tier} role="img" aria-label={`Score ${score} out of 100`}>
      <svg viewBox="0 0 36 36" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="15.5" className="track" /><circle cx="18" cy="18" r="15.5" className="arc" style={{ strokeDasharray: `${(shown / 100) * 97.4} 97.4` }} /></svg>
      <b>{shown}</b>
    </span>
  );
}

/** The honest verdict: a plain label and the one reason that matters most. */
export function Verdict({ critique, compact = false }) {
  if (!critique) return null;
  const v = verdictOf(critique);
  return (
    <div className={'verdict ' + v.tone}>
      <span className="verdict-label">{v.label}</span>
      {!compact && <span className="verdict-line">{v.line}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

export const WARDROBE_IDEAS = ['Work', 'Home', 'Going out', 'Church', 'Weekend'];

/** Put a saved outfit into one of this person's wardrobes (Work, Home…), or start one. */
export function WardrobePicker({ outfitId, compact = false }) {
  const { state, dispatch, profile } = useStore();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const mine = state.wardrobes.filter((w) => w.profileId === profile.id);
  const outfit = state.outfits.find((o) => o.id === outfitId);
  if (!outfit) return null;
  const set = (id) => dispatch({ type: 'SET_OUTFIT_WARDROBE', id: outfitId, wardrobe: outfit.wardrobe === id ? null : id });
  const create = (n) => {
    const clean = n.trim(); if (!clean) return;
    const existing = mine.find((w) => w.name.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase());
    const id = existing?.id || 'wr-' + Date.now().toString(36);
    if (!existing) dispatch({ type: 'ADD_WARDROBE', wardrobe: { id, name: clean, profileId: profile.id } });
    dispatch({ type: 'SET_OUTFIT_WARDROBE', id: outfitId, wardrobe: id });
    setAdding(false); setName('');
  };
  const ideas = WARDROBE_IDEAS.filter((n) => !mine.some((w) => w.name.toLowerCase() === n.toLowerCase())).slice(0, mine.length ? 0 : 3);
  return (
    <div className={'wardrobe-picker' + (compact ? ' compact' : '')}>
      <span className="tiny">Wardrobe:</span>
      {mine.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip sm' + (outfit.wardrobe === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set(w.id)} aria-pressed={outfit.wardrobe === w.id}>{w.name}</button>)}
      {ideas.map((n) => <button key={n} className="chip sm ghost-chip" onClick={() => create(n)}>+ {n}</button>)}
      {adding ? (
        <form className="wardrobe-new" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); create(name); }}>
          <input className="input" autoFocus value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Office" aria-label="New wardrobe name" />
          <button className="btn sm primary" type="submit">Add</button>
        </form>
      ) : <button className="chip sm ghost-chip" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>+ New</button>}
    </div>
  );
}

/** A heart button that bursts when it saves. */
export function HeartButton({ saved, onSave, label = 'Save', savedLabel = 'Saved', className = 'btn sm accent' }) {
  const [burst, setBurst] = useState(0);
  return (
    <button className={className + ' heart-btn' + (saved ? ' is-saved' : '')} disabled={saved} onClick={() => { onSave(); setBurst((b) => b + 1); }}>
      <span className="heart" aria-hidden="true">{saved ? '♥' : '♡'}</span> {saved ? savedLabel : label}
      {burst > 0 && <span key={burst} className="burst" aria-hidden="true">{Array.from({ length: 8 }, (_, i) => <i key={i} style={{ '--a': `${i * 45}deg` }} />)}</span>}
    </button>
  );
}

// Realistic product-style garment renders (SVG, 200×250 viewBox).
// Each piece gets studio lighting (gradient), a fabric texture matched to its material
// (procedural noise), soft fold shading, stitching and a soft contact shadow.
// Uploaded garments show their real cut-out photo instead.
import { useId } from 'react';

import { usePhotos, markBroken } from '../data/photos.js';
