// Fabric look by material/shape: noise frequency, contrast, sheen
export function fabricFor(item) {
  const m = (item.material || '').toLowerCase();
  if (/leather|patent/.test(m) && !/suede/.test(m)) return { f: '0.035', k: 0.05, sheen: 0.5 };
  if (/suede/.test(m)) return { f: '1.6', k: 0.2, sheen: 0 };
  if (/rubber|nylon|down|waterproof|polyester/.test(m)) return { f: '0.04', k: 0.05, sheen: 0.35 };
  if (/denim/.test(m) || item.art === 'jeans') return { f: '0.9 0.5', k: 0.28, sheen: 0.05, twill: true };
  if (/knit|lambswool|cable|souffle|merino|cashmere|fleece|acrylic/.test(m) || item.art === 'knit') return { f: '0.06 0.9', k: 0.22, sheen: 0.05 };
  if (/linen/.test(m)) return { f: '1.1 0.35', k: 0.2, sheen: 0.05 };
  if (/wool|hopsack|gabardine|tweed/.test(m)) return { f: '1.2', k: 0.16, sheen: 0.08 };
  if (/canvas/.test(m)) return { f: '1.4 1.0', k: 0.18, sheen: 0.04 };
  return { f: '1.5', k: 0.12, sheen: 0.1 }; // cotton & default
}

export function Defs({ id, c, fab }) {
  const k = fab.k;
  return (
    <defs>
      <linearGradient id={`${id}g`} x1="0.15" y1="0" x2="0.85" y2="1">
        <stop offset="0" stopColor={shade(c, 0.16)} />
        <stop offset="0.5" stopColor={c} />
        <stop offset="1" stopColor={shade(c, -0.22)} />
      </linearGradient>
      <radialGradient id={`${id}h`} cx="0.38" cy="0.3" r="0.55">
        <stop offset="0" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity={0.18 + fab.sheen * 0.3} />
        <stop offset="1" stopColor="#fff" stopOpacity="0" />
      </radialGradient>
      <filter id={`${id}t`} x="0" y="0" width="100%" height="100%">
        <feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency={fab.f} numOctaves="2" seed="7" result="n" />
        <feColorMatrix in="n" type="saturate" values="0" result="m" />
        <feComponentTransfer in="m" result="s">
          <feFuncR type="linear" slope={k * 2} intercept={1 - k} />
          <feFuncG type="linear" slope={k * 2} intercept={1 - k} />
          <feFuncB type="linear" slope={k * 2} intercept={1 - k} />
        </feComponentTransfer>
        <feBlend in="SourceGraphic" in2="s" mode="multiply" result="b" />
        <feComposite in="b" in2="SourceGraphic" operator="in" />
      </filter>
      <filter id={`${id}b`} x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="140%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="3.2" /></filter>
      <filter id={`${id}s`} x="-30%" y="-30%" width="160%" height="160%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="6" /></filter>
      {fab.twill && (
        <pattern id={`${id}w`} width="3" height="3" patternUnits="userSpaceOnUse" patternTransform="rotate(-30)">
          <rect width="3" height="1.2" fill="#fff" opacity=".10" />
        </pattern>
      )}
    </defs>
  );
}

/** A fabric body: lit fill + texture + folds clipped to the shape + subtle edge. */
export function Body({ id, d, c, fab, folds = [], lights = [], children, part = '' }) {
  return (
    <g>
      <clipPath id={`${id}c${part}`}><path d={d} /></clipPath>
      <path d={d} fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} />
      <g clipPath={`url(#${id}c${part})`}>
        {fab.twill && <path d={d} fill={`url(#${id}w)`} />}
        <g filter={`url(#${id}b)`}>
          {folds.map((f, i) => <path key={'f' + i} d={f} fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.5)} strokeOpacity=".2" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" />)}
          {lights.map((f, i) => <path key={'l' + i} d={f} fill="none" stroke="#fff" strokeOpacity=".15" strokeWidth="4.5" strokeLinecap="round" />)}
        </g>
        <path d={d} fill={`url(#${id}h)`} />
        {children}
      </g>
      <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.45)} strokeOpacity=".35" strokeWidth="0.8" />
    </g>
  );
}

const stitch = (c, d, w = 0.7) => <path d={d} fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".55" strokeWidth={w} strokeDasharray="2.2 1.6" />;

function Top({ id, item, c, fab }) {
  const art = item.art;
  const d = art === 'tee' ? P.tee : art === 'knit' ? P.knit : P.long;
  const folds = art === 'tee'
    ? ['M66 96 Q78 130 70 170', 'M134 96 Q124 135 130 175']
    : ['M64 118 Q76 150 68 196', 'M136 118 Q126 150 132 196', 'M48 120 Q54 150 46 180', 'M152 120 Q146 150 154 180', 'M90 160 Q100 170 112 160'];
  const lights = ['M96 70 Q100 120 98 190', 'M58 80 Q54 110 50 150'];
  const c2 = item.colors?.[1]?.hex;
  const ribbed = art === 'knit' || art === 'turtleneck' || art === 'stripe';
  return (
    <g>
      <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={folds} lights={lights}>
        {art === 'stripe' && Array.from({ length: 14 }, (_, i) => (
          <path key={i} d={`M20 ${58 + i * 11} Q100 ${61 + i * 11} 180 ${58 + i * 11}`} stroke={c2 || '#1F2A44'} strokeWidth="4.2" fill="none" opacity=".92" />
        ))}
        {art === 'knit' && (
          <g opacity=".5">
            {[76, 88, 100, 112, 124].map((x) => <path key={x} d={`M${x} 60 C${x + 5} 80 ${x - 5} 100 ${x} 120 C${x + 5} 140 ${x - 5} 160 ${x} 200`} stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeWidth="3" fill="none" />)}
            {[76, 88, 100, 112, 124].map((x) => <path key={'h' + x} d={`M${x + 2} 60 C${x + 7} 80 ${x - 3} 100 ${x + 2} 120 C${x + 7} 140 ${x - 3} 160 ${x + 2} 200`} stroke={shade(c, 0.25)} strokeWidth="1.4" fill="none" />)}
          </g>
        )}
        {ribbed && <rect x="58" y="200" width="84" height="14" fill={shade(c, -0.1)} opacity=".7" />}
        {ribbed && Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, i) => <line key={i} x1={60 + i * 4} y1="201" x2={60 + i * 4} y2="213" stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeOpacity=".35" strokeWidth="1" />)}
        {art === 'tee' && stitch(c, 'M62.5 203 L138 203')}
        {art === 'tee' && stitch(c, 'M45 75 L57 87 M155 75 L143 87')}
      </Body>
      {(art === 'tee' || art === 'stripe' || art === 'knit') && (
        <path d="M90 36.5 C94 47 106 47 110 36.5" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, art === 'tee' ? -0.18 : -0.25)} strokeWidth={art === 'knit' ? 5 : 3} strokeLinecap="round" />
      )}
      {art === 'turtleneck' && (
        <g>
          <path d="M86 18 C92 16 108 16 114 18 L115 42 C108 46 92 46 85 42 Z" fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} />
          {[88, 92, 96, 100, 104, 108, 112].map((x) => <line key={x} x1={x} y1="19" x2={x} y2="43" stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".35" />)}
          <path d="M85 42 C92 46 108 46 115 42" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.5)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth="1.5" />
        </g>
      )}
      {art === 'shirt' && (
        <g>
          <path d="M88 34 L100 58 L112 34 L109 30 C104 33 96 33 91 30 Z" fill={shade(c, 0.05)} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".45" strokeWidth=".8" />
          <path d="M88 34 L79 46 L94 52 Z M112 34 L121 46 L106 52 Z" fill={shade(c, 0.08)} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".45" strokeWidth=".8" />
          <rect x="97.5" y="58" width="5" height="154" fill={shade(c, -0.04)} opacity=".6" />
          {stitch(c, 'M97 58 L97 212 M103 58 L103 212', 0.6)}
          {[76, 102, 128, 154, 180].map((y) => <g key={y}><circle cx="100" cy={y} r="2.1" fill={shade(c, 0.35)} stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeWidth=".5" /><circle cx="99.4" cy={y - 0.4} r=".4" fill={shade(c, -0.4)} /></g>)}
          <path d="M70 84 L86 84 L86 100 L78 103 L70 100 Z" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".45" strokeWidth=".8" />
          {stitch(c, 'M144 176 L163 174 M56 176 L37 174', 0.6)}
        </g>
      )}
    </g>
  );
}

function Bottom({ id, item, c, fab }) {
  const art = item.art;
  if (art === 'skirt') {
    return (
      <Body id={id} d={P.skirt} c={c} fab={fab}>
        {[52, 64, 76, 88, 100, 112, 124, 136, 148].map((x, i) => (
          <g key={x}>
            <path d={`M${100 + (x - 100) * 0.3} 52 L${x} 220`} stroke={shade(c, -0.5)} strokeOpacity=".35" strokeWidth="2.4" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />
            <path d={`M${100 + (x - 100) * 0.3 + 2} 52 L${x + 5} 220`} stroke="#fff" strokeOpacity={i % 2 ? 0.12 : 0.2} strokeWidth="2" />
          </g>
        ))}
        <rect x="70" y="40" width="60" height="12" fill={shade(c, -0.08)} />
      </Body>
    );
  }
  const d = art === 'shorts' ? P.shorts : item.fit === 'relaxed' || item.fit === 'oversized' ? P.wide : P.trousers;
  const top = art === 'shorts' ? 58 : 30;
  const folds = art === 'shorts'
    ? ['M78 100 Q82 130 76 160', 'M122 100 Q118 130 124 160']
    : ['M80 110 Q84 160 76 220', 'M120 110 Q116 160 124 220', 'M64 200 Q74 206 86 200', 'M114 200 Q126 206 136 200'];
  const lights = art === 'shorts' ? ['M70 80 Q66 120 62 150'] : ['M72 50 Q68 140 64 215', 'M128 50 Q132 140 136 215'];
  return (
    <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={folds} lights={lights}>
      <rect x="60" y={top} width="80" height="11" fill={shade(c, -0.06)} opacity=".9" />
      {stitch(c, `M60 ${top + 11} L140 ${top + 11}`)}
      {[70, 86, 114, 130].map((x) => <rect key={x} x={x} y={top - 1} width="3" height="13" fill={shade(c, -0.12)} />)}
      <circle cx="100" cy={top + 5.5} r="2.2" fill={art === 'jeans' ? '#B98B3E' : shade(c, -0.3)} />
      {stitch(c, `M100 ${top + 12} L100 ${top + 60}`, 0.7)}
      {art === 'jeans' ? (
        <g fill="none" stroke="#C99A4B" strokeOpacity=".85" strokeWidth=".8" strokeDasharray="2 1.4">
          <path d={`M66 ${top + 14} Q74 ${top + 34} 90 ${top + 32}`} /><path d={`M134 ${top + 14} Q126 ${top + 34} 110 ${top + 32}`} />
          <path d="M60 222 L90 222 M110 222 L140 222" />
        </g>
      ) : (
        <g>
          <path d={`M68 ${top + 14} L72 ${top + 34}`} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".5" />
          <path d={`M132 ${top + 14} L128 ${top + 34}`} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".5" />
          {art !== 'shorts' && <g stroke="#fff" strokeOpacity=".16" strokeWidth="1.2"><line x1="81" y1={top + 12} x2="76" y2="226" /><line x1="119" y1={top + 12} x2="124" y2="226" /></g>}
        </g>
      )}
    </Body>
  );
}

function Outer({ id, item, c, fab }) {
  const art = item.art;
  const d = art === 'coat' || art === 'trench' ? P.coat : art === 'puffer' ? P.puffer : P.jacket;
  const hem = art === 'coat' || art === 'trench' ? 233 : art === 'puffer' ? 195 : 189;
  const openFront = art !== 'puffer';
  const folds = ['M66 120 Q76 160 70 ' + (hem - 10), 'M134 120 Q124 160 130 ' + (hem - 10), 'M44 110 Q50 150 42 190', 'M156 110 Q150 150 158 190'];
  return (
    <g>
      <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={folds} lights={['M78 60 Q74 120 72 ' + (hem - 20), 'M50 70 Q46 110 42 150']}>
        {art === 'puffer' && [62, 84, 106, 128, 150, 172].map((y) => (
          <g key={y}>
            <path d={`M20 ${y} Q100 ${y + 5} 180 ${y}`} stroke={shade(c, -0.6)} strokeOpacity=".55" strokeWidth="2.2" fill="none" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />
            <path d={`M20 ${y + 9} Q100 ${y + 14} 180 ${y + 9}`} stroke="#fff" strokeOpacity=".22" strokeWidth="4" fill="none" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />
          </g>
        ))}
        {art === 'trench' && (
          <g>
            <rect x="56" y="128" width="88" height="9" fill={shade(c, -0.06)} />
            {stitch(c, 'M56 129.5 L144 129.5 M56 135.5 L144 135.5', 0.6)}
            <rect x="104" y="126" width="9" height="13" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.45)} strokeWidth="1.4" />
            <path d="M60 54 L82 64 L80 92 L60 84 Z" fill={shade(c, -0.03)} stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth=".6" />
          </g>
        )}
        {art === 'cardigan' && Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => <line key={i} x1={60 + i * 6.6} y1="178" x2={60 + i * 6.6} y2="189" stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeOpacity=".4" />)}
        {art === 'jacket' && (
          <g>
            {stitch(c, 'M60 112 L140 112')}
            <path d="M66 86 L86 86 L86 104 L76 107 L66 104 Z" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth=".8" />
            <path d="M134 86 L114 86 L114 104 L124 107 L134 104 Z" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth=".8" />
            {[120, 150, 176].map((y) => <circle key={y} cx="104" cy={y} r="2.4" fill="#B98B3E" />)}
          </g>
        )}
        {(art === 'coat' || art === 'blazer') && <path d={`M70 ${hem - 70} L86 ${hem - 72} M114 ${hem - 72} L130 ${hem - 70}`} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".45" strokeWidth="1.2" />}
      </Body>
      {openFront && (
        <g>
          <path d="M88 24 L82 46 L97 98 L100 72 Z" fill={shade(c, 0.04)} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth=".7" />
          <path d="M112 24 L118 46 L103 98 L100 72 Z" fill={shade(c, -0.08)} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth=".7" />
          <path d="M97 98 L100 72 L103 98" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.6)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth="2" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />
          <line x1="100" y1="96" x2="100" y2={hem} stroke={shade(c, -0.5)} strokeOpacity=".55" strokeWidth="1.1" />
          {(art === 'coat' || art === 'blazer' || art === 'trench' || art === 'cardigan') && [118, 156].filter((y) => y < hem - 10).map((y) => (
            <g key={y}><circle cx="107" cy={y} r="3.2" fill={shade(c, -0.55)} /><circle cx="106.2" cy={y - 0.8} r="1" fill="#fff" opacity=".25" /></g>
          ))}
        </g>
      )}
      {art === 'puffer' && <line x1="100" y1="36" x2="100" y2={hem} stroke={shade(c, -0.6)} strokeOpacity=".7" strokeWidth="1.6" />}
    </g>
  );
}

function Shoe({ id, item, c, fab, x, y, back }) {
  const art = item.art;
  const sole = art === 'sneaker' ? '#F2F0EC' : art === 'boot' ? '#262626' : shade(c, -0.45);
  let upper, soleD, extra = null;
  if (art === 'boot') {
    upper = 'M30 60 C30 56 33 54 37 54 L78 54 C82 54 84 57 84 61 L86 128 C104 134 126 142 140 152 C148 158 152 164 152 172 L152 176 L28 176 C26 120 28 90 30 60 Z';
    soleD = 'M26 176 L154 176 C154 182 151 186 146 186 L74 186 L72 190 L34 190 C29 190 26 186 26 182 Z';
    extra = <path d="M52 62 Q46 100 54 140 L76 140 Q70 100 76 62 Z" fill={shade(c, -0.18)} opacity=".85" />;
  } else if (art === 'loafer') {
    upper = 'M18 160 C18 146 28 138 44 138 C70 136 108 140 132 150 C144 155 150 162 150 170 L150 174 L18 174 Z';
    soleD = 'M16 174 L152 174 C152 179 149 182 144 182 L22 182 C18 182 16 179 16 176 Z';
    extra = (
      <g>
        <path d="M70 146 C90 140 120 144 134 152" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.45)} strokeOpacity=".6" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="2 1.4" />
        <rect x="84" y="146" width="30" height="7" rx="2" fill={shade(c, -0.15)} />
        <ellipse cx="99" cy="149.5" rx="7" ry="1.6" fill={shade(c, -0.55)} />
      </g>
    );
  } else if (art === 'sandal') {
    upper = 'M14 166 C14 160 18 158 24 158 L140 160 C148 160 152 164 152 170 L152 174 L14 174 Z';
    soleD = 'M12 174 L154 174 C154 179 151 182 146 182 L18 182 C14 182 12 179 12 176 Z';
    extra = (
      <g fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.05)} strokeWidth="7" strokeLinecap="round">
        <path d="M40 160 Q64 128 92 160" /><path d="M78 160 Q104 132 126 161" />
      </g>
    );
  } else {
    upper = 'M20 150 C20 128 30 118 46 116 C58 114 66 118 74 126 C90 132 112 138 130 146 C142 151 148 158 148 166 L148 172 L20 172 Z';
    soleD = 'M18 172 L150 172 C150 180 146 184 140 184 L24 184 C20 184 18 181 18 178 Z';
    extra = (
      <g>
        <path d="M60 124 C76 128 96 134 118 142" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth="1" strokeDasharray="2 1.4" />
        {[0, 1, 2, 3, 4].map((i) => <line key={i} x1={74 + i * 9} y1={128 + i * 2.4} x2={80 + i * 9} y2={138 + i * 2.4} stroke={shade(c, -0.25)} strokeWidth="2" strokeLinecap="round" />)}
        <path d="M22 160 C40 158 60 160 80 166" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth=".8" strokeDasharray="2 1.4" />
        <line x1="20" y1="178" x2="150" y2="178" stroke="#C9C6C0" strokeWidth=".8" />
      </g>
    );
  }
  const sid = id + (back ? 'k' : 'f');
  return (
    <g transform={`translate(${x} ${y})`} style={back ? { filter: 'brightness(.8)' } : undefined}>
      <clipPath id={`${sid}c`}><path d={upper} /></clipPath>
      <path d={upper} fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} />
      <g clipPath={`url(#${sid}c)`}>
        <path d={upper} fill={`url(#${id}h)`} />
        {fab.sheen > 0.3 && <ellipse cx="70" cy="132" rx="40" ry="6" fill="#fff" opacity=".28" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />}
        {extra}
      </g>
      <path d={upper} fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.45)} strokeOpacity=".4" strokeWidth=".8" />
      <path d={soleD} fill={sole} />
      <path d={soleD} fill="none" stroke="#000" strokeOpacity=".15" strokeWidth=".6" />
    </g>
  );
}

function Shoes(props) {
  // product-shot pair: one shoe behind, slightly offset
  return (
    <g transform="translate(14 22)">
      <Shoe {...props} x={22} y={-22} back />
      <Shoe {...props} x={0} y={0} />
    </g>
  );
}

function Dress({ id, item, c, fab }) {
  const c2 = item.colors?.[1]?.hex;
  return (
    <g>
      <Body id={id} d={P.dress} c={c} fab={fab}
        folds={['M86 100 Q80 160 70 214', 'M114 100 Q120 160 130 214', 'M100 110 Q98 170 100 220']}
        lights={['M92 40 Q96 70 94 92', 'M96 110 Q90 170 84 216']}>
        {item.pattern === 'floral' && Array.from({ length: 26 }, (_, i) => (
          <g key={i} transform={`translate(${60 + (i * 37) % 84} ${34 + Math.floor(i / 4) * 30 + (i % 2) * 8})`}>
            {[0, 72, 144, 216, 288].map((a) => <ellipse key={a} cx="0" cy="-3" rx="2" ry="3.4" fill={c2 || shade(c, 0.45)} transform={`rotate(${a})`} opacity=".85" />)}
            <circle r="1.3" fill={shade(c, -0.4)} />
          </g>
        ))}
        {item.pattern === 'stripe' && Array.from({ length: 16 }, (_, i) => <line key={i} x1="40" y1={30 + i * 13} x2="160" y2={30 + i * 13} stroke={c2 || '#1F2A44'} strokeWidth="4" />)}
        <path d="M76 92 C90 98 110 98 124 92" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth="1.2" />
      </Body>
      {item.sleeve && item.sleeve !== 'sleeveless' && (
        <g>
          <path d="M80 26 C70 30 60 40 54 56 L66 62 C70 50 76 42 80 38 Z" fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} />
          <path d="M120 26 C130 30 140 40 146 56 L134 62 C130 50 124 42 120 38 Z" fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} />
        </g>
      )}
      {(!item.sleeve || item.sleeve === 'sleeveless') && <path d="M86 22 L86 8 M114 22 L114 8" stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeWidth="2" />}
      <path d="M88 22 C94 30 106 30 112 22" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeWidth="1.4" />
    </g>
  );
}

function Accessory({ id, item, c, fab }) {
  if (item.art === 'hat') {
    const d = 'M30 150 C30 138 60 130 100 130 C140 130 170 138 170 150 C170 160 140 166 100 166 C60 166 30 160 30 150 Z M62 140 C62 100 76 80 100 80 C124 80 138 100 138 140 Z';
    return (
      <g>
        <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={['M70 150 Q100 158 130 150']} lights={['M80 100 Q90 90 110 90']}>
          <rect x="62" y="124" width="76" height="10" fill={shade(c, -0.3)} />
        </Body>
      </g>
    );
  }
  if (item.art === 'necklace') {
    return (
      <g fill="none" strokeLinecap="round">
        <path d="M58 60 C58 150 142 150 142 60" stroke={shade(c, -0.25)} strokeWidth="3.5" />
        <path d="M58 60 C58 150 142 150 142 60" stroke={shade(c, 0.35)} strokeWidth="1.2" strokeDasharray="2 3" />
        <circle cx="100" cy="132" r="8" fill={c} stroke={shade(c, -0.35)} strokeWidth="1.5" />
        <circle cx="97" cy="129" r="2.5" fill="#fff" opacity=".6" />
      </g>
    );
  }
  if (item.art === 'belt') {
    return (
      <g>
        <path d="M24 120 C60 110 140 110 176 120 L176 134 C140 124 60 124 24 134 Z" fill={`url(#${id}g)`} filter={`url(#${id}t)`} stroke={shade(c, -0.4)} strokeWidth=".8" />
        <rect x="88" y="108" width="24" height="30" rx="3" fill="none" stroke="#B9A35A" strokeWidth="4" />
        {stitch(c, 'M28 124 C60 115 140 115 172 124')}
      </g>
    );
  }
  if (item.art === 'scarf') {
    const d = 'M72 36 C88 32 112 32 128 36 L132 196 C126 198 116 198 110 196 L110 74 L90 74 L90 212 C84 214 74 214 68 212 Z';
    return (
      <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={['M100 40 Q96 60 100 74', 'M80 90 Q84 150 78 200', 'M120 90 Q116 140 122 190']} lights={['M74 60 Q72 140 72 200']}>
        <g stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeOpacity=".3">{Array.from({ length: 30 }, (_, i) => { const x = 68 + (i % 15) * 1.6 + (i > 14 ? 42 : 0); return <line key={i} x1={x} y1="40" x2={x} y2="210" />; })}</g>
      </Body>
    );
  }
  if (item.art === 'crossbody') {
    const d = 'M54 122 C54 116 58 112 64 112 L136 112 C142 112 146 116 146 122 L146 182 C146 188 142 192 136 192 L64 192 C58 192 54 188 54 182 Z';
    return (
      <g>
        <path d="M58 116 C62 40 138 40 142 116" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.25)} strokeWidth="4.5" />
        <path d="M58 116 C62 40 138 40 142 116" fill="none" stroke="#fff" strokeOpacity=".15" strokeWidth="1.5" />
        <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={['M60 186 Q100 190 140 186']} lights={['M64 120 Q100 116 136 120']}>
          <path d="M54 112 L146 112 L146 148 C120 154 80 154 54 148 Z" fill={shade(c, 0.04)} />
          <path d="M54 148 C80 154 120 154 146 148" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.6)} strokeOpacity=".5" strokeWidth="2" filter={`url(#${id}b)`} />
          {stitch(c, 'M58 116 L142 116 M58 145 C82 150 118 150 142 145', 0.6)}
        </Body>
        <rect x="94" y="144" width="12" height="10" rx="1.5" fill="#C9C6BE" stroke="#8C8A84" strokeWidth=".6" />
      </g>
    );
  }
  const d = 'M50 100 L150 100 L158 214 C158 218 155 220 151 220 L49 220 C45 220 42 218 42 214 Z';
  return (
    <g>
      <path d="M74 102 C74 54 126 54 126 102" fill="none" stroke={shade(c, -0.3)} strokeWidth="6" />
      <Body id={id} d={d} c={c} fab={fab} folds={['M70 110 Q74 160 66 214', 'M130 110 Q126 160 134 214']} lights={['M90 110 Q96 160 92 210']}>
        {stitch(c, 'M50 106 L150 106')}
      </Body>
    </g>
  );
}

/** Which picture shows for a piece, and whether it is AI-made. */
export function photoOf(item, photos) {
  if (item?.image) return { src: item.image, ai: false };
  const ai = photos?.garments?.[item?.id] || item?.aiImage;
  return ai ? { src: ai, ai: true } : { src: null, ai: false };
}

export default function GarmentArt({ item, className = '', style }) {
  const rid = useId().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '');
  const photos = usePhotos();
  if (!item) return null;
  // 1) the user's own photo, 2) an AI-made sample photo (demo photo slots, or one she asked for), 3) the drawn render
  const { src: photo, ai } = photoOf(item, photos);
  if (photo) {
    return <img className={'garment-img ' + (item.cutout === 'kept-original' && !ai ? 'uncut ' : '') + (ai ? 'ai-photo ' : '') + className} style={style} src={photo}
      alt={`${ai ? 'AI-made photo of ' : ''}${item.colors?.[0]?.name || ''} ${item.name}`} title={ai ? 'AI-made sample photo, not a photo of the real piece' : undefined} draggable="false"
      onError={item.image ? undefined : () => markBroken(photo)} />;
  }
  const c = item.colors?.[0]?.hex || '#999999';
  const fab = fabricFor(item);
  const id = 'g' + rid;
  const props = { id, item, c, fab };
  const body = { top: <Top {...props} />, bottom: <Bottom {...props} />, dress: <Dress {...props} />, outer: <Outer {...props} />, shoes: <Shoes {...props} />, accessory: <Accessory {...props} /> }[item.category]
    || <Body id={id} d="M50 40 L150 40 L150 210 L50 210 Z" c={c} fab={fab} />;
  return (
    <svg className={'garment-svg ' + className} style={style} viewBox="0 0 200 250" role="img" aria-label={`${item.colors?.[0]?.name || ''} ${item.name}`}>
      <Defs id={id} c={c} fab={fab} />
      <ellipse className="contact-shadow" cx="100" cy="236" rx="74" ry="7" fill="#000" opacity=".10" filter={`url(#${id}s)`} />
      {body}
    </svg>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/components/ItemForm.jsx

// One form for every way an item is described: after an upload, when adding by hand, and when editing later.
import { useState } from 'react';
import { NAMED_COLORS } from '../ai/color.js';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { CATEGORIES, ARTS, STYLES, SLEEVES, OCCASIONS } from '../data/closet.js';

export const SEASONS = ['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter'];
const PATTERNS = ['solid', 'stripe', 'check', 'floral', 'dots', 'graphic', 'other'];
export const STATUSES = [
  { id: 'available', label: 'Available' },
  { id: 'laundry', label: 'In the laundry' },
  { id: 'repair', label: 'Needs repair' },
  { id: 'stored', label: 'Stored away' },
];
const toggle = (list = [], v) => (list.includes(v) ? list.filter((x) => x !== v) : [...list, v]);

/** A sensible blank item for manual entry (no photo). */
export function blankItem(category = 'top') {
  const c = NAMED_COLORS.find((x) => x.name === 'black') || NAMED_COLORS[0];
  return {
    id: 'm-' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
    name: '', category, art: ARTS[category][0], colors: [{ name: c.name, hex: c.hex }], pattern: 'solid',
    material: '', weight: 'mid', fit: 'regular', warmth: 2, water: 1, formality: 2, style: 'casual', sleeve: null,
    seasons: [...SEASONS], occasions: [], status: 'available', manual: true,
  };
}

export const LOCATION_IDEAS = ['Bedroom closet', 'Dresser', 'Under-bed box', 'Shoe rack', 'Storage room'];

/** Pick where a piece is kept, or add a new place on the spot. */
export function LocationPicker({ value, onChange }) {
  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  function add(n) {
    const clean = (n || '').trim();
    if (!clean) return;
    const existing = state.locations.find((l) => l.name.toLowerCase() === clean.toLowerCase());
    const id = existing?.id || 'loc-' + Date.now().toString(36);
    if (!existing) dispatch({ type: 'ADD_LOCATION', location: { id, name: clean } });
    onChange(id); setAdding(false); setName('');
  }
  if (adding) {
    return (
      <div>
        <div className="anchor-row">
          <input className="input" autoFocus value={name} placeholder="e.g. Hallway cabinet" aria-label="New place" onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); add(name); } }} />
          <button type="button" className="btn sm primary" onClick={() => add(name)}>Add</button>
          <button type="button" className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => setAdding(false)}>Cancel</button>
        </div>
        {state.locations.length === 0 && <div className="chips" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>{LOCATION_IDEAS.map((l) => <button type="button" key={l} className="chip" onClick={() => add(l)}>{l}</button>)}</div>}
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
    <select className="select" value={value || ''} aria-label="Where it's kept" onChange={(e) => (e.target.value === '__new' ? setAdding(true) : onChange(e.target.value || null))}>
      <option value="">Not set</option>
      {state.locations.map((l) => <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name}</option>)}
      <option value="__new">+ Add a place…</option>
    </select>
  );
}

export const FIELD_LABEL = { category: 'category', art: 'type', pattern: 'pattern', material: 'fabric', fit: 'fit', warmth: 'warmth', formality: 'formality', water: 'rain-readiness', style: 'style' };

/** A small "not checked" marker for details that are still a guess. */
const Q = ({ on }) => (on ? <span className="unchecked" title="A guess, not checked yet">not checked</span> : null);

export default function ItemForm({ item, onChange }) {
  const uv = item.unverified || [];
  const u = (k) => uv.includes(k);
  // Editing a detail means she has looked at it: it stops being a guess.
  const clear = (keys, next) => ({ ...next, unverified: (next.unverified || uv).filter((x) => !keys.includes(x)) });
  const set = (k, v) => onChange(clear([k], { ...item, [k]: v }));
  const setColor = (i, name) => {
    const c = NAMED_COLORS.find((x) => x.name === name);
    const colors = [...item.colors];
    // Keep the measured hex of a photographed item when only the name is corrected; manual items take the named color.
    if (name === '') colors.splice(i, 1);
    else colors[i] = { name, hex: item.image && i === 0 && colors[0] ? colors[0].hex : c.hex };
    onChange({ ...item, colors: colors.length ? colors : item.colors });
  };
  const hasSleeve = ['top', 'dress', 'outer'].includes(item.category);
  return (
    <div className="item-form">
      {uv.length > 0 && (
        <div className="check-banner">
          <span className="small">{item.tagSource === 'ai' ? 'Suggested by Gemini from the photo.' : item.tagSource === 'device' ? 'A rough guess made on this device (only the color was measured).' : 'Some details are guesses.'} Please check the details marked <b>not checked</b>.</span>
          <button type="button" className="btn sm" onClick={() => onChange({ ...item, unverified: [] })}>Everything looks right</button>
        </div>
      )}
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Name</label>
        <input className="input" value={item.name} placeholder="e.g. Navy Linen Shirt" onChange={(e) => set('name', e.target.value)} /></div>
      <div className="field-row">
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Category <Q on={u('category')} /></label>
          <select className="select" value={item.category} onChange={(e) => onChange(clear(['category'], { ...item, category: e.target.value, art: ARTS[e.target.value][0] }))}>
            {CATEGORIES.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.label}</option>)}
          </select></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Type <Q on={u('art')} /></label>
          <select className="select" value={item.art} onChange={(e) => set('art', e.target.value)}>
            {ARTS[item.category].map((a) => <option key={a} value={a}>{a}</option>)}
          </select></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field-row">
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Main color</label>
          <select className="select" value={item.colors[0]?.name} onChange={(e) => setColor(0, e.target.value)}>
            {NAMED_COLORS.map((c) => <option key={c.name}>{c.name}</option>)}
          </select></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Second color</label>
          <select className="select" value={item.colors[1]?.name || ''} onChange={(e) => setColor(1, e.target.value)}>
            <option value="">none</option>
            {NAMED_COLORS.map((c) => <option key={c.name}>{c.name}</option>)}
          </select></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field-row">
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Pattern <Q on={u('pattern')} /></label>
          <select className="select" value={item.pattern || 'solid'} onChange={(e) => set('pattern', e.target.value)}>{PATTERNS.map((p) => <option key={p}>{p}</option>)}</select></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Material <Q on={u('material')} /></label>
          <input className="input" value={item.material || ''} placeholder="cotton, wool, denim…" onChange={(e) => set('material', e.target.value)} /></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field-row">
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Style <Q on={u('style')} /></label>
          <select className="select" value={item.style || 'casual'} onChange={(e) => set('style', e.target.value)}>{STYLES.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}</select></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Fit <Q on={u('fit')} /></label>
          <select className="select" value={item.fit || 'regular'} onChange={(e) => set('fit', e.target.value)}>{['fitted', 'regular', 'relaxed', 'oversized'].map((f) => <option key={f}>{f}</option>)}</select></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field-row">
        {hasSleeve ? (
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Sleeve</label>
            <select className="select" value={item.sleeve || ''} onChange={(e) => set('sleeve', e.target.value || null)}><option value="">not sure</option>{SLEEVES.map((s) => <option key={s}>{s}</option>)}</select></div>
        ) : <div className="field" />}
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Weight</label>
          <select className="select" value={item.weight || 'mid'} onChange={(e) => set('weight', e.target.value)}>{['light', 'mid', 'heavy'].map((f) => <option key={f}>{f}</option>)}</select></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field-row three">
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Warmth · {item.warmth}/5 <Q on={u('warmth')} /></label><input type="range" min="0" max="5" value={item.warmth} onChange={(e) => set('warmth', +e.target.value)} /></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Rain-ready · {item.water || 1}/5 <Q on={u('water')} /></label><input type="range" min="1" max="5" value={item.water || 1} onChange={(e) => set('water', +e.target.value)} /></div>
        <div className="field"><label className="label">Formality · {item.formality}/5 <Q on={u('formality')} /></label><input type="range" min="1" max="5" value={item.formality} onChange={(e) => set('formality', +e.target.value)} /></div>
      </div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Seasons</label>
        <div className="chips">{SEASONS.map((s) => <button type="button" key={s} className={'chip' + ((item.seasons || []).includes(s) ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('seasons', toggle(item.seasons, s))}>{s}</button>)}</div></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Good for</label>
        <div className="chips">{OCCASIONS.map((o) => <button type="button" key={o.id} className={'chip' + ((item.occasions || []).includes(o.id) ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('occasions', toggle(item.occasions, o.id))}>{o.label}</button>)}</div></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Where it's kept</label>
        <LocationPicker value={item.location} onChange={(v) => set('location', v)} /></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Status</label>
        <div className="chips">{STATUSES.map((s) => <button type="button" key={s.id} className={'chip' + ((item.status || 'available') === s.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => set('status', s.id)}>{s.label}</button>)}</div>
        <span className="tiny">Only available pieces are used in outfit suggestions.</span></div>
    </div>
  );
}

// Motion & graphic pieces: the "stylist at work" loader and the Home hero showcase.
// Everything respects prefers-reduced-motion (see styles.css).
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Stage from './Stage.jsx';
import { DEMO_CLOSET } from '../data/closet.js';

const STEPS = ['Reading your closet', 'Matching colors', 'Balancing proportions', 'Checking the weather', 'Choosing shoes', 'Finishing touches'];

/** Shown while outfits are being put together. Real progress text, not a fake percentage. */
export function StylistLoader({ count }) {
  const [i, setI] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => { const t = setInterval(() => setI((x) => (x + 1) % STEPS.length), 900); return () => clearInterval(t); }, []);
  return (
    <div className="stylist-loader" role="status" aria-live="polite">
      <div className="loader-rack" aria-hidden="true">
        {['M30 12 L42 8 Q50 14 58 8 L70 12 L80 28 L72 32 L68 26 L68 70 L32 70 L32 26 L28 32 L20 28 Z',
          'M34 8 L66 8 L70 72 L54 72 L50 30 L46 72 L30 72 Z',
          'M28 40 Q40 24 60 30 L76 36 Q82 44 76 50 L30 52 Q22 48 28 40 Z'].map((d, k) => (
          <svg key={k} viewBox="0 0 100 80" className={'loader-piece p' + k}><path d={d} /></svg>
        ))}
      </div>
      <div className="loader-text"><b key={i}>{STEPS[i]}…</b><span className="tiny">Looking through {count} available pieces</span></div>
    </div>
  );
}

const byId = (id) => DEMO_CLOSET.find((g) => g.id === id);
const EXAMPLES = [
  { name: 'Trench & olive', o: { top: byId('t-black-turtle'), bottom: byId('b-olive-chino'), outer: byId('o-trench'), shoes: byId('s-black-boot'), bag: byId('a-crossbody') } },
  { name: 'Weekend stripes', o: { top: byId('t-stripe-tee'), bottom: byId('b-dark-denim'), shoes: byId('s-white-sneaker'), bag: byId('a-tote') } },
  { name: 'Evening navy', o: { top: byId('d-navy-dress'), outer: byId('o-wool-coat'), shoes: byId('s-suede-loafer'), accessory: byId('a-necklace') } },
].map((e) => ({ ...e, o: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(e.o).filter(([, v]) => v)) }));

/** Cycling example looks for the Home hero (clearly labelled as illustrations). */
export function HeroShowcase() {
  const [i, setI] = useState(0);
  const [mode, setMode] = useState('flatlay');
  useEffect(() => {
    const reduce = window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (reduce) return undefined;
    const t = setInterval(() => setI((x) => { const n = (x + 1) % EXAMPLES.length; if (n === 0) setMode((m) => (m === 'flatlay' ? 'mannequin' : 'flatlay')); return n; }), 3600);
    return () => clearInterval(t);
  }, []);
  const ex = EXAMPLES[i];
  return (
    <div className="hero-showcase">
      <div className="hero-blob b1" aria-hidden="true" /><div className="hero-blob b2" aria-hidden="true" />
      <div className="hero-frame">
        <Stage key={i + mode} outfit={ex.o} mode={mode} />
        <div className="hero-tag"><span className="tiny">Example look · illustration</span><b>{ex.name}</b></div>
      </div>
      <div className="hero-dots" role="tablist" aria-label="Example looks">
        {EXAMPLES.map((e, k) => <button key={k} role="tab" aria-selected={k === i} aria-label={e.name} className={k === i ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setI(k)} />)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './common.jsx';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';

export default function SaveModal({ defaultName = '', onClose, onDone }) {
  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
  const [name, setName] = useState(defaultName);
  const [target, setTarget] = useState(state.catalogs[0]?.id || 'new');
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('collection');
  const [city, setCity] = useState('');
  const [days, setDays] = useState(3);

  function save() {
    if (target === 'new') {
      const t = title.trim() || 'New lookbook';
      dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', name, newCatalog: { title: t, kind, ...(kind === 'trip' ? { trip: { city: city || '—', days: +days } } : {}) } });
      onDone(t);
    } else {
      dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', name, catalogId: target });
      onDone(state.catalogs.find((c) => c.id === target)?.title);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Modal title="Save to lookbook" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Look name</label><input className="input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Rainy café uniform" autoFocus /></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Lookbook</label>
        <select className="select" value={target} onChange={(e) => setTarget(e.target.value)}>
          {state.catalogs.map((c) => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}{c.kind === 'trip' ? ' (trip)' : ''}</option>)}
          <option value="new">+ New lookbook…</option>
        </select></div>
      {target === 'new' && (
        <>
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Title</label><input className="input" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Spring Rotation, 4-Day Kyoto Trip…" /></div>
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Type</label>
            <div className="chips">
              <button className={'chip' + (kind === 'collection' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setKind('collection')}>Collection</button>
              <button className={'chip' + (kind === 'trip' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setKind('trip')}>Trip (with versatility score)</button>
            </div></div>
          {kind === 'trip' && (
            <div className="field-row">
              <div className="field"><label className="label">City</label><input className="input" value={city} onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)} /></div>
              <div className="field"><label className="label">Days</label><input className="input" type="number" min="1" max="21" value={days} onChange={(e) => setDays(e.target.value)} /></div>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      <button className="btn accent block" onClick={save}>Save look</button>
    </Modal>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/components/Stage.jsx
====================================================================================================

// The 4:5 visual stage: flat-lay, mannequin, or scene.
import GarmentArt from './GarmentArt.jsx';
import { SCENES } from '../data/scenes.jsx';
import { usePhotos, markBroken } from '../data/photos.js';
import { photoOf } from './GarmentArt.jsx';
import Worn from './Worn.jsx';

// Box positions in % of the 4:5 stage: [left, top, width, height]
const FLATLAY_WITH_OUTER = {
  outer: [2, 4, 50, 56], top: [46, 2, 44, 44], bottom: [50, 40, 38, 56], shoes: [4, 60, 40, 34], bag: [78, 66, 22, 26], accessory: [36, 60, 20, 20],
};
const FLATLAY = {
  top: [4, 4, 52, 50], bottom: [50, 14, 42, 60], shoes: [8, 56, 40, 36], bag: [68, 72, 28, 26], accessory: [52, 2, 20, 20],
};
// A dress takes the whole left/centre column
const DRESS = { top: [10, 2, 50, 72], outer: [54, 2, 44, 52], shoes: [12, 72, 40, 26], bag: [66, 66, 28, 28], accessory: [60, 46, 22, 20] };

// Soft-focus "photographic" treatment: background out of focus, film grain, vignette.
function PhotoFinish({ children }) {
  return (
    <svg className="stage-layer" viewBox="0 0 400 500" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice" aria-hidden="true">
      <defs>
        <filter id="ph-blur" x="-10%" y="-10%" width="120%" height="120%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="7" /></filter>
        <filter id="ph-grain"><feTurbulence type="fractalNoise" baseFrequency=".9" numOctaves="2" seed="3" /><feColorMatrix type="saturate" values="0" /><feComponentTransfer><feFuncA type="linear" slope=".09" /></feComponentTransfer></filter>
        <radialGradient id="ph-vig" cx=".5" cy=".45" r=".75"><stop offset=".55" stopColor="#000" stopOpacity="0" /><stop offset="1" stopColor="#000" stopOpacity=".42" /></radialGradient>
      </defs>
      <g filter="url(#ph-blur)">{children}</g>
      <rect width="400" height="500" fill="url(#ph-vig)" />
      <rect width="400" height="500" filter="url(#ph-grain)" />
    </svg>
  );
}

const box = ([l, t, w, h], extra = {}) => ({ left: l + '%', top: t + '%', width: w + '%', height: h + '%', ...extra });

export default function Stage({ outfit, mode = 'flatlay', sceneId = 'studio', customBg = null, personPhoto = null, caption, compact = false, child = false }) {
  const o = outfit || {};
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const person = personPhoto || photos.model;
  const scenePhoto = photos.scenes[sceneId];
  const scene = SCENES.find((s) => s.id === sceneId) || SCENES[0];
  const onBody = mode === 'mannequin' || mode === 'scene';
  const KEYS = ['outer', 'bottom', 'top', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'];
  const empty = !KEYS.some((k) => o[k]);

  let layers;
  if (!onBody) {
    const L = o.top?.category === 'dress' ? DRESS : o.outer ? FLATLAY_WITH_OUTER : FLATLAY;
    layers = KEYS.filter((k) => o[k]).map((k, i) => (
      <div key={k + o[k].id} className="stage-item" style={{ ...box(L[k] || FLATLAY[k]), '--i': i, '--r': `${((i * 37) % 7) - 3}deg` }}>
        <GarmentArt item={o[k]} />
      </div>
    ));
  } else {
    // dressed on the mannequin: every piece drawn to fit the body
    layers = <Worn key={KEYS.map((k) => o[k]?.id || '').join('|')} outfit={o} child={child} photoFor={(it) => photoOf(it, photos).src} />;
  }

  // "On you": a real photo of a person, with the outfit pinned beside it as a product card.
  if (mode === 'person') {
    return (
      <div className={`stage ${compact ? 'compact' : ''} mode-person`}>
        {person ? <img className="stage-layer cover" src={person} alt={personPhoto ? 'Your photo' : 'Model'} /> : <div className="stage-empty">Add a photo of yourself (or a model you like) to see your outfit beside a real person.</div>}
        <div className="person-card">
          {['outer', 'top', 'bottom', 'shoes', 'bag', 'accessory'].filter((k) => o[k]).map((k) => <div key={k} className="person-piece"><GarmentArt item={o[k]} /></div>)}
        </div>
        {caption && <div className="stage-caption">{caption}</div>}
      </div>
    );
  }

  const bg = mode === 'scene'
    ? (customBg ? <img className="stage-layer cover" src={customBg} alt="" />
      : scenePhoto ? <img className="stage-layer cover" src={scenePhoto} alt="" onError={() => markBroken(scenePhoto)} />
      : <PhotoFinish>{scene.render()}</PhotoFinish>)
    : null;

  const has = (it) => !!photoOf(it, photos).src;
  const anyAi = KEYS.some((k) => o[k] && photoOf(o[k], photos).ai);
  const photoOnBody = onBody && KEYS.some((k) => o[k] && has(o[k]));
  return (
    <div className={`stage ${compact ? 'compact' : ''} mode-${mode}`}>
      {bg}
      {layers}
      {photoOnBody && !compact && <div className="stage-note">Photos are placed as taken, not reshaped to the body{anyAi ? '; pieces marked AI use AI-made sample photos' : ''}</div>}
      {!compact && !empty && mode === 'flatlay' && (KEYS.some((k) => o[k] && !has(o[k])) || anyAi) && (
        <div className="stage-note">{KEYS.every((k) => !o[k] || !has(o[k])) ? 'Illustrations drawn from each piece\'s details, not photos'
          : KEYS.some((k) => o[k] && !has(o[k])) ? `Some pieces are illustrations${anyAi ? ', some AI-made sample photos' : ''}; add photos to see the real thing`
          : 'Some pieces show AI-made sample photos, not photos of the real pieces'}</div>
      )}
      {empty && !compact && <div className="stage-empty">Pick pieces from the slots below to start a look.</div>}
      {caption && <div className="stage-caption">{caption}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/components/TipJar.jsx

// 🍩 "Treat the developer to a donut": a discreet tip jar, same idea as Tiffo's.
// Shows nothing until CONFIG.GCASH_QR or CONFIG.TIP_URL is set, so visitors never see a broken button.
import { useState } from 'react';
import { CONFIG } from '../config.js';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { Modal } from './common.jsx';

export const tipsOn = () => !!(CONFIG.GCASH_QR || CONFIG.TIP_URL);

export default function TipJar({ compact = false }) {
  const { state } = useStore();
  const [qr, setQr] = useState(false);
  if (!tipsOn()) return null;
  // Honest "time saved" line: only counts things she actually did in Wearwise.
  const outfits = state.outfits.filter((o) => !o.demo).length + state.plans.length + state.history.length;
  return (
    <section className={'tip-card' + (compact ? ' compact' : '')} aria-label="Support the developer">
      <div className="tip-row">
        <span className="tip-donut" aria-hidden="true">🍩</span>
        <div>
          <b>Saved you time? Treat the developer to a donut</b>
          <div className="small">{outfits > 0 ? `You've saved, planned or worn ${outfits} outfit${outfits > 1 ? 's' : ''} with Wearwise. ` : ''}Wearwise is free, with no ads. Tips are optional and keep it that way.</div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="tip-actions">
        {CONFIG.GCASH_QR && <button className="btn sm tip-gcash" onClick={() => setQr(true)}><span className="g" aria-hidden="true">G</span> GCash / QR Ph</button>}
        {CONFIG.TIP_URL && <a className="btn sm primary" href={CONFIG.TIP_URL} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">💳 Card / PayPal</a>}
      </div>
      {qr && (
        <Modal title="🍩 Treat the developer" onClose={() => setQr(false)}>
          <div className="tip-qr">
            <p className="small muted">{CONFIG.GCASH_NAME ? `GCash · ${CONFIG.GCASH_NAME}` : 'GCash · Maya · any PH bank'}</p>
            <img src={CONFIG.GCASH_QR} alt="GCash / QR Ph code for tips" />
            <p className="tiny">Scan with GCash, Maya or your bank app (QR Ph). Any amount is appreciated. Thank you!</p>
          </div>
        </Modal>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/components/UploadModal.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './common.jsx';
import GarmentArt from './GarmentArt.jsx';
import { processUpload } from '../ai/tagger.js';
import { tagGarment } from '../ai/stylist.js';
import ItemForm, { blankItem } from './ItemForm.jsx';

const STEPS = ['Reading photo', 'Removing background', 'Reading colors', 'Tagging (category, fabric, season)'];

/** Makes a "messy" demo photo: a sweater lying on a bed sheet. */
async function samplePhoto() {
  const c = document.createElement('canvas');
  c.width = 600; c.height = 700;
  const x = c.getContext('2d');
  x.fillStyle = '#CDBFA8'; x.fillRect(0, 0, 600, 700);
  for (let i = 0; i < 2500; i++) { x.fillStyle = `rgba(${150 + Math.random() * 60},${130 + Math.random() * 50},${100 + Math.random() * 40},.18)`; x.fillRect(Math.random() * 600, Math.random() * 700, 3, 3); }
  x.translate(60, 70); x.scale(2.4, 2.4);
  x.fillStyle = '#2F6B4A';
  x.fill(new Path2D('M64 46 L86 38 Q100 50 114 38 L136 46 L156 74 L170 186 L150 190 L138 104 L138 210 L62 210 L62 104 L50 190 L30 186 L44 74 Z'));
  x.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,.18)'; x.lineWidth = 1.5;
  for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { x.beginPath(); x.moveTo(78 + i * 9, 70); x.quadraticCurveTo(82 + i * 9, 140, 76 + i * 9, 205); x.stroke(); }
  const blob = await new Promise((r) => c.toBlob(r, 'image/jpeg', 0.9));
  return new File([blob], 'green-knit-sweater-on-bed.jpg', { type: 'image/jpeg' });
}

export default function UploadModal({ onClose, onSaved, ownerName }) {
  const [phase, setPhase] = useState('pick'); // pick | working | review
  const [queue, setQueue] = useState([]);     // [{ key, file, state: 'waiting'|'working'|'done'|'failed', step, item, taggedBy, error }]
  const [sel, setSel] = useState(0);
  const [drag, setDrag] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const inputRef = useRef();
  const patch = (key, p) => setQueue((q) => q.map((x) => (x.key === key ? { ...x, ...p } : x)));

  async function runAll(fileList) {
    const files = [...(fileList || [])].filter((f) => f.type.startsWith('image/')).slice(0, 12);
    if (!files.length) { setError('Please choose one or more image files.'); return; }
    setError('');
    const jobs = files.map((file, i) => ({ key: Date.now() + '-' + i, file, state: 'waiting', step: 0 }));
    setQueue(jobs); setSel(0); setPhase('working');
    for (const job of jobs) { // one at a time keeps the browser responsive and the AI within rate limits
      patch(job.key, { state: 'working' });
      try {
        const g = await processUpload(job.file, (label) => patch(job.key, { step: STEPS.indexOf(label) }));
        g.id = 'u-' + Date.now().toString(36) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6);
        g.status = 'available';
        if (g.cutout === 'kept-original') {
          // The cut-out failed, so the "image" is her whole original photo. Never send that without asking.
          patch(job.key, { state: 'done', item: g, taggedBy: '', needsConsent: true });
        } else {
          const blob = await (await fetch(g.image)).blob();
          const ai = await tagGarment(blob, g);
          if (ai?.garment) patch(job.key, { state: 'done', item: ai.garment, taggedBy: ai.provider, sent: 'cut-out' });
          else patch(job.key, { state: 'done', item: g, taggedBy: '', error: ai?.offline ? '' : ai?.error || '' });
        }
      } catch {
        patch(job.key, { state: 'failed', error: 'This photo could not be read.' });
      }
    }
    setPhase('review');
  }

  async function askWithFullPhoto(job) {
    patch(job.key, { asking: true });
    const blob = await (await fetch(job.item.image)).blob();
    const ai = await tagGarment(blob, job.item);
    if (ai?.garment) patch(job.key, { asking: false, needsConsent: false, item: { ...ai.garment, cutout: 'kept-original' }, taggedBy: ai.provider, sent: 'full photo', error: '' });
    else patch(job.key, { asking: false, error: ai?.error || 'Gemini is not available right now. Please fill in the details yourself.' });
  }

  function manual() {
    setQueue([{ key: 'manual', state: 'done', item: blankItem('top'), taggedBy: '', manual: true }]);
    setSel(0); setPhase('review');
  }

  const ready = queue.filter((x) => x.state === 'done' && x.item);
  const current = queue[sel];
  const valid = (it) => it.name.trim().length > 0;

  function addAll() {
    const items = ready.map((x) => ({ ...x.item, name: x.item.name.trim() || 'Untitled piece' }));
    onSaved(items);
  }

  return (
    <Modal title={phase === 'review' ? (queue.length > 1 ? `Review ${ready.length} pieces` : 'Check the details') : 'Add pieces'} onClose={onClose} wide={phase !== 'pick'}>
      {phase === 'pick' && (
        <>
          <p className="muted small" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Choose one photo or several at once (up to 12). Messy photos are fine: on a bed, a hanger or the floor. Each piece is cut out, its colors are measured, and the AI drafts the details for you to check.{ownerName && ownerName !== 'Me' ? <> These go into <b>{ownerName}</b>'s closet.</> : null}</p>
          <div
            className={'dropzone' + (drag ? ' drag' : '')}
            onDragOver={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDrag(true); }}
            onDragLeave={() => setDrag(false)}
            onDrop={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); setDrag(false); runAll(e.dataTransfer.files); }}
          >
            <div className="label">Drop photos here</div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
              <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => inputRef.current.click()}>Choose photos</button>
              <label className="btn">Take a photo<input type="file" accept="image/*" capture="environment" hidden onChange={(e) => runAll(e.target.files)} /></label>
            </div>
            <input ref={inputRef} type="file" accept="image/*" multiple hidden onChange={(e) => runAll(e.target.files)} />
            <span className="tiny">Plain backgrounds give the cleanest cut-out.</span>
          </div>
          {error && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--accent)' }}>{error}</p>}
          <div style={{ marginTop: 16, display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn sm" onClick={manual}>Add without a photo</button>
            <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={async () => runAll([await samplePhoto()])}>Test with a sample photo</button>
          </div>
        </>
      )}

      {phase === 'working' && (
        <>
          <span className="ai-badge">Reading {queue.length} photo{queue.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span>
          <div className="loading-bar"><i /></div>
          <ul className="steps">
            {queue.map((x, i) => (
              <li key={x.key} className={x.state === 'done' ? 'done' : x.state === 'working' ? 'now' : ''}>
                Photo {i + 1} · {x.state === 'working' ? (STEPS[x.step] || 'Asking the AI for details') : x.state === 'done' ? 'ready' : x.state === 'failed' ? 'could not be read' : 'waiting'}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <p className="tiny"><b>On this device:</b> background removal, cropping and color reading. <b>Sent to Google Gemini</b> (through your Puter account, only if you've signed in to it): the cut-out of each piece, to suggest its details. If a cut-out fails, the photo is <b>not</b> sent unless you ask.</p>
        </>
      )}

      {phase === 'review' && current && (
        <div className="review">
          {queue.length > 1 && (
            <div className="review-list" role="tablist" aria-label="Uploaded pieces">
              {queue.map((x, i) => (
                <button key={x.key} role="tab" aria-selected={i === sel} className={'review-thumb' + (i === sel ? ' on' : '') + (x.state === 'failed' ? ' failed' : '')} onClick={() => setSel(i)}>
                  {x.item ? <GarmentArt item={x.item} /> : <span className="tiny">Failed</span>}
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {current.state === 'failed' ? (
            <div className="empty"><p className="small">{current.error} Try another photo, or add the piece without one.</p></div>
          ) : (
            <div className="confirm">
              <div>
                <div className="card-stage"><GarmentArt item={current.item} /></div>
                {current.item.image && <div className="swatches" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}><span className="swatch" style={{ background: current.item.colors[0].hex }} /> <span className="small">Color measured from your photo</span></div>}
                <p style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
                  {current.manual ? <span className="tiny">Added by hand. Fill in what you know.</span>
                    : current.taggedBy ? <span className="ai-badge">Details suggested by {current.taggedBy}</span>
                    : <span className="tiny">The AI wasn't used, so only the color was measured. The type is a rough guess and the other details are placeholders: please check them.</span>}
                </p>
                {current.item.cutout === 'kept-original' && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)' }}>The piece couldn't be separated from the background, so your photo is kept exactly as taken. Please check the color, which may include some of the background. A plain, contrasting background gives a clean cut-out.</p>}
                {current.needsConsent && (
                  <div className="consent">
                    <p className="small">Your photo was <b>not</b> sent to the AI. You can ask Gemini to suggest the details from the <b>whole photo, including its background</b> (sent to Google through your Puter account), or fill them in yourself.</p>
                    <button className="btn sm" disabled={current.asking} onClick={() => askWithFullPhoto(current)}>{current.asking ? 'Asking Gemini…' : 'Ask Gemini using the whole photo'}</button>
                  </div>
                )}
                {current.sent && <p className="tiny">Sent to Gemini: the {current.sent}.</p>}
                {current.item.aiNote && <p className="small muted">“{current.item.aiNote}”</p>}
                {current.error && <p className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)' }}>{current.error}</p>}
              </div>
              <div>
                <ItemForm item={current.item} onChange={(item) => patch(current.key, { item })} />
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14, justifyContent: 'flex-end', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            {queue.length > 1 && current.state === 'done' && <button className="btn" onClick={() => { setQueue((q) => q.filter((x) => x.key !== current.key)); setSel(0); }}>Skip this one</button>}
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setPhase('pick')}>Start over</button>
            <button className="btn primary" disabled={!ready.length || (current.manual && !valid(current.item))} onClick={addAll}>
              {ready.length > 1 ? `Add all ${ready.length} to closet` : 'Add to closet'}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/components/Worn.jsx
