// Outfit Calendar: plan outfits for days ahead (from saved outfits or a fresh suggestion), plan whole trips with a
// packing list, and see what was actually worn. One screen, per person.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import { Modal, PieceList, ScoreBadge, Verdict, ConfirmButton, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { ContextPicker, DEFAULT_CHOICE, contextOf, toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { generateOutfits } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { critique } from '../ai/critic.js';
import { planTrip, daysBetween, iso } from '../ai/planner.js';
import { OCCASIONS } from '../data/closet.js';
import { WEATHER_CHOICES } from '../ai/context.js';

const WEEK = ['Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat', 'Sun'];
const todayIso = () => iso(Date.now() - new Date().getTimezoneOffset() * 60000);
const nice = (d, o = { weekday: 'long', day: 'numeric', month: 'long' }) => new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined, o);

function monthGrid(year, month) {
  const first = new Date(year, month, 1, 12);
  const lead = (first.getDay() + 6) % 7; // Monday first
  const cells = [];
  for (let i = -lead; i < 42 - lead; i++) {
    const d = new Date(year, month, 1 + i, 12);
    cells.push({ date: iso(d.getTime() - d.getTimezoneOffset() * 60000), day: d.getDate(), inMonth: d.getMonth() === month });
  }
  // drop a trailing week that's entirely next month
  return cells.slice(35).every((c) => !c.inMonth) ? cells.slice(0, 35) : cells;
}

function DayPanel({ date, onClose }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, profile, owned, outfitsOf, historyOf } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const plan = state.plans.find((p) => p.profileId === profile.id && p.date === date);
  const worn = historyOf(profile.id).filter((h) => h.date === date);
  const trip = plan?.tripId && state.trips.find((t) => t.id === plan.tripId);
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('saved');
  const [wardrobe, setWardrobe] = useState('all');
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(DEFAULT_CHOICE);
  const [ideas, setIdeas] = useState(null);
  const saved = outfitsOf(profile.id).filter((o) => wardrobe === 'all' || (wardrobe === 'fav' ? o.favorite : o.wardrobe === wardrobe));
  const wardrobes = state.wardrobes.filter((w) => w.profileId === profile.id);
  const isPastOrToday = date <= todayIso();
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';
  const planIt = (slots, name, extra = {}) => { dispatch({ type: 'PLAN_DAY', plan: { date, slots, name, ...extra } }); toast(`Planned for ${nice(date, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}`); };

  const planned = plan && resolve(plan.slots);
  const ctx = plan?.context || contextOf(DEFAULT_CHOICE);
  return (
    <Modal title={nice(date)} onClose={onClose} wide>
      {worn.length > 0 && (
        <div className="notice"><b>Worn this day:</b> {worn.map((h) => Object.values(resolve(h.slots)).filter(Boolean).map((g) => g.name).join(', ')).join(' · ')}</div>
      )}
      {plan ? (
        <div className="day-plan">
          <Stage outfit={planned} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
          <div className="day-plan-body">
            {trip && <span className="trip-pill">{trip.name}</span>}
            <div className="outfit-card-head"><h3 className="h2" style={{ margin: 0 }}>{plan.name}</h3><ScoreBadge score={critique(planned, ctx).overall} /></div>
            <Verdict critique={critique(planned, ctx)} />
            <PieceList outfit={planned} />
            <div className="outfit-card-actions">
              {isPastOrToday && <button className="btn sm accent" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { date, slots: plan.slots, context: plan.context } }); toast('Marked as worn'); }}>I wore it</button>}
              <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'UNPLAN_DAY', date }); }}>Change</button>
              <ConfirmButton label="Remove" confirmLabel="Click to remove" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'UNPLAN_DAY', date }); onClose(); }} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="seg" role="tablist" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
            <button role="tab" aria-selected={tab === 'saved'} className={tab === 'saved' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setTab('saved')}>From saved outfits</button>
            <button role="tab" aria-selected={tab === 'suggest'} className={tab === 'suggest' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setTab('suggest')}>Suggest one</button>
          </div>
          {tab === 'saved' ? (
            <>
              <div className="chips" style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
                <button className={'chip sm' + (wardrobe === 'all' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setWardrobe('all')}>All</button>
                <button className={'chip sm' + (wardrobe === 'fav' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setWardrobe('fav')}>♥ Favorites</button>
                {wardrobes.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip sm' + (wardrobe === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setWardrobe(w.id)}>{w.name}</button>)}
              </div>
              {saved.length === 0 ? <p className="small muted">No saved outfits here yet. Try “Suggest one”.</p> : (
                <div className="pick-grid">
                  {saved.map((o) => (
                    <button key={o.id} className="pick-outfit" onClick={() => { planIt(o.slots, o.name, { outfitId: o.id, context: o.context }); onClose(); }}>
                      <Stage outfit={resolve(o.slots)} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
                      <span className="tiny">{o.name}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <ContextPicker value={choice} onChange={(c) => { setChoice(c); setIdeas(null); }} compact />
              <button className="btn accent" style={{ marginTop: 12 }} onClick={() => setIdeas(generateOutfits(owned, contextOf(choice)))}>Suggest outfits</button>
              {ideas?.missing?.length > 0 && <p className="small">Add {ideas.missing.join(', ')} to the closet first.</p>}
              {ideas?.outfits?.length > 0 && (
                <div className="pick-grid" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
                  {ideas.outfits.map((r, i) => (
                    <button key={i} className="pick-outfit" onClick={() => { planIt(toSlots(r.outfit), r.name, { context: contextOf(choice) }); onClose(); }}>
                      <Stage outfit={r.outfit} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
                      <span className="tiny">{r.name} · {r.critique.overall}</span>
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

function TripPlanner({ onClose }) {
  const { dispatch, owned, profile } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const t0 = todayIso();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [start, setStart] = useState(t0);
  const [end, setEnd] = useState(iso(new Date(t0 + 'T12:00:00').getTime() + 3 * 86400000));
  const [occasion, setOccasion] = useState('vacation');
  const [weather, setWeather] = useState('warm');
  const [plan, setPlan] = useState(null);
  const [packedOff, setPackedOff] = useState({});
  const dates = end >= start ? daysBetween(start, end) : [];
  const ctx = useMemo(() => contextOf({ ...DEFAULT_CHOICE, occasion, weather }), [occasion, weather]);
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';

  function save() {
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_TRIP', trip: { name: name.trim() || `Trip ${nice(start, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}`, start, end, occasion, weather }, days: plan.days.map((d) => ({ date: d.date, slots: toSlots(d.outfit), name: d.name, context: ctx })) });
    toast(`Trip planned: ${plan.days.length} outfits on your calendar`);
    onClose();
  }

  return (
    <Modal title="Plan a trip" onClose={onClose} wide>
      {!plan ? (
        <div className="trip-form">
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Trip name</label><input className="input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Baguio weekend" /></div>
          <div className="field-row">
            <div className="field"><label className="label">From</label><input type="date" className="input" value={start} onChange={(e) => setStart(e.target.value)} /></div>
            <div className="field"><label className="label">To</label><input type="date" className="input" value={end} min={start} onChange={(e) => setEnd(e.target.value)} /></div>
          </div>
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Mostly for</label>
            <div className="chips">{OCCASIONS.map((o) => <button key={o.id} className={'chip sm' + (occasion === o.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setOccasion(o.id)}>{o.label}</button>)}</div></div>
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Expected weather</label>
            <div className="chips">{WEATHER_CHOICES.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip sm' + (weather === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setWeather(w.id)}>{w.label}</button>)}</div></div>
          <button className="btn accent" disabled={!dates.length} onClick={() => setPlan(planTrip(owned, ctx, dates))}>{dates.length ? `Plan ${dates.length} day${dates.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}` : 'Pick valid dates'}</button>
          {dates.length >= 31 && <span className="tiny">Trips are planned up to 31 days.</span>}
        </div>
      ) : plan.missing.length || !plan.days.length ? (
        <div className="empty"><p className="small">{plan.missing.length ? `Add ${plan.missing.join(', ')} to the closet first.` : plan.note}</p><button className="btn" onClick={() => setPlan(null)}>Back</button></div>
      ) : (
        <div className="trip-result">
          {plan.note && <div className="notice">{plan.note}</div>}
          <div className="trip-days">
            {plan.days.map((d) => (
              <div key={d.date} className="trip-day">
                <span className="tiny">{nice(d.date, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</span>
                <Stage outfit={d.outfit} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
                <span className="tiny trip-day-name">{d.name}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <h3 className="h2" style={{ marginTop: 18 }}>Packing list · {plan.packing.length} pieces</h3>
          <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Pieces worn on several days are re-used on purpose to keep the bag light.</p>
          <ul className="packing">
            {plan.packing.map(({ item, days }) => (
              <li key={item.id}><label><input type="checkbox" checked={!!packedOff[item.id]} onChange={() => setPackedOff((x) => ({ ...x, [item.id]: !x[item.id] }))} /> <span>{item.name}</span><span className="tiny">{days} day{days > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span></label></li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn accent" onClick={save}>Add to calendar</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setPlan(planTrip(owned, ctx, dates, plan.days))}>Try other outfits</button>
            <button className="btn ghost" onClick={() => setPlan(null)}>Change trip details</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

export function TripCard({ trip }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, profile } = useStore();
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const plans = state.plans.filter((p) => p.tripId === trip.id).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date));
  const counts = new Map();
  for (const p of plans) for (const id of Object.values(p.slots)) if (id) counts.set(id, (counts.get(id) || 0) + 1);
  const items = [...counts.entries()].map(([id, n]) => ({ g: state.garments.find((x) => x.id === id), n })).filter((x) => x.g);
  return (
    <div className="trip-card">
      <div className="trip-card-head">
        <div><b>{trip.name}</b><div className="tiny">{nice(trip.start, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })} – {nice(trip.end, { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })} · {plans.length} outfits · {items.length} pieces to pack</div></div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
          <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} aria-expanded={open}>{open ? 'Hide' : 'Packing list'}</button>
          <ConfirmButton label="Delete" confirmLabel="Delete trip and its plans?" onConfirm={() => dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_TRIP', id: trip.id })} />
        </div>
      </div>
      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="trip-days">{plans.map((p) => <div key={p.id} className="trip-day"><span className="tiny">{nice(p.date, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric' })}</span><Stage outfit={resolve(p.slots)} mode="flatlay" compact child={profile.kind === 'child'} /></div>)}</div>
          <ul className="packing">{items.map(({ g, n }) => <li key={g.id}><label><input type="checkbox" /> <span>{g.name}</span><span className="tiny">{n} day{n > 1 ? 's' : ''}</span></label></li>)}</ul>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Calendar({ go }) {
  const { state, resolve, profile, historyOf } = useStore();
  const now = new Date();
  const [ym, setYm] = useState({ y: now.getFullYear(), m: now.getMonth() });
  const [day, setDay] = useState(null);
  const [tripOpen, setTripOpen] = useState(false);
  const cells = monthGrid(ym.y, ym.m);
  const plans = new Map(state.plans.filter((p) => p.profileId === profile.id).map((p) => [p.date, p]));
  const worn = new Set(historyOf(profile.id).map((h) => h.date));
  const trips = state.trips.filter((t) => t.profileId === profile.id).sort((a, b) => a.start.localeCompare(b.start));
  const tripOf = (date) => trips.find((t) => date >= t.start && date <= t.end);
  const t0 = todayIso();
  const shift = (k) => setYm(({ y, m }) => { const d = new Date(y, m + k, 1); return { y: d.getFullYear(), m: d.getMonth() }; });
  const upcoming = [...plans.values()].filter((p) => p.date >= t0).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).slice(0, 5);

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'Your' : `${profile.name}'s`} outfit calendar</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Calendar</h1>
          <p className="small muted" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Tap a day to plan what to wear. Planning a trip fills every day and makes a packing list.</p>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/history')}>Outfit history</button>
          <button className="btn accent" onClick={() => setTripOpen(true)}>✈ Plan a trip</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="cal">
        <div className="cal-head">
          <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => shift(-1)} aria-label="Previous month">‹</button>
          <h2 className="h2">{new Date(ym.y, ym.m, 1).toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}</h2>
          <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => shift(1)} aria-label="Next month">›</button>
          {(ym.y !== now.getFullYear() || ym.m !== now.getMonth()) && <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setYm({ y: now.getFullYear(), m: now.getMonth() })}>Today</button>}
        </div>
        <div className="cal-grid">
          {WEEK.map((w) => <div key={w} className="cal-wd" aria-hidden="true">{w}</div>)}
          {cells.map((c) => {
            const p = plans.get(c.date);
            const trip = tripOf(c.date);
            return (
              <button key={c.date} className={'cal-cell' + (c.inMonth ? '' : ' out') + (c.date === t0 ? ' today' : '') + (p ? ' has-plan' : '') + (c.date < t0 ? ' past' : '')}
                onClick={() => setDay(c.date)} aria-label={`${nice(c.date)}${p ? ', planned: ' + p.name : ''}${worn.has(c.date) ? ', worn' : ''}`}>
                <span className="cal-num">{c.day}</span>
                {trip && <span className="cal-trip" title={trip.name}>{c.date === trip.start || c.day === 1 ? trip.name : ''}</span>}
                {p && <span className="cal-thumb"><Stage outfit={resolve(p.slots)} mode="flatlay" compact child={profile.kind === 'child'} /></span>}
                {p && <span className="cal-dot" aria-hidden="true" />}
                {worn.has(c.date) && <span className="cal-worn" title="Worn">✓</span>}
              </button>
            );
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="cal-legend tiny"><span><i className="cal-dot" /> planned</span><span><b className="cal-worn">✓</b> worn</span><span><i className="cal-trip-key" /> trip</span></div>
      </div>

      {upcoming.length > 0 && (
        <section style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>
          <h2 className="h2">Coming up</h2>
          <ul className="upcoming">
            {upcoming.map((p) => <li key={p.id}><button onClick={() => setDay(p.date)}><b>{nice(p.date, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</b><span>{p.name}</span></button></li>)}
          </ul>
        </section>
      )}
      {trips.length > 0 && (
        <section style={{ marginTop: 24 }}>
          <h2 className="h2">Trips</h2>
          <div className="trip-list">{trips.map((t) => <TripCard key={t.id} trip={t} />)}</div>
        </section>
      )}
      {day && <DayPanel date={day} onClose={() => setDay(null)} />}
      {tripOpen && <TripPlanner onClose={() => setTripOpen(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Closet.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { ProductCard, Modal, Meter, OutfitThumb, ConfirmButton, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { PieceAiPhoto, BulkAiPhotos } from '../components/AiPhoto.jsx';
import { usePhotos } from '../data/photos.js';
import GarmentArt, { photoOf } from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import UploadModal from '../components/UploadModal.jsx';
import ItemForm, { STATUSES, LocationPicker, FIELD_LABEL } from '../components/ItemForm.jsx';
import { CATEGORIES, STYLES, OCCASIONS, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';
import { NAMED_COLORS } from '../ai/color.js';
import { wearStats } from '../ai/insights.js';

const SEASONS = ['spring', 'summer', 'autumn', 'winter'];
const cap = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);

function ItemDetail({ item: opened, onClose, go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, get } = useStore();
  const item = get(opened.id) || opened; // live, so an AI photo made here shows straight away
  const toast = useToast();
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null);
  const usedIn = state.outfits.filter((o) => Object.values(o.slots).includes(item.id));
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const status = STATUSES.find((s) => s.id === (item.status || 'available'));
  if (editing) {
    return (
      <Modal title={`Edit · ${item.name}`} onClose={onClose} wide>
        <div className="drawer-detail">
          <div className="card-stage"><GarmentArt item={editing} /></div>
          <div>
            <ItemForm item={editing} onChange={setEditing} />
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 10 }}>
              <button className="btn primary" style={{ flex: 1 }} disabled={!editing.name.trim()} onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { ...editing, name: editing.name.trim() } }); setEditing(null); onClose(); toast('Changes saved'); }}>Save changes</button>
              <button className="btn" onClick={() => setEditing(null)}>Cancel</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Modal>
    );
  }
  return (
    <Modal title={item.name} onClose={onClose} wide>
      <div className="drawer-detail">
        <div>
          <div className="card-stage"><GarmentArt item={item} />{photoOf(item, photos).ai && <span className="card-flag ai">AI photo</span>}</div>
          <PieceAiPhoto item={item} />
        </div>
        <div>
          <span className="label">{item.demo ? 'Demo item · sample data' : item.uploaded ? 'Added from your photo' : item.manual ? 'Added by hand' : 'In the closet'} · {status.label}{item.tagSource === 'ai' ? ' · details suggested by Gemini' : item.tagSource === 'device' ? ' · details guessed on this device' : ''}</span>
          {item.unverified?.length > 0 && (
            <div className="check-banner" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>
              <span className="small">Not checked yet: <b>{item.unverified.map((k) => FIELD_LABEL[k] || k).join(', ')}</b>. These are guesses and may be wrong.</span>
              <span style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
                <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setEditing({ ...item })}>Check now</button>
                <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: item.id, unverified: [] } }); onClose(); toast('Marked as checked'); }}>Looks right</button>
              </span>
            </div>
          )}
          <div className="rows">
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Category</span><span>{CATEGORIES.find((c) => c.id === item.category)?.label} · {item.art}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Color</span><span><span className="swatch" style={{ background: item.colors[0].hex, verticalAlign: 'middle', marginRight: 8 }} />{item.colors.map((c) => c.name).join(' / ')}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Material</span><span>{item.material || <span className="muted">not set</span>} · {item.weight} weight</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Fit</span><span>{cap(item.fit || 'regular')}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Warmth</span><span><Meter value={item.warmth} /></span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Rain-ready</span><span><Meter value={item.water || 1} /></span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Formality</span><span><Meter value={item.formality} /></span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Seasons</span><span>{(item.seasons || []).map(cap).join(' / ')}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Kept in</span><span className="loc-row"><LocationPicker value={item.location} onChange={(v) => { dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: item.id, location: v } }); toast(v ? `Kept in ${state.locations.find((l) => l.id === v)?.name || 'the new place'}` : 'Location cleared'); }} /></span></div>
            {item.pattern && item.pattern !== 'solid' && <div className="row"><span className="label">Pattern</span><span>{cap(item.pattern)}</span></div>}
            {item.style && <div className="row"><span className="label">Style</span><span>{STYLES.find((s) => s.id === item.style)?.label || item.style}</span></div>}
            {item.occasions?.length > 0 && <div className="row"><span className="label">Good for</span><span>{item.occasions.map((o) => OCCASIONS.find((x) => x.id === o)?.label || o).join(', ')}</span></div>}
          </div>
          <div className="chips" style={{ marginTop: 14 }} role="group" aria-label="Status">
            {STATUSES.map((s) => <button key={s.id} className={'chip' + ((item.status || 'available') === s.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_GARMENT', garment: { id: item.id, status: s.id } }); onClose(); toast(`Marked: ${s.label.toLowerCase()}`); }}>{s.label}</button>)}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 14, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn accent" style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={() => { onClose(); go('#/style/' + encodeURIComponent(item.id)); }}>Style me with this</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setEditing({ ...item })}>Edit details</button>
            <ConfirmButton className="btn" label="Delete" confirmLabel="Click again to delete" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_GARMENT', id: item.id }); onClose(); toast('Deleted from the closet'); }} />
          </div>
          {usedIn.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="label" style={{ marginTop: 22 }}>In {usedIn.length} saved look{usedIn.length > 1 ? 's' : ''}</div>
              <div style={{ display: 'grid', gridTemplateColumns: 'repeat(4, 1fr)', gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
                {usedIn.slice(0, 4).map((o) => <div key={o.id} title={o.name}><OutfitThumb outfit={o} resolve={resolve} /></div>)}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function Closet({ go, initial = '' }) {
  const [initKind, initVal] = initial.split(':');
  const { state, dispatch, owned, profile, hasDemo } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [cat, setCat] = useState(initKind === 'category' ? initVal : 'all');
  const [season, setSeason] = useState(null);
  const [color, setColor] = useState(initKind === 'color' ? initVal : '');
  const [style, setStyle] = useState('');
  const [occasion, setOccasion] = useState('');
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('');
  const [place, setPlace] = useState('');
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  const [sort, setSort] = useState('newest');
  const stats = useMemo(() => wearStats(state.history.filter((h) => h.profileId === profile.id)), [state.history, profile.id]);
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState(null);
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const [justAdded, setJustAdded] = useState([]);
  const who = profile.name === 'Me' ? 'My' : `${profile.name}'s`;

  const list = useMemo(() => owned.filter((g) =>
    (cat === 'all' || g.category === cat)
    && (!season || (g.seasons || []).includes(season))
    && (!color || g.colors.some((c) => c.name === color))
    && (!style || g.style === style)
    && (!occasion || (g.occasions || []).includes(occasion))
    && (!status || (g.status || 'available') === status)
    && (!place || (place === 'none' ? !g.location : g.location === place))
    && (!q.trim() || q.toLowerCase().trim().split(/\s+/).every((w) => [g.name, g.material, g.art, g.category, g.style, g.pattern, ...(g.colors || []).map((c) => c.name), state.locations.find((l) => l.id === g.location)?.name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(w))))
    .sort((a, b) => sort === 'name' ? a.name.localeCompare(b.name)
      : sort === 'most' ? (stats[b.id]?.wears || 0) - (stats[a.id]?.wears || 0)
      : sort === 'least' ? (stats[a.id]?.wears || 0) - (stats[b.id]?.wears || 0)
      : 0), [owned, cat, season, color, style, occasion, status, place, q, sort, stats, state.locations]);

  const counts = Object.fromEntries(CATEGORIES.map((c) => [c.id, owned.filter((g) => g.category === c.id).length]));
  const colorsHere = NAMED_COLORS.filter((c) => owned.some((g) => g.colors.some((x) => x.name === c.name)));
  const filtered = season || color || style || occasion || status || place || q;

  function saved(items) {
    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_GARMENTS', garments: items });
    setUploading(false);
    setJustAdded(items.map((g) => g.id));
    toast(items.length > 1 ? `${items.length} pieces added` : `${items[0].name} added`);
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.kind === 'child' ? 'Child\'s wardrobe' : 'Wardrobe'}</div>
          <h1 className="h1">{who} Closet <span className="muted" style={{ fontWeight: 400 }}>· {owned.length} pieces</span></h1>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
          {owned.length > 0 && <button className="btn ghost" onClick={() => go('#/insights')}>Insights</button>}
          {owned.length > 0 && <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Create an Outfit</button>}
          <button className="btn accent" onClick={() => setUploading(true)}>+ Add clothes</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      {owned.some((g) => !g.demo) && <BulkAiPhotos items={owned.filter((g) => !g.demo)} />}

      {hasDemo && owned.some((g) => g.demo) && (
        <div className="notice demo-banner">
          <span>Demo items (labelled “Demo”) are sample data for trying the app. They're not your clothes.</span>
          <ConfirmButton className="btn sm" label="Remove demo items" confirmLabel="Click again to remove" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_DEMO' }); toast('Demo items removed'); }} />
        </div>
      )}

      {owned.length > 0 && (
        <div className="toolbar">
          <div className="search-row">
            <div className="search-box">
              <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="16" height="16" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" strokeWidth="1.8" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" /><path d="m20 20-3.5-3.5" /></svg>
              <input type="search" value={q} onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search: “navy linen”, “boots”, “under-bed”…" aria-label="Search the closet" />
            </div>
            <select className="select sm" value={sort} onChange={(e) => setSort(e.target.value)} aria-label="Sort">
              <option value="newest">Newest first</option><option value="name">A–Z</option><option value="most">Most worn</option><option value="least">Least worn</option>
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Category">
            <button className={'chip' + (cat === 'all' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setCat('all')}>All</button>
            {CATEGORIES.map((c) => <button key={c.id} className={'chip' + (cat === c.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setCat(c.id)}>{c.label} {counts[c.id] ? <span style={{ opacity: .6 }}>&nbsp;{counts[c.id]}</span> : null}</button>)}
          </div>
          <div className="filters" role="group" aria-label="Filters">
            <select className="select sm" value={color} onChange={(e) => setColor(e.target.value)} aria-label="Color"><option value="">Any color</option>{colorsHere.map((c) => <option key={c.name}>{c.name}</option>)}</select>
            <select className="select sm" value={style} onChange={(e) => setStyle(e.target.value)} aria-label="Style"><option value="">Any style</option>{STYLES.map((s) => <option key={s.id} value={s.id}>{s.label}</option>)}</select>
            <select className="select sm" value={occasion} onChange={(e) => setOccasion(e.target.value)} aria-label="Occasion"><option value="">Any occasion</option>{OCCASIONS.map((o) => <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>{o.label}</option>)}</select>
            <select className="select sm" value={season || ''} onChange={(e) => setSeason(e.target.value || null)} aria-label="Season"><option value="">Any season</option>{SEASONS.map((x) => <option key={x} value={x}>{cap(x)}</option>)}</select>
            <select className="select sm" value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)} aria-label="Status"><option value="">Any status</option>{STATUSES.map((x) => <option key={x.id} value={x.id}>{x.label}</option>)}</select>
            {state.locations.length > 0 && <select className="select sm" value={place} onChange={(e) => setPlace(e.target.value)} aria-label="Where it's kept"><option value="">Anywhere</option>{state.locations.map((l) => <option key={l.id} value={l.id}>{l.name}</option>)}<option value="none">No place set</option></select>}
            {filtered && <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={() => { setSeason(null); setColor(''); setStyle(''); setOccasion(''); setStatus(''); setPlace(''); setQ(''); }}>Clear</button>}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {owned.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <p className="h2" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>{who === 'My' ? 'Your' : who} closet is empty.</p>
          <p className="small">Add photos (several at once is fine) or add pieces by hand. Outfit suggestions only use what's in here.</p>
          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setUploading(true)}>+ Add clothes</button>
            {!hasDemo && <button className="btn" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DEMO' }); toast('Demo closet added'); }}>Try the demo closet</button>}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : list.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty"><p className="small">Nothing matches{q ? ` “${q}”` : ' these filters'}.</p><button className="btn sm" onClick={() => { setSeason(null); setColor(''); setStyle(''); setOccasion(''); setStatus(''); setPlace(''); setQ(''); setCat('all'); }}>Show everything</button></div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid">
          {list.map((g) => (
            <ProductCard key={g.id} item={g} place={state.locations.find((l) => l.id === g.location)?.name} flag={justAdded.includes(g.id) ? 'New' : null} onClick={() => setDetail(g)}
              action={<button className="btn primary sm block" onClick={() => go('#/style/' + encodeURIComponent(g.id))}>Style me with this</button>} />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {detail && <ItemDetail item={detail} onClose={() => setDetail(null)} go={go} />}
      {uploading && <UploadModal onClose={() => setUploading(false)} onSaved={saved} ownerName={profile.name} />}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Create.jsx
====================================================================================================

// Create an Outfit: choose → get 3 outfits → compare → save.
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import { StylistLoader } from '../components/Motion.jsx';
import { ContextPicker, OutfitCard, Compare, DEFAULT_CHOICE, contextOf, toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { styleWithAI } from '../ai/stylist.js';
import { isAvailable } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { useClaudeStatus, puterSignIn } from '../ai/claude.js';
import { CATEGORY_LABEL } from '../data/closet.js';

export function MissingNotice({ missing, go, name }) {
  const { dispatch, hasDemo } = useStore();
  return (
    <div className="empty">
      <p className="h2" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>{name ? `${name}'s closet` : 'Your closet'} needs a few more pieces first.</p>
      <p className="small">To build a complete outfit, add at least: <b>{missing.join(', ')}</b>. Suggestions only ever use clothes that are really in the closet.</p>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, justifyContent: 'center', flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => go('#/closet')}>Add clothes</button>
        {!hasDemo && <button className="btn" onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DEMO' })}>Try the demo closet</button>}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Create({ go, autoRun = false }) {
  const { state, dispatch, owned, profile, personal } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(() => state.draft.choice || DEFAULT_CHOICE);
  const [anchorId, setAnchorId] = useState('');
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [res, setRes] = useState(null);       // { ctx, outfits, note, error, missing, unavailable }
  const [comparing, setComparing] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState({});     // index → outfit id
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(true);
  const resultsRef = useRef(null);
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';

  useEffect(() => { if (autoRun) { run(); window.history.replaceState(null, '', '#/create'); } }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const anchorChoices = owned.filter(isAvailable);
  const anchor = anchorChoices.find((g) => g.id === anchorId) || null;

  async function run() {
    const ctx = { ...contextOf(choice), personal: personal.rated ? personal : null };
    setBusy(true); setRes(null); setComparing(false); setSaved({});
    try {
      const [r] = await Promise.all([styleWithAI({ ctx, pool: owned, anchor }), new Promise((ok) => setTimeout(ok, 650))]); // let the reveal animation breathe
      setRes({ ctx, ...r });
      if (r.outfits?.length) {
        setFormOpen(false);
        setTimeout(() => resultsRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: window.matchMedia?.('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth', block: 'start' }), 60);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setRes({ ctx, outfits: [], error: 'Something went wrong while building outfits. Please try again.' });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  function save(i, favorite = false) {
    const r = res.outfits[i];
    const id = 'o-' + Date.now().toString(36) + i;
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', outfit: { id, name: r.name, slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: res.ctx, stage: { mode: 'flatlay' }, favorite, why: r.why, source: r.source || 'rules' } });
    setSaved((s) => ({ ...s, [i]: id }));
    toast(favorite ? 'Saved as a favorite' : 'Saved to Saved Outfits');
  }

  function open(i) {
    const r = res.outfits[i];
    dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: toSlots(r.outfit), name: r.name, context: res.ctx, choice } });
    go('#/builder');
  }

  const replace = (i, next) => setRes((x) => ({ ...x, outfits: x.outfits.map((o, k) => (k === i ? next : o)) }));

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'Your closet' : `${profile.name}'s closet`} · {owned.length} pieces</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Create an Outfit</h1>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!formOpen && res ? (
        <div className="ctx-summary">
          <span className="small"><b>{res.ctx.occasionLabel}</b> · {res.ctx.weather} {res.ctx.tempC}°C{choice.style ? ` · ${choice.style}` : ''}{choice.prefs.length ? ` · ${choice.prefs.join(', ')}` : ''}{anchor ? ` · with the ${anchor.name}` : ''}</span>
          <span style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
            <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setFormOpen(true)}>Change</button>
            <button className="btn sm accent" onClick={run} disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Styling…' : 'New ideas'}</button>
          </span>
        </div>
      ) : (
      <div className="create-form">
        <ContextPicker value={choice} onChange={setChoice} />
        <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Wear this piece <span className="muted">(optional)</span></span>
          <div className="anchor-row">
            {anchor && <span className="mini" style={{ width: 40 }}><GarmentArt item={anchor} /></span>}
            <select className="select" value={anchorId} onChange={(e) => setAnchorId(e.target.value)} aria-label="Piece to include">
              <option value="">No, surprise me</option>
              {anchorChoices.map((g) => <option key={g.id} value={g.id}>{CATEGORY_LABEL[g.category]} · {g.name}</option>)}
            </select>
          </div></div>
        <div className="create-go">
          <button className="btn accent" onClick={run} disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Styling…' : res ? 'Get 3 new outfits' : 'Get 3 outfits'}</button>
          <span className="tiny">
            {ai.status === 'ready' ? `${ai.name} picks the outfits from the details of your clothes (type, colors, fabric, warmth…), not by looking at your photos; the rules engine checks every one before you see it.`
              : ai.status === 'signin' ? <>Gemini is off until you sign in to Puter (free). The rules engine builds the outfits meanwhile. <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => puterSignIn().catch(() => toast('Sign-in was closed'))}>Sign in for Gemini</button></>
              : 'The AI stylist is not connected here, so the rules engine builds the outfits.'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>
      )}
      {busy && <StylistLoader count={owned.filter(isAvailable).length} />}

      {res && !busy && (
        <>
          {res.missing?.length > 0 && <MissingNotice missing={res.missing} go={go} name={profile.name === 'Me' ? '' : profile.name} />}
          {res.unavailable && <div className="notice">The {res.unavailable.name} isn't available right now (marked as {res.unavailable.status}). Pick another piece, or mark it available in the closet.</div>}
          {res.error && <div className="notice warn">{res.error}</div>}
          {res.note && <div className="notice">{res.note}</div>}
          {res.ctx.personal && <p className="tiny">Personalized with {res.ctx.personal.rated} rated outfit{res.ctx.personal.rated > 1 ? 's' : ''} from your history.</p>}
          {res.outfits.length > 0 && (
            <>
              <div className="results-head" ref={resultsRef}>
                <h2 className="h2">{res.outfits.length === 1 ? 'Your outfit' : `${res.outfits.length} outfits for you`}</h2>
                {res.outfits.length > 1 && !comparing && <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setComparing(!comparing)}>{comparing ? 'Back to cards' : `Compare ${res.outfits.length} side by side`}</button>}
              </div>
              {comparing ? (
                <Compare results={res.outfits} ctx={res.ctx} child={child} onClose={() => setComparing(false)}
                  savedIdx={Object.keys(saved).map(Number)} onPick={(i) => save(i, true)} />
              ) : (
                <div className="outfit-grid">
                  {res.outfits.map((r, i) => (
                    <OutfitCard key={i} index={i} result={r} ctx={res.ctx} pool={owned} child={child} saved={!!saved[i]} savedId={saved[i]}
                      onReplace={(n) => { replace(i, n); setSaved((s) => { const x = { ...s }; delete x[i]; return x; }); }}
                      onSave={() => save(i)} onOpen={() => open(i)}
                      onWore={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: res.ctx } }); toast('Added to Outfit History. Rate it there later.'); }} />
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/DressingRoom.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { OutfitAiLook, outfitPieces } from '../components/AiPhoto.jsx';
import { useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import SaveModal from '../components/SaveModal.jsx';
import { Meter, ScoreBadge, Verdict, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { critique, isDress } from '../ai/critic.js';
import { critiqueWithClaude } from '../ai/stylist.js';
import { useClaudeStatus } from '../ai/claude.js';
import { alternatives, isAvailable } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { SCENES } from '../data/scenes.jsx';
import { SLOTS, SLOT_LABEL, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';
import { ContextPicker, DEFAULT_CHOICE, contextOf, toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';

const cap = (s) => s.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + s.slice(1);

export default function DressingRoom({ go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, pool, profile } = useStore();
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';
  const toast = useToast();
  const { draft } = state;
  const outfit = useMemo(() => resolve(draft.slots), [draft.slots, resolve]);
  const ctx = draft.context;
  const [openSlot, setOpenSlot] = useState(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState({ proportions: true, fabric: true, color: true, occasion: true, finish: true });
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(draft.choice || DEFAULT_CHOICE);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [showCtx, setShowCtx] = useState(false);
  const bgRef = useRef();

  const rules = useMemo(() => critique(outfit, ctx), [outfit, ctx]);
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const lookKey = JSON.stringify([draft.slots, ctx.prompt]);
  const [aiCrit, setAiCrit] = useState(() => (state.aiCritiques?.[lookKey] ? { key: lookKey, critique: state.aiCritiques[lookKey] } : null));
  const [aiBusy, setAiBusy] = useState(false);
  const [aiErr, setAiErr] = useState('');
  const [source, setSource] = useState('claude');
  const cached = aiCrit?.key === lookKey ? aiCrit.critique : state.aiCritiques?.[lookKey] || null;
  const result = cached && source === 'claude' ? cached : rules;
  async function askClaude() {
    setAiBusy(true); setAiErr('');
    const r = await critiqueWithClaude(outfit, ctx, rules);
    setAiBusy(false);
    if (r?.critique) { setAiCrit({ key: lookKey, critique: r.critique }); setSource('claude'); dispatch({ type: 'CACHE_CRITIQUE', key: lookKey, critique: r.critique }); }
    else setAiErr(r?.error || 'The AI stylist is not available here, so the rules engine critique is shown.');
  }
  const complete = !!(outfit.top && (outfit.bottom || isDress(outfit.top)) && outfit.shoes);
  const alts = useMemo(() => (complete ? alternatives(outfit, ctx, pool).slice(0, 5) : []), [complete, outfit, ctx, pool]);
  const stage = draft.stage;

  const choices = openSlot ? pool.filter((g) => slotOf(g) === openSlot) : [];
  const pick = (slot, g) => dispatch({ type: 'SET_SLOT', slot, id: g?.id || null, isDress: isDress(g), topIsDress: isDress(outfit.top) });

  function applyChoice(c) {
    setChoice(c);
    dispatch({ type: 'SET_CONTEXT', context: contextOf(c), choice: c });
  }
  function saveOutfit() {
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', name: draft.name || 'Untitled look' });
    toast('Saved to Saved Outfits');
  }

  function uploadBg(file) {
    if (!file) return;
    const r = new FileReader();
    r.onload = () => dispatch({ type: 'SET_STAGE', stage: { mode: 'scene', customBg: r.result } });
    r.readAsDataURL(file);
  }

  const sceneName = stage.customBg ? 'Your background' : SCENES.find((s) => s.id === stage.scene)?.name;

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="dressing">
        {/* ───────── LEFT: visual stage ───────── */}
        <div className="dressing-left">
          <div className="stage-tools">
            <div className="seg" role="tablist" aria-label="Stage mode">
              {[['flatlay', 'Flat-lay'], ['mannequin', 'Mannequin'], ['scene', 'Scene'], ['person', 'Your photo']].map(([m, l]) => (
                <button key={m} role="tab" aria-selected={stage.mode === m} className={stage.mode === m ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'SET_STAGE', stage: { mode: m } })}>{l}</button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <span className="tiny">4:5 · {stage.mode === 'scene' ? sceneName : stage.mode === 'mannequin' ? 'Studio mannequin' : stage.mode === 'person' ? 'Your photo + the outfit beside it' : 'Studio flat-lay'}</span>
          </div>

          <Stage outfit={outfit} mode={stage.mode} sceneId={stage.scene} customBg={stage.customBg} personPhoto={stage.personPhoto}
            child={child}
            caption={stage.mode === 'scene' && !stage.customBg ? 'Illustrated backdrop · not a photo' : stage.mode === 'mannequin' ? (child ? "Child's mannequin · a styling guide, not a try-on" : 'Mannequin · a styling guide, not a virtual try-on') : stage.mode === 'person' ? 'Not a try-on: the pieces are shown beside the photo' : null} />
          {(stage.mode === 'mannequin' || stage.mode === 'scene') && <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>Illustrated preview. Colors are taken from each item; uploaded photos are shown as they are, never recolored.</p>}
          <OutfitAiLook pieces={outfitPieces(outfit)} />

          {stage.mode === 'person' && (
            <div className="context-box" style={{ marginTop: 12, alignItems: 'center', justifyContent: 'space-between' }}>
              <span className="small muted">Use a full-length photo of yourself, or a model you'd like to dress like. It stays on this device.{!stage.personPhoto ? ' Until then, the default model photo (if one is set) is shown.' : ''}</span>
              <label className="btn sm primary" style={{ cursor: 'pointer', whiteSpace: 'nowrap' }}>{stage.personPhoto ? 'Change photo' : 'Add a photo'}
                <input type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={(e) => { const f = e.target.files[0]; if (!f) return; const r = new FileReader(); r.onload = () => dispatch({ type: 'SET_STAGE', stage: { personPhoto: r.result } }); r.readAsDataURL(f); }} />
              </label>
            </div>
          )}

          {stage.mode === 'scene' && (
            <>
              <div className="scene-row">
                {SCENES.filter((s) => s.id !== 'studio').map((s) => (
                  <button key={s.id} className={'scene-thumb' + (stage.scene === s.id && !stage.customBg ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'SET_STAGE', stage: { scene: s.id, customBg: null } })}>
                    <svg viewBox="0 0 400 500" preserveAspectRatio="xMidYMid slice">{s.render()}</svg><span>{s.name}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
                <button className={'scene-thumb' + (stage.customBg ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => bgRef.current.click()}>
                  <div className="thumb-box">+</div><span>Upload your own</span>
                </button>
                <input ref={bgRef} type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={(e) => uploadBg(e.target.files[0])} />
              </div>
            </>
          )}

          <div className="slots">
            {SLOTS.map((s) => (
              <button key={s} className={'slot' + (openSlot === s ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setOpenSlot(openSlot === s ? null : s)} aria-expanded={openSlot === s}>
                <div className="slot-art">{outfit[s] ? <GarmentArt item={outfit[s]} /> : <span className="slot-empty">+</span>}</div>
                <span className="label">{s === 'top' && isDress(outfit.top) ? 'Dress' : SLOT_LABEL[s]}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {openSlot && (
            <div className="picker">
              <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center', marginBottom: 10, gap: 10 }}>
                <span className="label">Choose {SLOT_LABEL[openSlot].toLowerCase()}</span>
                <span className="tiny">{choices.length} in this closet</span>
              </div>
              <div className="picker-grid">
                <button className={'pick' + (!outfit[openSlot] ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => pick(openSlot, null)}>
                  <div className="pick-art"><span className="slot-empty">∅</span></div><span>None</span>
                </button>
                {choices.map((g) => (
                  <button key={g.id} className={'pick' + (outfit[openSlot]?.id === g.id ? ' on' : '') + (isAvailable(g) ? '' : ' unavailable')} onClick={() => pick(openSlot, g)}>
                    <div className="pick-art"><GarmentArt item={g} /></div>
                    {!isAvailable(g) && <span className="basic-tag">{g.status}</span>}
                    <span>{g.name}</span>
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
              {choices.length === 0 && <p className="tiny">Nothing in this closet for this slot yet. <a href="#/closet">Add some</a>.</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>

        {/* ───────── RIGHT: stylist spec sheet ───────── */}
        <div>
          <div className="label">Outfit builder{profile.name !== 'Me' ? ` · ${profile.name}` : ''}</div>
          <div className="spec-title">
            <input value={draft.name} placeholder="Untitled look" onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'SET_NAME', name: e.target.value })} aria-label="Look name" />
          </div>

          <div className="rows">
            <div className="row"><span className="label">For</span>
              <div>
                <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setShowCtx(!showCtx)} aria-expanded={showCtx}>{ctx.prompt} · change</button>
                {showCtx && <div style={{ marginTop: 10 }}><ContextPicker value={choice} onChange={applyChoice} compact /></div>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Reading</span><span className="small">{ctx.weather} · {ctx.tempC}°C{ctx.rain >= 0.6 ? ' · rain likely' : ''} · {ctx.occasionLabel}{ctx.city ? ` · ${ctx.city}` : ''}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Season</span><span>{result.seasons.length ? result.seasons.map(cap).join(' / ') : <span className="muted">Mixed-season pieces</span>}</span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Weather</span>
              <span><Meter value={result.weatherMeter} /><span className="small">{['', 'Poor', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Excellent'][result.weatherMeter]} · warmth {result.warmth.have} / ~{result.warmth.need} needed</span></span></div>
            <div className="row"><span className="label">Formality</span>
              <span><Meter value={result.formalityMeter} /><span className="small">{result.formalityLabel} (target: {ctx.occasionLabel.toLowerCase()})</span></span></div>
          </div>

          <div className="critique-head">
            <div>
              <span className="ai-badge">{result.by === 'claude' ? `AI stylist · ${result.provider || ai.name || 'AI'}` : 'Rules engine'}</span>
              <h3 className="h2" style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>Critique</h3>
            </div>
            <ScoreBadge score={result.overall} />
          </div>
          {complete ? <Verdict critique={result} /> : <p className="small muted">Add a top and bottom (or a dress) and shoes for an honest verdict.</p>}
          <div className="ai-bar">
            {ai.status === 'ready' ? (
              <>
                <button className="btn sm primary" onClick={askClaude} disabled={aiBusy}>{aiBusy ? 'The AI stylist is thinking…' : cached ? '↻ Ask the AI stylist again' : '✦ Ask the AI stylist'}</button>
                {cached && (
                  <div className="seg">
                    <button className={source === 'claude' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setSource('claude')}>AI stylist</button>
                    <button className={source === 'rules' ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => setSource('rules')}>Rules</button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </>
            ) : (
              <span className="tiny">{ai.status === 'checking' ? 'Connecting to the AI stylist…' : ai.status === 'signin' ? 'Sign in (top right) to get the AI stylist\'s critique. Until then, the rules engine is critiquing.' : (ai.status === 'notsetup' ? 'The AI stylist isn\'t switched on yet, so the built-in rules engine is critiquing.' : 'The AI stylist is not connected here, so the built-in rules engine is critiquing.')}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          {aiBusy && <div className="loading-bar"><i /></div>}
          {aiErr && <p className="small" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)' }}>{aiErr}</p>}
          {result.verdict && <p className="verdict">“{result.verdict}”</p>}
          <div style={{ borderTop: '1px solid var(--ink)' }}>
            {result.pillars.map((p) => (
              <div className="pillar" key={p.id}>
                <button onClick={() => setOpen((o) => ({ ...o, [p.id]: !o[p.id] }))} aria-expanded={open[p.id]}>
                  <span>{p.label}</span>
                  <span className={'bar' + (p.score < 65 ? ' low' : '')}><i style={{ width: p.score + '%' }} /></span>
                  <span className="small" style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>{p.score}</span>
                  <span className="muted">{open[p.id] ? '−' : '+'}</span>
                </button>
                {open[p.id] && (
                  <div className="pillar-body">
                    {p.pros.map((t, i) => <div key={'p' + i} className="finding pro"><span className="mark">✓</span><span>{t}</span></div>)}
                    {p.cons.map((t, i) => <div key={'c' + i} className="finding con"><span className="mark">△</span><span>{t}</span></div>)}
                    {!p.pros.length && !p.cons.length && <div className="small muted">Nothing to flag.</div>}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>

          {result.tip && (
            <div className="swap">
              <div style={{ flex: 1 }}><span className="label">Stylist's tip</span><p>{result.tip}</p></div>
            </div>
          )}

          {!complete && <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>Add a top and bottom (or a dress) and shoes to get alternatives.</p>}
          {complete && (() => {
            const best = alts.filter((a) => a.delta > 0).sort((a, b) => b.delta - a.delta)[0];
            return best ? (
              <button className="improve-btn" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: toSlots(best.outfit) } }); toast(`Improved: ${best.label.toLowerCase()} (+${best.delta})`); }}>
                <span className="spark" aria-hidden="true">✦</span>
                <span><b>Improve this outfit</b><span className="tiny">{best.label} · +{best.delta} points</span></span>
              </button>
            ) : <p className="tiny improve-done">✓ This is already the strongest version of this outfit from your closet. The ideas below change the mood instead.</p>;
          })()}
          {alts.length > 0 && (
            <div className="alts builder-alts">
              <span className="label">Other ideas (from this closet)</span>
              {alts.map((a) => (
                <button key={a.id} className="alt" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: toSlots(a.outfit) } }); toast(a.label); }}>
                  <span>{a.label}</span>
                  <span className={'tiny ' + (a.delta >= 0 ? 'up' : 'down')}>{a.score} ({a.delta >= 0 ? '+' : ''}{a.delta})</span>
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 24, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
            <button className="btn accent" style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={saveOutfit} disabled={!complete}>♡ Save outfit</button>
            <button className="btn" onClick={() => setSaving(true)} disabled={!complete}>Add to lookbook</button>
            <button className="btn" disabled={!complete} onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: draft.slots, context: ctx } }); toast('Added to Outfit History'); }}>I wore this</button>
            <button className="btn ghost" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Create an Outfit</button>
          </div>
          <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>The AI stylist's critique is grounded in Wearwise’s measured facts (warmth, water resistance, formality), so it can add taste and nuance but not contradict the weather math. The alternatives and live score come from the rules engine. AI requests go to Google Gemini through Puter, only after you sign in with the Gemini button at the top (free, no key).</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      {saving && <SaveModal defaultName={draft.name} onClose={() => setSaving(false)} onDone={(t) => { setSaving(false); toast(`Saved to “${t}”`); }} />}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Family.jsx

// Family Looks: coordinated (not identical) outfits, each from that person's own closet.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import { PieceList, ScoreBadge, ConfirmButton, Verdict, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { ContextPicker, DEFAULT_CHOICE, contextOf, toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { familyLook } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { ProfileManager } from './Settings.jsx';

export default function Family({ go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, closetOf, get, resolve } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [ids, setIds] = useState(() => state.profiles.map((p) => p.id));
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState({ ...DEFAULT_CHOICE, occasion: 'church' });
  const [res, setRes] = useState(null);
  const [savedKey, setSavedKey] = useState(null);
  const [managing, setManaging] = useState(false);
  const chosen = state.profiles.filter((p) => ids.includes(p.id));

  function run() {
    const ctx = contextOf(choice);
    const r = familyLook(chosen.map((p) => ({ profile: p, pool: closetOf(p.id) })), ctx);
    setRes({ ...r, ctx }); setSavedKey(null);
  }
  function save() {
    const name = `${res.ctx.occasionLabel} · ${res.members.map((m) => m.profile.name).join(', ')}`;
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_ENSEMBLE', ensemble: { name, context: res.ctx, why: res.why, members: res.members.map((m) => ({ profileId: m.profile.id, slots: toSlots(m.outfit) })) } });
    setSavedKey(JSON.stringify(res.members.map((m) => toSlots(m.outfit))));
    toast('Family look saved');
  }
  const ensembles = state.ensembles;

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">Family looks</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Dress the whole family</h1>
          <p className="small muted" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Each person is dressed only from their own closet. The looks are coordinated in color and formality, not matching.</p>
        </div>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => setManaging(!managing)}>{managing ? 'Done' : 'Manage family members'}</button>
      </div>

      {managing && <div className="create-form"><ProfileManager /></div>}

      <div className="create-form">
        <div className="ctx-row"><span className="label">Who's going?</span>
          <div className="chips">
            {state.profiles.map((p) => (
              <button key={p.id} className={'chip' + (ids.includes(p.id) ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setIds(ids.includes(p.id) ? ids.filter((x) => x !== p.id) : [...ids, p.id])}>
                {p.name}{p.kind === 'child' ? ' · child' : ''} <span style={{ opacity: .6 }}>&nbsp;{closetOf(p.id).length}</span>
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          {state.profiles.length < 2 && <span className="tiny">Add family members (each with their own closet) with “Manage family members”.</span>}
        </div>
        <ContextPicker value={choice} onChange={setChoice} compact />
        <div className="create-go">
          <button className="btn accent" disabled={chosen.length < 2} onClick={run}>{res ? 'Try again' : 'Create family look'}</button>
          {chosen.length < 2 && <span className="tiny">Choose at least two people.</span>}
        </div>
      </div>

      {res?.missing?.length > 0 && (
        <div className="notice">
          {res.missing.map((m) => <div key={m.name}><b>{m.name}</b> needs {m.missing.join(', ')} before a full outfit can be made.</div>)}
          <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>Switch to that person (top right) and add their clothes in My Closet.</div>
        </div>
      )}
      {res?.members?.length > 0 && (
        <section>
          <div className="results-head">
            <p className="small" style={{ margin: 0 }}><b>Why it works together:</b> {res.why}</p>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8, alignItems: 'center' }}>
              <button className="btn sm accent" onClick={save} disabled={savedKey === JSON.stringify(res.members.map((m) => toSlots(m.outfit)))}>♡ Save family look</button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="family-grid" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${res.members.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))` }}>
            {res.members.map((m) => (
              <div key={m.profile.id} className="family-col">
                <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', alignItems: 'center' }}>
                  <h3 className="h3">{m.profile.name}</h3><ScoreBadge score={m.critique.overall} />
                </div>
                <Verdict critique={m.critique} compact />
                <Stage outfit={m.outfit} mode="mannequin" compact child={m.profile.kind === 'child'} />
                <PieceList outfit={m.outfit} />
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}

      {ensembles.length > 0 && (
        <section style={{ marginTop: 36 }}>
          <h2 className="h2">Saved family looks</h2>
          <div className="ensemble-list">
            {ensembles.map((e) => (
              <div key={e.id} className="ensemble">
                <div className="ensemble-head"><div><b>{e.name}</b><div className="tiny">{e.why}</div></div>
                  <ConfirmButton label="Delete" confirmLabel="Click to delete" onConfirm={() => dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_ENSEMBLE', id: e.id })} /></div>
                <div className="ensemble-row">
                  {e.members.map((m) => {
                    const p = state.profiles.find((x) => x.id === m.profileId);
                    const o = resolve(m.slots);
                    return <div key={m.profileId} className="ensemble-member"><Stage outfit={o} mode="flatlay" compact child={p?.kind === 'child'} /><span className="tiny">{p?.name || 'Removed member'}</span></div>;
                  })}
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

 FILE: wearwise/src/views/History.jsx

// Outfit History: what was actually worn, with ratings and quick feedback that personalize future suggestions.
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import { ConfirmButton, PieceList, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { FEEDBACK_TAGS, describePreferences } from '../ai/insights.js';

function Stars({ value, onChange }) {
  return (
    <span className="stars" role="radiogroup" aria-label="Rating">
      {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((n) => (
        <button key={n} role="radio" aria-checked={value === n} aria-label={`${n} star${n > 1 ? 's' : ''}`} className={n <= (value || 0) ? 'on' : ''} onClick={() => onChange(value === n ? null : n)}>★</button>
      ))}
    </span>
  );
}

export default function History({ go }) {
  const { dispatch, resolve, historyOf, profile, personal, get } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const list = historyOf(profile.id);
  const learned = useMemo(() => describePreferences(personal, get), [personal, get]);
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';
  const fmt = (d) => new Date(d + 'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' });

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'Your' : `${profile.name}'s`} outfit history</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Outfit History <span className="muted" style={{ fontWeight: 400 }}>· {list.length} worn</span></h1>
          <p className="small muted" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Tap “I wore this” on any outfit to log it. Rate it afterwards: your ratings quietly shape future suggestions.</p>
        </div>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/insights')}>Closet insights</button>
      </div>

      <div className="notice">
        <b>What Wearwise has learned{personal.rated ? ` from ${personal.rated} rated outfit${personal.rated > 1 ? 's' : ''}` : ''}:</b>{' '}
        {personal.rated === 0 ? 'Nothing yet. Rate a few outfits you wore and suggestions will lean toward what you love.' : learned.length ? learned.join(' ') : 'Not enough of a pattern yet. Keep rating.'}
        {personal.rated > 0 && <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 4 }}>Learned taste only chooses between outfits that already pass every styling check; it never overrides weather or occasion rules.</div>}
      </div>

      {list.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <p className="h2" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>No outfits logged yet.</p>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Create an Outfit</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="history-list">
          {list.map((h) => {
            const o = resolve(h.slots);
            const gone = Object.values(h.slots).filter(Boolean).length > Object.values(o).filter(Boolean).length;
            return (
              <article key={h.id} className="history-row">
                <div className="history-thumb"><Stage outfit={o} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} /></div>
                <div className="history-body">
                  <div className="history-head">
                    <div>
                      <input type="date" className="input date-input" value={h.date} aria-label="Date worn" onChange={(e) => e.target.value && dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_WEAR_DATE', id: h.id, date: e.target.value })} />
                      {h.context?.prompt && <div className="tiny">{h.context.prompt}</div>}
                    </div>
                    <Stars value={h.rating} onChange={(rating) => { dispatch({ type: 'RATE_WEAR', id: h.id, rating }); if (rating) toast('Thanks! Suggestions will take this into account.'); }} />
                  </div>
                  <div className="chips">
                    {FEEDBACK_TAGS.map((t) => {
                      const on = (h.tags || []).includes(t.id);
                      return <button key={t.id} className={'chip sm' + (on ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'RATE_WEAR', id: h.id, tags: on ? h.tags.filter((x) => x !== t.id) : [...(h.tags || []), t.id] })}>{t.label}</button>;
                    })}
                  </div>
                  <PieceList outfit={o} />
                  {gone && <p className="tiny">Some pieces from this outfit are no longer in the closet.</p>}
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6 }}>
                    <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: h.slots, name: 'Worn ' + fmt(h.date) } }); go('#/builder'); }}>Open in builder</button>
                    <ConfirmButton label="Remove" confirmLabel="Click to remove" onConfirm={() => dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_WEAR', id: h.id })} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </article>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
