 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Insights.jsx
====================================================================================================

// Closet insights: Rediscover My Closet + Wardrobe Versatility, for the active profile's own closet.
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import { PieceList, ScoreBadge, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { versatilityAsync, rediscover, wearStats, whyHardToStyle, VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS } from '../ai/insights.js';
import { missingForOutfit } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { CATEGORY_LABEL } from '../data/closet.js';

export default function Insights({ go }) {
  const { dispatch, owned, profile, historyOf, personal } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
  const history = useMemo(() => historyOf(profile.id), [historyOf, profile.id]);
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';

  // Rediscover is quick; versatility checks every piece in six situations, so it runs in small batches
  // with a progress line and the page stays responsive even for a big closet.
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setData(null); setProgress(null);
    let cancelled = false;
    const t = setTimeout(async () => {
      const ctxP = personal.rated ? personal : null;
      const r = rediscover(owned, history, { contexts: VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS.slice(0, 2).map((c) => ({ ...c, personal: ctxP })) });
      setData({ v: null, r, stats: wearStats(history) });
      const v = await versatilityAsync(owned, (done, total) => !cancelled && setProgress({ done, total }), VERSATILITY_CONTEXTS, () => cancelled);
      if (!cancelled && v) setData((d) => ({ ...d, v }));
    }, 30);
    return () => { cancelled = true; clearTimeout(t); };
  }, [owned, history, personal]);

  const missing = missingForOutfit(owned);
  if (missing.length) {
    return <div className="page"><div className="empty"><p className="small">Insights need a closet that can make full outfits. Add {missing.join(', ')} first.</p><button className="btn primary" onClick={() => go('#/closet')}>Add clothes</button></div></div>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'Your' : `${profile.name}'s`} closet</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Closet insights</h1>
        </div>
        <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/history')}>Outfit History</button>
      </div>

      {!data ? <div className="loading-block"><div className="loading-bar"><i /></div><p className="small muted">Checking every piece against the rest of the closet…</p></div> : (
        <>
          <section>
            <h2 className="h2">Rediscover My Closet</h2>
            <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>{history.length ? 'Pieces you haven\'t worn in 30 days or more, each in a strong outfit.' : 'Nothing is logged in Outfit History yet, so every piece counts as unworn. Tap "I wore this" on outfits to make this smarter.'}</p>
            {data.r.length === 0 ? <p className="small">Nothing forgotten. Everything has been worn recently, or the pieces you haven't worn don't make a strong outfit yet.</p> : (
              <div className="outfit-grid">
                {data.r.map((x) => (
                  <article key={x.item.id} className="outfit-card">
                    <Stage outfit={x.outfit} mode="flatlay" compact child={child} />
                    <div className="outfit-card-body">
                      <div className="outfit-card-head">
                        <div>
                          <div className="tiny">{x.daysSince === null ? 'Never worn' : `Last worn ${x.daysSince} days ago`}</div>
                          <h3 className="h3">{x.item.name}</h3>
                        </div>
                        <ScoreBadge score={x.critique.overall} />
                      </div>
                      <p className="small why">{x.why}</p>
                      <PieceList outfit={x.outfit} />
                      <div className="outfit-card-actions">
                        <button className="btn sm accent" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: toSlots(x.outfit), context: x.ctx } }); toast('Logged in Outfit History'); }}>I'll wear this</button>
                        <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => go('#/style/' + encodeURIComponent(x.item.id))}>More ideas</button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section style={{ marginTop: 36 }}>
            <div className="results-head" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>
              <h2 className="h2" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Wardrobe versatility</h2>
              {data.v && <span className="versatility-score"><b>{data.v.score}</b>/100</span>}
            </div>
            <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>How many other pieces each item makes strong outfits with, and in which of six everyday situations (everyday warm and cool, work, date, church, hot-weather vacation). The score is the share of pieces that pair with at least 3 others in at least 2 situations.</p>
            {!data.v ? (
              <div className="loading-block"><div className="loading-bar"><i style={progress ? { width: `${Math.round((progress.done / progress.total) * 100)}%`, animation: 'none' } : undefined} /></div><p className="small muted">Checking each piece against the rest of the closet{progress ? ` · ${progress.done} of ${progress.total}` : ''}…</p></div>
            ) : (<>
            <table className="vtable">
              <thead><tr><th>Piece</th><th>Pairs well with</th><th>Works for</th><th>Worn</th></tr></thead>
              <tbody>
                {data.v.items.map((x) => (
                  <tr key={x.item.id} className={x.contexts.length === 0 ? 'hard' : ''}>
                    <td><button className="vlink" onClick={() => go('#/style/' + encodeURIComponent(x.item.id))}><span className="mini" style={{ width: 34 }}><GarmentArt item={x.item} /></span><span>{x.item.name}<span className="tiny"> · {CATEGORY_LABEL[x.item.category]}</span></span></button></td>
                    <td>{x.partners} piece{x.partners === 1 ? '' : 's'}</td>
                    <td className="small">{x.contexts.length ? x.contexts.join(', ') : <span className="watch">{whyHardToStyle(x.item, owned)}</span>}</td>
                    <td className="small">{data.stats[x.item.id]?.wears || 0}×</td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
            {data.v.hardToStyle.length > 0 && <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>{data.v.hardToStyle.length} piece{data.v.hardToStyle.length > 1 ? 's don\'t' : ' doesn\'t'} make a strong outfit with anything else right now. The note says what's missing.</p>}
            </>)}
          </section>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}



====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Inspiration.jsx
====================================================================================================

import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import { Modal, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { decomposeLook, matchLook } from '../ai/inspiration.js';
import { SLOTS, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';
import { decomposeWithClaude } from '../ai/stylist.js';
import { useClaudeStatus } from '../ai/claude.js';

// Render a decomposed look as an "inspiration board" using generic illustrations.
function lookAsOutfit(items) {
  const o = {};
  for (const it of items) {
    const k = slotOf(it);
    if (o[k]) continue;
    o[k] = { id: 'look-' + it.label, name: it.label, category: it.category, art: it.art, colors: [it.color || { name: 'gray', hex: '#9A9B9E' }], fit: 'regular' };
  }
  return o;
}

function AddInspiration({ onClose, onAdded }) {
  const { dispatch } = useStore();
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [url, setUrl] = useState('');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [photo, setPhoto] = useState(null); // { blob, dataUrl }
  const [aiItems, setAiItems] = useState(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [err, setErr] = useState('');
  const items = aiItems || decomposeLook(text);

  async function pickPhoto(file) {
    if (!file) return;
    const dataUrl = await new Promise((r) => { const fr = new FileReader(); fr.onload = () => r(fr.result); fr.readAsDataURL(file); });
    setPhoto({ blob: file, dataUrl }); setAiItems(null); setErr(''); // nothing is sent until she asks
  }
  async function identify() {
    setBusy(true); setErr('');
    const r = await decomposeWithClaude(photo.blob);
    setBusy(false);
    if (r?.items) { setAiItems(r.items); if (!title && r.title) setTitle(r.title); }
    else setErr(r?.offline ? 'Gemini is off (sign in to Puter at the top to use it). You can type the pieces below instead.' : (r?.error || 'Gemini could not read this photo. Please type the pieces below.'));
  }
  return (
    <Modal title="Add inspiration" onClose={onClose}>
      <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Type the pieces of a look you love, or add a photo of it. Typed looks are matched on this device by keywords (no AI). A photo is only sent to Google Gemini if you tap "Identify the pieces", and the photo is saved with your Wearwise data (this browser, and your account if you're signed in).</p>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Inspiration photo (optional)</label>
        <div className="context-box" style={{ alignItems: 'center' }}>
          {photo && <img src={photo.dataUrl} alt="" style={{ width: 56, height: 70, objectFit: 'cover' }} />}
          <label className="btn sm" style={{ cursor: 'pointer' }}>{photo ? 'Change photo' : 'Choose photo'}<input type="file" accept="image/*" hidden onChange={(e) => pickPhoto(e.target.files[0])} /></label>
          {busy && <span className="small">Gemini is looking at the outfit…</span>}
        </div>
        {photo && !aiItems && !busy && (
          <div className="consent" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
            <p className="tiny">To list the pieces automatically, this photo is <b>sent to Google Gemini</b> through your Puter account. Nothing has been sent yet.</p>
            <button type="button" className="btn sm" onClick={identify} disabled={ai.status !== 'ready'}>{ai.status === 'ready' ? 'Identify the pieces with Gemini' : 'Gemini is off: sign in to Puter to use it'}</button>
          </div>
        )}
        {err && <span className="tiny" style={{ color: 'var(--warn)' }}>{err}</span>}
      </div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Title</label><input className="input" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Copenhagen street style" /></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Source link (optional)</label><input className="input" value={url} onChange={(e) => setUrl(e.target.value)} placeholder="https://…" /></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">What's in the look</label>
        <textarea className="textarea" rows="3" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder="black turtleneck, olive trousers, white sneakers, black crossbody bag" />
        <span className="tiny">{aiItems ? 'Gemini identified' : 'Keyword matching found'} {items.length} item{items.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}{items.length ? ': ' + items.map((i) => i.label).join(' · ') : ''}</span>
      </div>
      <button className="btn primary block" disabled={!items.length || busy} onClick={() => {
        const id = 'insp-' + Date.now().toString(36);
        dispatch({ type: 'ADD_INSPIRATION', inspiration: { id, title: title || 'Saved look', source: url ? 'Your reference' : photo ? 'Your photo' : 'Your reference', url, text: aiItems ? '' : text, items: aiItems || undefined, image: photo?.dataUrl, mood: '#CFCAC2', by: aiItems ? 'claude' : 'text' } });
        onAdded?.(id);
        onClose();
      }}>Match to my closet</button>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function Inspiration({ go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, owned } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [sel, setSel] = useState(state.inspirations[0]?.id);
  const [adding, setAdding] = useState(false);
  const current = state.inspirations.find((i) => i.id === sel) || state.inspirations[0];

  const scored = useMemo(() => state.inspirations.map((i) => {
    const items = i.items || decomposeLook(i.text || '');
    return { ...i, items, match: matchLook(items, owned) };
  }), [state.inspirations, owned]);
  const cur = scored.find((s) => s.id === current?.id);

  function recreate() {
    const slots = Object.fromEntries(SLOTS.map((k) => [k, null]));
    for (const m of cur.match.matches) { const k = slotOf(m.garment); if (!slots[k]) slots[k] = m.garment.id; }
    if (slots.top && owned.find((g) => g.id === slots.top)?.category === 'dress') slots.bottom = null;
    dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots, name: cur.title } });
    toast(cur.match.missing.length ? `Recreated with your pieces. ${cur.match.missing.length} piece${cur.match.missing.length > 1 ? 's are' : ' is'} missing from your closet.` : 'Recreated with your pieces.');
    go('#/builder');
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div><div className="label">Inspiration bridge</div><h1 className="h1">How close are you to this look?</h1></div>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setAdding(true)}>+ Add inspiration</button>
      </div>

      <div className="grid" style={{ marginBottom: 36 }}>
        {scored.map((s) => (
          <button key={s.id} className="card" onClick={() => setSel(s.id)} style={{ outline: s.id === cur?.id ? '2px solid var(--ink)' : 'none', outlineOffset: 3 }}>
            <div className="insp-board" style={{ background: s.mood }}>
              {s.image ? <img src={s.image} alt={s.title} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Stage outfit={lookAsOutfit(s.items)} compact />}
            </div>
            <div className="card-body">
              <div className="tiny">{s.source}</div>
              <div className="card-name">{s.title}</div>
              <div className="card-meta">You own {s.match.coverage}%</div>
            </div>
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      {cur && (
        <div className="insp-split" style={{ borderTop: '1px solid var(--ink)', paddingTop: 28 }}>
          <div>
            <div className="label">Inspiration · {cur.source}{cur.url ? <> · <a href={cur.url} target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">source</a></> : null}</div>
            <div className="insp-board" style={{ background: cur.mood, marginTop: 8 }}>{cur.image ? <img src={cur.image} alt={cur.title} style={{ width: '100%', height: '100%', objectFit: 'cover' }} /> : <Stage outfit={lookAsOutfit(cur.items)} />}</div>
            <span className={cur.by === 'claude' ? 'ai-badge' : 'tiny'} style={{ marginTop: 8, display: 'inline-block' }}>{cur.by === 'claude' ? 'Pieces identified by Gemini from the photo' : 'Pieces read from the text by keyword matching (no AI)'}</span>
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="label">{cur.title}</div>
            <div className="coverage" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>You own {cur.match.coverage}% of this look</div>
            <div className="tiny">{['exact', 'close', 'approximate'].map((l) => { const n = cur.match.matches.filter((m) => m.level === l).length; return n ? `${n} ${l}` : null; }).filter(Boolean).concat(cur.match.missing.length ? [`${cur.match.missing.length} missing`] : []).join(' · ')}</div>
            <p className="muted" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              {cur.match.matches.length ? <>Pair your {cur.match.matches.map((m) => m.garment.name.toLowerCase()).join(' + ')}.</> : 'Nothing in your closet matches yet.'}
              {cur.match.missing.length ? <> Missing: {cur.match.missing.map((m) => m.label).join(', ')}.</> : ' You have every piece!'}
            </p>
            <div style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
              {cur.match.matches.map((m) => (
                <div className="match-row" key={m.want.label}>
                  <div className="mini" style={{ background: cur.mood }}><GarmentArt item={Object.values(lookAsOutfit([m.want]))[0]} /></div>
                  <span className="muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>→</span>
                  <div className="mini"><GarmentArt item={m.garment} /></div>
                  <div><div className="small" style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{m.garment.name}</div><div className="tiny"><span className={'match-level ' + m.level}>{m.level === 'exact' ? 'Exact match' : m.level === 'close' ? 'Close match' : 'Approximate'}</span> for “{m.want.label}”{m.level !== 'exact' && m.why ? ` · ${m.why}` : ''}</div></div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {cur.match.missing.map((m) => (
                <div className="match-row" key={m.label}>
                  <div className="mini" style={{ background: cur.mood }}><GarmentArt item={Object.values(lookAsOutfit([m]))[0]} /></div>
                  <span className="muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center' }}>→</span>
                  <div className="mini missing"><span className="tiny">missing</span></div>
                  <div><div className="small" style={{ fontWeight: 600 }}>{m.label}</div><div className="tiny">{m.reason || 'Not in your closet'}</div></div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, marginTop: 20 }}>
              <button className="btn primary" style={{ flex: 1 }} onClick={recreate}>Recreate with my clothes</button>
            </div>
            <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Recreating uses your exact, close and approximate matches; missing pieces stay empty so you can choose what to wear instead.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {adding && <AddInspiration onClose={() => setAdding(false)} onAdded={(id) => setSel(id)} />}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Lookbooks.jsx

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { ConfirmButton, Modal, OutfitThumb, ScoreBadge, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import { SLOTS, slotOf } from '../data/closet.js';

/** Versatility = 1 − unique items / total item slots (docs/ARCHITECTURE.md §5). */
export function versatility(outfits) {
  const all = outfits.flatMap((o) => SLOTS.map((k) => o.slots[k]).filter(Boolean));
  const unique = new Set(all);
  const pct = all.length ? Math.round((1 - unique.size / all.length) * 100) : 0;
  const counts = {};
  all.forEach((id) => { counts[id] = (counts[id] || 0) + 1; });
  return { pct, unique: unique.size, slots: all.length, counts };
}

// ── share links: the lookbook is encoded in the URL (no server needed for the prototype)
// Each piece travels as a tiny description (name, type, colors, pattern), never a photo, so the link works on any
// device: the recipient sees illustrations of the pieces.
export function encodeShare(catalog, outfits, get = () => null) {
  const g = {};
  for (const o of outfits) for (const id of Object.values(o.slots || {})) {
    const it = id && get(id);
    if (it && !g[id]) g[id] = [it.name, it.category, it.art, (it.colors || []).slice(0, 2).map((c) => [c.name, c.hex]), it.pattern || 'solid', it.sleeve || null];
  }
  const payload = {
    t: catalog.title, k: catalog.kind, tr: catalog.trip, g,
    o: outfits.map((o) => ({ n: o.name, s: o.slots, m: o.stage?.mode === 'mannequin' ? 'mannequin' : 'flatlay', c: o.context?.prompt })),
  };
  const json = JSON.stringify(payload);
  return btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(json))).replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '');
}
export function decodeShare(s) {
  try {
    const json = decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(s.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))));
    return JSON.parse(json);
  } catch { return null; }
}

function NewLookbook({ onClose }) {
  const { dispatch } = useStore();
  const [title, setTitle] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('collection');
  const [city, setCity] = useState('');
  const [days, setDays] = useState(3);
  return (
    <Modal title="New lookbook" onClose={onClose}>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Title</label><input className="input" value={title} onChange={(e) => setTitle(e.target.value)} placeholder="Autumn Office Rotation" autoFocus /></div>
      <div className="field"><label className="label">Type</label>
        <div className="chips">
          <button className={'chip' + (kind === 'collection' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setKind('collection')}>Collection</button>
          <button className={'chip' + (kind === 'trip' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setKind('trip')}>Trip</button>
        </div></div>
      {kind === 'trip' && (
        <div className="field-row">
          <div className="field"><label className="label">City</label><input className="input" value={city} onChange={(e) => setCity(e.target.value)} /></div>
          <div className="field"><label className="label">Days</label><input className="input" type="number" min="1" max="21" value={days} onChange={(e) => setDays(e.target.value)} /></div>
        </div>
      )}
      <button className="btn primary block" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'CREATE_CATALOG', catalog: { title: title || 'Untitled lookbook', kind, ...(kind === 'trip' ? { trip: { city, days: +days } } : {}) } }); onClose(); }}>Create</button>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function Cover({ outfits, resolve }) {
  const three = outfits.slice(0, 3);
  return (
    <div className="lb-cover">
      {three.map((o) => <OutfitThumb key={o.id} outfit={o} resolve={resolve} />)}
      {Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, 3 - three.length) }, (_, i) => <div key={i} className="filler" />)}
    </div>
  );
}

export function LookbookList({ go }) {
  const { state, resolve, profile } = useStore();
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);
  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div><div className="label">Saved looks</div><h1 className="h1">Lookbooks</h1></div>
        <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setCreating(true)}>+ New lookbook</button>
      </div>
      <div className="grid three">
        {state.catalogs.filter((c) => !c.profileId || c.profileId === profile.id).map((c) => {
          const outfits = c.outfitIds.map((id) => state.outfits.find((o) => o.id === id)).filter(Boolean);
          const v = versatility(outfits);
          return (
            <button key={c.id} className="lb-card" onClick={() => go('#/lookbooks/' + c.id)}>
              <Cover outfits={outfits} resolve={resolve} />
              <div className="card-body">
                <div className="tiny">{c.kind === 'trip' ? `TRIP · ${c.trip?.city || ''} · ${c.trip?.days || '?'} DAYS` : 'COLLECTION'}</div>
                <div className="card-name" style={{ fontSize: 16 }}>{c.title}</div>
                <div className="card-meta">{outfits.length} look{outfits.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}{c.kind === 'trip' && outfits.length ? ` · ${v.pct}% versatile` : ''}</div>
              </div>
            </button>
          );
        })}
      </div>
      {creating && <NewLookbook onClose={() => setCreating(false)} />}
    </div>
  );
}

export function LookbookDetail({ id, go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, get, scoreOf } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [sharing, setSharing] = useState(false);
  const c = state.catalogs.find((x) => x.id === id);
  if (!c) return <div className="page"><div className="empty">This lookbook no longer exists. <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => go('#/lookbooks')}>Back</button></div></div>;
  const outfits = c.outfitIds.map((oid) => state.outfits.find((o) => o.id === oid)).filter(Boolean);
  const v = versatility(outfits);
  const isTrip = c.kind === 'trip';

  const packing = Object.entries(v.counts).map(([gid, n]) => ({ g: get(gid), n })).filter((x) => x.g)
    .sort((a, b) => SLOTS.indexOf(slotOf(a.g)) - SLOTS.indexOf(slotOf(b.g)) || b.n - a.n);

  const hasUploads = outfits.some((o) => Object.values(o.slots).some((sid) => sid && sid.startsWith('u-')));
  const shareUrl = `${location.origin}${location.pathname}#/share/${encodeShare(c, outfits, get)}`;
  function copy() {
    const ok = () => toast('Read-only link copied');
    try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareUrl).then(ok, () => toast('Select the link and copy it')); } catch { toast('Select the link and copy it'); }
  }

  function edit(o) {
    dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: o.slots, context: o.context, name: o.name, stage: { mode: o.stage?.mode || 'flatlay', scene: o.stage?.scene || 'studio', customBg: null } } });
    go('#/builder');
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <button className="btn ghost sm" onClick={() => go('#/lookbooks')}>← All lookbooks</button>
      <div className="page-head" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>
        <div>
          <div className="label">{isTrip ? `Trip · ${c.trip?.city} · ${c.trip?.days} days` : 'Collection'}</div>
          <h1 className="h1">{c.title}</h1>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10 }}>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/share/' + encodeShare(c, outfits, get))}>Preview shared page</button>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setSharing(true)} disabled={!outfits.length}>Share</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="stats">
        <div className="stat"><span className="label">Looks</span><b>{outfits.length}</b></div>
        <div className="stat"><span className="label">Unique pieces</span><b>{v.unique}</b></div>
        <div className="stat"><span className="label">Versatility score</span><b>{v.pct}%</b><span className="tiny">{v.slots} slots filled by {v.unique} pieces</span></div>
        <div className="stat"><span className="label">Avg. critique</span><b>{outfits.length ? Math.round(outfits.reduce((s, o) => s + scoreOf(o), 0) / outfits.length) : '—'}</b></div>
      </div>

      {outfits.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">No looks yet. Build one in the outfit builder and save it here.<div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}><button className="btn primary sm" onClick={() => go('#/builder')}>Open the outfit builder</button></div></div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid">
          {outfits.map((o, i) => (
            <div key={o.id}>
              <button className="card" onClick={() => edit(o)} aria-label={`Edit ${o.name}`}><OutfitThumb outfit={o} resolve={resolve} compactStage={false} /></button>
              <div className="card-body" style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 10 }}>
                <div>
                  <div className="tiny">{isTrip ? `DAY ${i + 1}` : `LOOK ${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`}</div>
                  <div className="card-name">{o.name}</div>
                  <div className="card-meta">{o.context?.weather} · {o.context?.tempC}°C</div>
                </div>
                <div style={{ textAlign: 'right' }}>
                  <ScoreBadge score={scoreOf(o)} />
                  <div><button className="btn ghost sm" style={{ padding: 0, height: 24 }} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_FROM_CATALOG', catalogId: c.id, outfitId: o.id })}>Remove</button></div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}

      {isTrip && packing.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 40 }}>
          <div className="page-head" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}><h2 className="h2">Packing list</h2><span className="small muted">{packing.length} pieces for {c.trip?.days} days</span></div>
          <div className="pack-list">
            {packing.map(({ g, n }) => (
              <div key={g.id}>
                <span style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 10 }}><span className="mini" style={{ width: 34 }}><GarmentArt item={g} /></span>{g.name}</span>
                <span className="muted">× {n} {n === 1 ? 'look' : 'looks'}</span>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          {(() => {
            const once = packing.filter((p) => p.n === 1);
            if (!once.length) return null;
            return <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>{once.length <= 3 ? `Worn once: ${once.map((p) => p.g.name).join(', ')}.` : `${once.length} of ${packing.length} pieces are worn only once.`} Re-wearing them would lighten your bag and raise the versatility score.</p>;
          })()}
        </div>
      )}

      {sharing && (
        <Modal title="Share this lookbook" onClose={() => setSharing(false)}>
          <p className="small muted" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}>Anyone with this link sees a clean, read-only lookbook. No sign-in needed, and critique notes stay private.</p>
          <div className="context-box">
            <input className="input" readOnly value={shareUrl} onFocus={(e) => e.target.select()} aria-label="Share link" />
            <button className="btn primary" onClick={copy}>Copy</button>
          </div>
          {hasUploads && <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 10 }}>Pieces you uploaded appear only on your device until accounts are switched on (Phase 1).</p>}
        </Modal>
      )}

      <div style={{ marginTop: 48, borderTop: '1px solid var(--line)', paddingTop: 16 }}>
        <ConfirmButton label="Delete lookbook" confirmLabel={`Delete “${c.title}”? Click again`} onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_CATALOG', id: c.id }); go('#/lookbooks'); }} />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function SharedLookbook({ data, go }) {
  const { get } = useStore();
  const p = decodeShare(data);
  if (!p) return <div className="page"><div className="empty">This share link is invalid or incomplete.</div></div>;
  const fromLink = (id) => {
    const d = p.g?.[id];
    if (!d) return null;
    const [name, category, art, colors, pattern, sleeve] = d;
    return { id, name, category, art, colors: (colors || []).map(([n, hex]) => ({ name: n, hex })), pattern, sleeve, shared: true, warmth: 2, formality: 2 };
  };
  // The link's own description wins, so everyone sees the same look (her own photos are never in the link).
  const resolveShared = (slots) => Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(slots || {}).map(([k, v]) => [k, v ? (fromLink(v) || get(v)) : null]));
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="share-hero">
        <div className="label">{p.k === 'trip' ? `Trip lookbook · ${p.tr?.city || ''}` : 'Seasonal lookbook'}</div>
        <h1 className="h1" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>{p.t}</h1>
        <p className="muted small">{p.o.length} looks · curated with Wearwise</p>
      </div>
      <div className="page" style={{ paddingTop: 0 }}>
        <div className="grid">
          {p.o.map((o, i) => (
            <div key={i}>
              <OutfitThumb outfit={{ slots: o.s, stage: { mode: o.m, scene: o.sc } }} resolve={resolveShared} />
              <div className="card-body">
                <div className="tiny">{p.k === 'trip' ? `DAY ${i + 1}` : `LOOK ${String(i + 1).padStart(2, '0')}`}</div>
                <div className="card-name">{o.n}</div>
                <div className="card-meta">{Object.values(resolveShared(o.s)).filter(Boolean).map((g) => g.name).join(' · ')}</div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 48 }}><button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/closet')}>Make your own lookbook</button></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Saved.jsx

import { lookKey, outfitPieces } from '../components/AiPhoto.jsx';
// Saved Outfits for the active profile: favorites, open, rename, delete.
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { OutfitThumb, ScoreBadge, ConfirmButton, WardrobePicker, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';

export default function Saved({ go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, resolve, outfitsOf, profile, scoreOf } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('all'); // 'all' | 'fav' | wardrobe id
  const all = outfitsOf(profile.id);
  const wardrobes = state.wardrobes.filter((w) => w.profileId === profile.id);
  const current = wardrobes.find((w) => w.id === tab);
  const list = tab === 'all' ? all : tab === 'fav' ? all.filter((o) => o.favorite) : all.filter((o) => o.wardrobe === tab);
  const onlyFav = tab === 'fav';
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div>
          <div className="label">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'Your' : `${profile.name}'s`} looks</div>
          <h1 className="h1">Saved Outfits <span className="muted" style={{ fontWeight: 400 }}>· {all.length}</span></h1>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 8 }}>
          <button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/lookbooks')}>Lookbooks</button>
          <button className="btn accent" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Create an Outfit</button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="toolbar">
        <div className="chips">
          <button className={'chip' + (tab === 'all' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setTab('all')}>All {all.length}</button>
          <button className={'chip' + (tab === 'fav' ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setTab('fav')}>♥ Favorites {all.filter((o) => o.favorite).length}</button>
          {wardrobes.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip' + (tab === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => setTab(w.id)}>{w.name} {all.filter((o) => o.wardrobe === w.id).length}</button>)}
        </div>
        {current && (
          <div className="wardrobe-tools">
            <input className="input" value={current.name} aria-label="Rename wardrobe" onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'RENAME_WARDROBE', id: current.id, name: e.target.value })} />
            <ConfirmButton label="Delete wardrobe" confirmLabel="Click again (outfits stay saved)" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_WARDROBE', id: current.id }); setTab('all'); }} />
          </div>
        )}
        <div className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>
          {wardrobes.length ? 'Wardrobes group your saved outfits (Work, Home…). Add an outfit to one from its card below.' : 'Tip: group saved outfits into wardrobes like Work or Home. Use the chips on any outfit below.'}
        </div>
      </div>
      {list.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="empty">
          <p className="h2" style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>{onlyFav ? 'No favorites yet.' : current ? `Nothing in ${current.name} yet.` : 'Nothing saved yet.'}</p>
          <p className="small">Create an outfit, then tap ♡ Save on the ones you like.</p>
          <button className="btn primary" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Create an Outfit</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid">
          {list.map((o) => {
            const score = scoreOf(o);
            return (
              <div key={o.id} className="card saved-card">
                <button className="card" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: o.slots, name: o.name, context: o.context, stage: o.stage } }); go('#/builder'); }} aria-label={`Open ${o.name}`}>
                  <div className="card-stage">{o.aiLook && o.aiLook.key === lookKey(outfitPieces(resolve(o.slots)))
                    ? <><img className="garment-img" style={{ objectFit: 'cover', width: '100%', height: '100%' }} src={o.aiLook.image} alt="AI-made photo of this outfit" /><span className="card-flag ai">✦ AI impression</span></>
                    : <OutfitThumb outfit={o} resolve={resolve} child={child} />}{o.demo && <span className="card-flag basic">Demo</span>}</div>
                </button>
                <div className="card-body">
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'space-between', gap: 8, alignItems: 'flex-start' }}>
                    <div>
                      <div className="card-name">{o.name}</div>
                      <div className="card-meta">{o.context?.prompt || ''}</div>
                    </div>
                    <ScoreBadge score={score} />
                  </div>
                  {o.why && <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 6 }}>{o.why}</p>}
                  <WardrobePicker outfitId={o.id} compact />
                  <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 6, marginTop: 8, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
                    <button className="btn sm" aria-pressed={!!o.favorite} onClick={() => dispatch({ type: 'TOGGLE_FAVORITE', id: o.id })}>{o.favorite ? '♥ Favorite' : '♡ Favorite'}</button>
                    <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: o.slots, context: o.context, outfitId: o.id } }); toast('Added to Outfit History'); }}>I wore this</button>
                    <ConfirmButton label="Delete" confirmLabel="Click to delete" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_OUTFIT', id: o.id }); toast('Outfit deleted'); }} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}
        </div>
      )}
      {state.catalogs.length > 0 && <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 20 }}>Group outfits into collections and trips in <a href="#/lookbooks">Lookbooks</a>.</p>}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================

// Settings: account, AI stylist, family members, demo data, delete everything.
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { useAuth, supabase, cloudTable, syncControl } from '../auth.jsx';
import { ConfirmButton, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import TipJar, { tipsOn } from '../components/TipJar.jsx';
import { deleteCloudPhotos, forgetCaches, local as localPhotos } from '../photos.js';
import { useClaudeStatus, puterUser, puterSignIn, puterSignOut } from '../ai/claude.js';

export function ProfileManager() {
  const { state, dispatch, closetOf } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState('adult');
  return (
    <div className="profile-manager">
      <span className="label">Family members</span>
      <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 2 }}>Each member has a separate closet and saved outfits. Suggestions for one person never use another person's clothes.</p>
      <ul className="profile-rows">
        {state.profiles.map((p) => (
          <li key={p.id}>
            <input className="input" value={p.name} aria-label={`Name of ${p.name}`} onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_PROFILE', profile: { id: p.id, name: e.target.value } })} />
            <select className="select" value={p.kind} aria-label={`Age group of ${p.name}`} onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'UPDATE_PROFILE', profile: { id: p.id, kind: e.target.value } })}>
              <option value="adult">Adult</option><option value="child">Child</option>
            </select>
            <span className="tiny">{closetOf(p.id).length} pieces{p.demo ? ' · demo' : ''}</span>
            {p.id === 'p-me'
              ? <span className="tiny muted">Main profile</span>
              : <ConfirmButton label="Remove" confirmLabel={`Delete ${p.name} and their clothes?`} onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_PROFILE', id: p.id }); toast(`${p.name} removed`); }} />}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <form className="profile-add" onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!name.trim()) return; dispatch({ type: 'ADD_PROFILE', name: name.trim(), kind }); toast(`${name.trim()} added. You're now viewing their closet.`); setName(''); }}>
        <input className="input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="Name, e.g. Dad or Mia" aria-label="New member name" />
        <select className="select" value={kind} onChange={(e) => setKind(e.target.value)} aria-label="New member age group"><option value="adult">Adult</option><option value="child">Child</option></select>
        <button className="btn primary" type="submit">Add member</button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

function LocationManager() {
  const { state, dispatch } = useStore();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const count = (id) => state.garments.filter((g) => g.location === id).length;
  return (
    <div>
      <h2 className="h2">Where clothes are kept</h2>
      <p className="small">Name the places you keep clothes (a closet, a storage box, Lola's house…). Set each piece's place in My Closet, then filter by it to find things fast.</p>
      <ul className="profile-rows">
        {state.locations.map((l) => (
          <li key={l.id} style={{ gridTemplateColumns: '1fr 110px auto' }}>
            <input className="input" value={l.name} aria-label={`Rename ${l.name}`} onChange={(e) => dispatch({ type: 'RENAME_LOCATION', id: l.id, name: e.target.value })} />
            <span className="tiny">{count(l.id)} pieces</span>
            <ConfirmButton label="Remove" confirmLabel="Click to remove" onConfirm={() => dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_LOCATION', id: l.id })} />
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <form className="profile-add" style={{ gridTemplateColumns: '1fr auto' }} onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (!name.trim()) return; dispatch({ type: 'ADD_LOCATION', location: { id: 'loc-' + Date.now().toString(36), name: name.trim() } }); setName(''); }}>
        <input className="input" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Bedroom closet" aria-label="New place" />
        <button className="btn primary" type="submit">Add place</button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Settings({ go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, hasDemo, deviceSave } = useStore();
  const { user, signOut } = useAuth() || {};
  const toast = useToast();
  const ai = useClaudeStatus();
  const [gem, setGem] = useState(undefined);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  if (gem === undefined && ai.via === 'puter') puterUser().then((u) => setGem(u || null)).catch(() => setGem(null));

  async function deleteEverything() {
    setBusy(true);
    syncControl.paused = true; // nothing may be re-uploaded while (or after) the account copy is deleted
    let cloud = '';
    if (supabase && user) {
      let error = null;
      try { await deleteCloudPhotos(supabase, user.id); } catch (e) { error = e; }
      if (!error) ({ error } = await supabase.from(await cloudTable()).delete().eq('user_id', user.id));
      cloud = error ? ` Your account copy could NOT be deleted (${error.message}). Try again when you're online.` : ' Your account copy was deleted too, and you were signed out.';
      if (!error) await signOut();
    }
    dispatch({ type: 'DELETE_ALL' });
    try { await localPhotos.clear(); } catch { /* none stored */ }
    forgetCaches();
    for (const k of ['wearwise.v1', 'unique-ava.v1']) { try { localStorage.removeItem(k); } catch { /* ignore */ } }
    syncControl.paused = false;
    setBusy(false);
    toast('All Wearwise data on this device was deleted.' + cloud);
    go('#/home');
  }

  return (
    <div className="page narrow">
      <div className="page-head"><div><div className="label">Settings</div><h1 className="h1">Settings</h1></div></div>

      <section className="settings-sec">
        <h2 className="h2">Account</h2>
        <p className="small">{user ? <>Signed in as <b>{user.email}</b>. Your closets, outfits and family members are saved to your account and load on any device where you sign in.</> : <>Not signed in. Everything is kept in this browser only. Sign in (top right) to save it to your account.</>}</p>
      </section>

      <section className="settings-sec">
        <h2 className="h2">AI stylist</h2>
        <p className="small">Wearwise uses Google Gemini through Puter: free, no API key. Each person signs in with their own Puter account, and Gemini's suggestions are checked by the built-in rules engine. Without it, the rules engine does all the styling.</p>
        {ai.via === 'puter' ? (
          gem ? <p className="small">Signed in to Puter as <b>{gem.username}</b>. <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => { puterSignOut(); setGem(null); toast('Signed out of Puter'); }}>Sign out</button></p>
            : <button className="btn sm primary" onClick={async () => { try { await puterSignIn(); setGem((await puterUser()) || null); } catch { toast('Sign-in was closed'); } }}>Sign in to Puter to switch on Gemini</button>
        ) : <p className="tiny">Status here: {ai.status === 'ready' ? `${ai.name} connected` : 'not connected'}.</p>}
      </section>

      <section className="settings-sec">
        <h2 className="h2">Your privacy: what happens where</h2>
        <ul className="privacy-list small">
          <li><b>Only on this device:</b> removing photo backgrounds, cropping, reading colors, outfit scoring and suggestions from the rules engine, matching typed inspiration looks, the calendar and trip planner.</li>
          <li><b>Sent to Google Gemini</b> (through Puter, only after you sign in to Puter and only when you use an AI feature):
            the <i>cut-out</i> of a clothing photo when you add it; the <i>whole</i> photo only if the cut-out failed <i>and</i> you tap "Ask Gemini using the whole photo"; an inspiration photo only when you tap "Identify the pieces"; and the <i>text details</i> of your clothes (names, colors, fabric, warmth…) when Gemini picks outfits or critiques one. Gemini picks outfits from these details; it doesn't look at your photos to do so.</li>
          <li><b>Sent to an AI image model</b> (through Puter, only when you tap "Make a realistic AI photo" or "Realistic photo (AI)"): the text details of the piece or outfit, and for an outfit, your own photos of those pieces (so the AI can copy them). The picture that comes back is labelled AI and kept separately; it never replaces your photos.</li>
          <li><b>Saved to your account</b> (Supabase, only when you're signed in): your whole Wearwise data. Clothing and inspiration photos are kept as private files in your own account folder. Each account can only read its own data and photos.</li>
          <li><b>Saved in this browser:</b> the same data, so it works offline. "Delete all my data" below removes both copies.</li>
          <li><b>Shared lookbook links</b> contain the outfit names and each piece's name, type and colors, <i>no photos</i>. Anyone you send the link to can see those, as illustrations.</li>
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section className="settings-sec"><ProfileManager /></section>

      <section className="settings-sec"><LocationManager /></section>

      <section className="settings-sec">
        <h2 className="h2">Demo closet</h2>
        <p className="small">A sample wardrobe (plus a sample Dad and Mia) for trying Wearwise out. Demo pieces are labelled “Demo” and can be removed in one click; your own pieces are never touched.</p>
        {hasDemo
          ? <ConfirmButton className="btn" label="Remove all demo items" confirmLabel="Click again to remove demo items" onConfirm={() => { dispatch({ type: 'REMOVE_DEMO' }); toast('Demo items removed'); }} />
          : <button className="btn" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DEMO' }); toast('Demo closet added'); }}>Add the demo closet</button>}
      </section>

      {tipsOn() && <section className="settings-sec"><TipJar compact /></section>}

      <section className="settings-sec danger">
        <h2 className="h2">Delete my data</h2>
        <p className="small">Deletes every closet, outfit, lookbook, family member and photo from this browser{user ? ' and from your account' : ''}. This cannot be undone. {user ? 'Your sign-in itself stays; ask us if you want the account removed too.' : ''}</p>
        <ConfirmButton className="btn" label={busy ? 'Deleting…' : 'Delete all my Wearwise data'} confirmLabel="Click again: this cannot be undone" onConfirm={deleteEverything} />
        {(() => {
          const total = new Blob([JSON.stringify(state)]).size;
          const photos = state.garments.reduce((a, g) => a + (g.image ? g.image.length : 0), 0) + state.inspirations.reduce((a, i) => a + (i.image ? i.image.length : 0), 0);
          const mb = (n) => (n / 1e6).toFixed(n < 1e6 ? 2 : 1);
          return (
            <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              Your Wearwise data: <b>{mb(total)} MB</b>, of which photos {mb(photos)} MB. Photos are stored separately from the rest{user ? ', on this device and as private files in your account' : ' on this device'}, so a big closet is fine.
              {deviceSave === 'full' ? <b className="warn-text"> This browser has no room left to save on this device (photo storage may be turned off, e.g. in private browsing). Your latest changes are only kept until you close this tab{user ? ', and in your account' : ''}.</b> : ''}
            </p>
          );
        })()}
        <p className="tiny" style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>{state.garments.length} pieces · {state.outfits.length} outfits · {state.profiles.length} profile{state.profiles.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} stored now.</p>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

 FILE: wearwise/src/views/StyleMe.jsx
====================================================================================================

// "Style Me With This": several styling directions around one piece she owns.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import { useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';
import GarmentArt from '../components/GarmentArt.jsx';
import { ContextPicker, OutfitCard, Compare, DEFAULT_CHOICE, contextOf, toSlots } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { styleWithThis, describe } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { MissingNotice } from './Create.jsx';

export default function StyleMe({ id, go }) {
  const { state, dispatch, owned, get, profile, personal } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const item = get(id);
  const [choice, setChoice] = useState(() => ({ ...(state.draft.choice || DEFAULT_CHOICE), style: null }));
  const ctx = useMemo(() => ({ ...contextOf(choice), personal: personal.rated ? personal : null }), [choice, personal]);
  const mine = item && owned.some((g) => g.id === item.id);
  const res = useMemo(() => (mine ? styleWithThis(owned, item, ctx) : null), [mine, owned, item, ctx]);
  const [edits, setEdits] = useState({});
  const [comparing, setComparing] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState({});
  const child = profile.kind === 'child';

  if (!item || !mine) {
    return <div className="page"><div className="empty"><p className="small">That piece isn't in {profile.name === 'Me' ? 'your' : `${profile.name}'s`} closet.</p><button className="btn" onClick={() => go('#/closet')}>Back to the closet</button></div></div>;
  }
  const outfits = (res?.outfits || []).map((r, i) => { const cur = edits[i] || r; return { ...cur, label: r.name, name: describe(cur.outfit), ctx: r.ctx }; });

  function save(i, favorite = false) {
    const r = outfits[i];
    const id = 'o-' + Date.now().toString(36) + i;
    dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', outfit: { id, name: `${item.name} · ${r.label}`, slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: r.ctx || ctx, stage: { mode: 'flatlay' }, favorite, why: r.why, source: 'rules' } });
    setSaved((s) => ({ ...s, [i]: id }));
    toast(favorite ? 'Saved as a favorite' : 'Saved to Saved Outfits');
  }

  return (
    <div className="page">
      <div className="page-head">
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 16, alignItems: 'center' }}>
          <span className="mini" style={{ width: 72 }}><GarmentArt item={item} /></span>
          <div>
            <div className="label">Style me with this</div>
            <h1 className="h1">{item.name}</h1>
            <p className="small muted" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Different directions, each one built around this piece, using only available clothes in this closet.</p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="create-form"><ContextPicker value={choice} onChange={(c) => { setChoice(c); setEdits({}); setSaved({}); }} compact /></div>

      {res?.missing?.length > 0 && <MissingNotice missing={res.missing} go={go} />}
      {res?.unavailable && <div className="notice">This piece is marked “{item.status}”, so it can't be styled right now. Mark it available in the closet first.</div>}
      {outfits.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <div className="results-head">
            <div className="small">{outfits.length} directions · {ctx.prompt} · styled by the rules engine</div>
            {!comparing && <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setComparing(true)}>Compare side by side</button>}
          </div>
          {comparing ? (
            <Compare results={outfits.slice(0, 3)} ctx={ctx} child={child} onClose={() => setComparing(false)} savedIdx={Object.keys(saved).map(Number)} onPick={(i) => save(i, true)} />
          ) : (
            <div className="outfit-grid">
              {outfits.map((r, i) => (
                <OutfitCard key={i} index={i} result={r} ctx={r.ctx || ctx} pool={owned} child={child} saved={!!saved[i]} savedId={saved[i]}
                  onReplace={(n) => { if (Object.values(n.outfit).some((g) => g?.id === item.id)) setEdits((e) => ({ ...e, [i]: n })); else toast('That change would drop this piece, so it was skipped.'); }}
                  onSave={() => save(i)}
                  onWore={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: r.ctx || ctx } }); toast('Added to Outfit History'); }}
                  onOpen={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DRAFT', draft: { slots: toSlots(r.outfit), name: `${item.name} · ${r.label}`, context: r.ctx || ctx, choice } }); go('#/builder'); }} />
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
          {comparing && outfits.length > 3 && <p className="tiny">Comparing the first 3 directions.</p>}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/src/views/Welcome.jsx
====================================================================================================

// Home: an honest start (empty closet → add clothes or try the demo), then quick actions.
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { HeroShowcase } from '../components/Motion.jsx';
import { ScoreBadge, PieceList, HeartButton, Verdict } from '../components/common.jsx';
import { contextOf, toSlots, DEFAULT_CHOICE } from '../components/Choose.jsx';
import { generateOutfits } from '../ai/generator.js';
import { WEATHER_CHOICES } from '../ai/context.js';
import { useStore } from '../store.jsx';
import Stage from '../components/Stage.jsx';
import TipJar from '../components/TipJar.jsx';
import UploadModal from '../components/UploadModal.jsx';
import { OutfitThumb, useToast } from '../components/common.jsx';

/** Today's outfit: a real suggestion from her own available clothes, one tap away. */
function TodayCard({ go }) {
  const { owned, dispatch, personal, profile } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [weather, setWeather] = useState('warm');
  const [n, setN] = useState(0);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState({});
  const ctx = useMemo(() => ({ ...contextOf({ ...DEFAULT_CHOICE, weather }), personal: personal.rated ? personal : null }), [weather, personal]);
  const list = useMemo(() => generateOutfits(owned, ctx).outfits, [owned, ctx]);
  if (!list.length) return null;
  const r = list[n % list.length];
  const key = weather + (n % list.length);
  return (
    <section className="today-card" aria-label="Today's outfit">
      <div className="today-stage"><Stage key={key} outfit={r.outfit} mode="flatlay" child={profile.kind === 'child'} /></div>
      <div className="today-body">
        <div className="label">Today's outfit · from {profile.name === 'Me' ? 'your' : `${profile.name}'s`} closet</div>
        <div className="chips" role="group" aria-label="Today's weather">
          {WEATHER_CHOICES.map((w) => <button key={w.id} className={'chip sm' + (weather === w.id ? ' on' : '')} onClick={() => { setWeather(w.id); setN(0); }}>{w.label}</button>)}
        </div>
        <div className="outfit-card-head"><h2 className="h2">{r.name}</h2><ScoreBadge score={r.critique.overall} /></div>
        <Verdict critique={r.critique} compact />
        <p className="small why">{r.why}</p>
        <PieceList outfit={r.outfit} />
        <div className="outfit-card-actions">
          <HeartButton saved={!!saved[key]} onSave={() => { dispatch({ type: 'SAVE_OUTFIT', outfit: { name: r.name, slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: ctx, stage: { mode: 'flatlay' }, why: r.why, source: 'rules' } }); setSaved((x) => ({ ...x, [key]: true })); toast('Saved'); }} />
          <button className="btn sm" onClick={() => setN(n + 1)}>Another idea</button>
          <button className="btn sm ghost" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOG_WEAR', entry: { slots: toSlots(r.outfit), context: ctx } }); toast('Logged in Outfit History'); }}>I'll wear this</button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}

const QUICK = [['work', 'Work'], ['date', 'Date night'], ['church', 'Church'], ['party', 'Party'], ['vacation', 'Vacation']];
import { usePhotos } from '../data/photos.js';
import { missingForOutfit } from '../ai/generator.js';


export default function Welcome({ go }) {
  const { dispatch, owned, profile, outfitsOf, hasDemo, resolve, historyOf } = useStore();
  const toast = useToast();
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false);
  const photos = usePhotos();
  const empty = owned.length === 0;
  const missing = missingForOutfit(owned);
  const recent = outfitsOf(profile.id).slice(0, 3);
  const who = profile.name === 'Me' ? 'your' : `${profile.name}'s`;

  function added(items) {
    dispatch({ type: 'ADD_GARMENTS', garments: items });
    dispatch({ type: 'ONBOARDED' });
    setUploading(false);
    toast(`${items.length} piece${items.length > 1 ? 's' : ''} added to ${who} closet`);
    go('#/closet');
  }

  if (!empty) {
    return (
      <div className="page home">
        <div className="home-head">
          <div>
            <span className="ai-badge">Your personal stylist</span>
            <h1 className="h1">{profile.name === 'Me' ? 'What are you dressing for?' : `What is ${profile.name} dressing for?`}</h1>
            <p className="muted small" style={{ margin: '4px 0 0' }}>{owned.length} pieces in {who} closet.{missing.length ? ` To build full outfits, add: ${missing.join(', ')}.` : ''}</p>
          </div>
          <div className="quick-row" role="group" aria-label="Quick outfit for">
            {QUICK.map(([id, l]) => <button key={id} className="chip" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'SET_CONTEXT', context: contextOf({ ...DEFAULT_CHOICE, occasion: id }), choice: { ...DEFAULT_CHOICE, occasion: id } }); go('#/create/go'); }}>{l}</button>)}
            <button className="chip accent-chip" onClick={() => go('#/create')}>Something else…</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        {missing.length === 0 ? <TodayCard go={go} /> : (
          <div className="empty"><p className="small">Add {missing.join(', ')} and Wearwise can suggest complete outfits.</p><button className="btn primary" onClick={() => setUploading(true)}>Add clothes</button></div>
        )}
        <div className="home-actions">
          <button className="home-tile" onClick={() => setUploading(true)}><b>Add clothes</b><span className="tiny">Photos or by hand</span></button>
          <button className="home-tile" onClick={() => go('#/insights')}><b>Rediscover my closet</b><span className="tiny">Forgotten pieces, new ideas</span></button>
          <button className="home-tile" onClick={() => go('#/family')}><b>Family look</b><span className="tiny">Coordinated, not matching</span></button>
          <button className="home-tile" onClick={() => go('#/history')}><b>Outfit history</b><span className="tiny">Rate what you wore</span></button>
        </div>
        {recent.length > 0 && (
          <section style={{ marginTop: 28 }}>
            <div className="results-head" style={{ marginTop: 0 }}><h2 className="h2">Recently saved</h2><a className="small" href="#/saved">See all</a></div>
            <div className="recent-row">
              {recent.map((o) => <button key={o.id} className="card" title={o.name} onClick={() => go('#/saved')}><OutfitThumb outfit={o} resolve={resolve} child={profile.kind === 'child'} /><span className="tiny recent-name">{o.name}</span></button>)}
            </div>
          </section>
        )}
        <div style={{ marginTop: 28 }}><TipJar /></div>
        {uploading && <UploadModal onClose={() => setUploading(false)} onSaved={added} ownerName={profile.name} />}
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="welcome">
      <div className="welcome-art">
        <div style={{ width: 'min(440px, 100%)' }}>
          {photos.hero
            ? <img src={photos.hero} alt="Wearwise look" style={{ width: '100%', aspectRatio: '4 / 5', objectFit: 'cover', display: 'block' }} />
            : <HeroShowcase />}
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className="welcome-copy">
        <span className="ai-badge">AI personal stylist</span>
        <h1 className="h1 hero-title"><span>Look your best.</span><span>Make the most of what you own.</span><span className="accent-line">Never turn your closet upside down again.</span></h1>
        <p className="muted" style={{ margin: 0 }}>Add photos of {who} clothes, however messy. Wearwise catalogs them, then builds complete outfits from what you really own, for the occasion and the weather, and explains why each one works.</p>
        <ol className="feature-list">
          <li><b>01</b><span>Add {who} clothes: several photos at once, or by hand</span></li>
          <li><b>02</b><span>Choose the occasion, style and weather</span></li>
          <li><b>03</b><span>Get 3 outfits, compare them side by side, save the favorite</span></li>
        </ol>
        <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 10, flexWrap: 'wrap' }}>
          <button className="btn accent" onClick={() => setUploading(true)}>Add {who} clothes</button>
          {!hasDemo && <button className="btn" onClick={() => { dispatch({ type: 'LOAD_DEMO' }); toast('Demo closet added. Remove it any time in Settings.'); go('#/home'); }}>Try the demo closet</button>}
        </div>
        <p className="tiny">The demo closet is sample data, clearly labelled, and removable in one click.</p>
      </div>
      {uploading && <UploadModal onClose={() => setUploading(false)} onSaved={added} ownerName={profile.name} />}
    </div>
  );
}


====================================================================================================
 FILE: wearwise/vite.config.js
====================================================================================================

import { defineConfig } from 'vite';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import { viteSingleFile } from 'vite-plugin-singlefile';

// Builds ONE self-contained HTML file, so it can live at the repo root as
// wearwise.html and be served by GitHub Pages (same as tiffo.html).
export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), viteSingleFile()],
  build: { outDir: 'dist', emptyOutDir: true },
});
